Off by default: add `"js/iam-kms-resource-wildcard"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6304

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const p = { Effect: 'Allow', Action: 'kms:Decrypt', Resource: '*' };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const p = { Effect: 'Allow', Action: 'kms:Decrypt', Resource: 'arn:aws:kms:eu-west-1:1:key/k' };
```

## js/non-null-assertion

**Non-null assertions should not be used.** x! silences the compiler instead of handling the null.

Off by default: add `"js/non-null-assertion"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2966

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const n = a!.length;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const n = a?.length;
```

## js/primitive-type-annotation

**Primitive types should be omitted from initialized or defaulted declarations.** The type is inferred from the value.

Off by default: add `"js/primitive-type-annotation"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3257

Noncompliant:

```typescript
let n: number = 5;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
let n = 5;
```

## js/empty-interface

**Interfaces should not be empty.** An empty interface accepts anything but null.

Off by default: add `"js/empty-interface"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4023

Noncompliant:

```typescript
interface A {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
interface A { id: number }
```

## js/type-assertion-angle

**Type assertions should use "as".** <T>x cannot be used in TSX and reads like a generic.

Off by default: add `"js/type-assertion-angle"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4137

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const a = <string>b;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const a = b as string;
```

## js/union-too-long

**Union types should not have too many elements.** A union of many members belongs in a named alias.

Off by default: add `"js/union-too-long"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4622

Noncompliant:

```typescript
let x: 'a' | 'b' | 'c' | 'd';
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type Letter = 'a' | 'b' | 'c' | 'd'; let x: Letter;
```

## js/optional-boolean-parameter

**Optional boolean parameters should have default value.** An absent boolean is a third state nobody asked for.

Off by default: add `"js/optional-boolean-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4798

Noncompliant:

```typescript
function f(flag?: boolean) {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
function f(flag = false) {}
```

## js/regex-empty-group

**Regular expressions should not contain empty groups.** An empty group matches nothing and captures nothing.

SonarQube: S6331

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /a()b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /ab/;
```

## js/regex-empty-alternative

**Alternation in regular expressions should not contain empty alternatives.** A stray | adds an alternative that matches everything.

SonarQube: S6323

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a|)b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a)?b/;
```

## js/regex-single-character-class

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain only one character.** [a] is a.

SonarQube: S6397

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /[a]b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /ab/;
```

## js/regex-concise-syntax

**Regular expression quantifiers and character classes should be used concisely.** [0-9] is \d and a{1,} is a+.

SonarQube: S6353

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /[0-9]+/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /\d+/;
```

## js/regex-single-character-alternation

**Single-character alternations in regular expressions should be replaced with character classes.** a|b|c is [abc].

SonarQube: S6035

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /a|b|c/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /[abc]/;
```

## js/regex-multiple-spaces

**Regular expressions should not contain multiple spaces.** Several spaces in a row are hard to count.

SonarQube: S6326

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /a   b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /a {3}b/;
```

## js/regex-duplicate-class-member

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain the same character twice.** The second one does nothing.

SonarQube: S5869

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /[aba]/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /[ab]/;
```

## js/regex-grapheme-in-class

**Unicode Grapheme Clusters should be avoided inside regex character classes.** A class matches single code points, not letters with accents.

SonarQube: S5868

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /[\u00e9\u0301]/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(?:e\u0301|x)/;
```

## js/regex-repeat-matches-empty

**Repeated patterns in regular expressions should not match the empty string.** (a*)* matches nothing, forever.

SonarQube: S5842

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a*)*b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a+)*b/;
```

## js/regex-anchor-alternation

**Alternatives in regular expressions should be grouped when used with anchors.** ^a|b$ anchors only the outer alternatives.

SonarQube: S5850

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /^a|b$/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /^(?:a|b)$/;
```

## js/regex-reluctant-at-end

**Reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions should be followed by an expression that can't match the empty string.** A lazy quantifier at the end matches as little as possible: nothing.

SonarQube: S6019

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /ab.*?/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /ab.*?c/;
```

## js/regex-too-complex

**Regular expressions should not be too complicated.** A pattern nobody can read is a pattern nobody can fix.

SonarQube: S5843

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /((a|b)+(c|d)+)+((e|f)+(g|h)+)+((i|j)+(k|l)+)+/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a|b)+/;
```

## js/regex-polynomial-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause non-linear backtracking.** Adjacent repetitions over the same characters take polynomial time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S8786

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /\d*\d*x/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /\d+x/;
```

## js/regex-catastrophic-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause catastrophic backtracking.** Nested repetitions that can match the same text take exponential time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S5852

Standards: [CWE-1333](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1333.html)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a+)+b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(a+b)+/;
```

## js/regex-empty-class

**Empty character classes should not be used.** [] never matches anything.

SonarQube: S2639

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /a[]b/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /a[^]b/;
```

## js/regex-control-characters

**Regular expressions should not contain control characters.** Control characters in a pattern are invisible and nearly always a mistake.

SonarQube: S6324

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /\x1f/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /\t/;
```

## js/regex-unicode-flag

**Regular expressions using Unicode character classes or property escapes should enable the unicode flag.** Without u, \p{L} matches the letters p, {, L and }.

Off by default: add `"js/regex-unicode-flag"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5867

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /\p{L}/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /\p{L}/u;
```

## js/regex-named-group-by-index

**Names of regular expressions named groups should be used.** A named group read by number defeats the name.

SonarQube: S5860

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = /(?<year>\d+)-\1/;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = /(?<year>\d+)-\k<year>/;
```

## js/regex-invalid

**Regular expressions should be syntactically valid.** An invalid pattern throws a SyntaxError when it is built.

SonarQube: S5856

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = new RegExp('(a');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = new RegExp('(a)');
```

## js/regex-replacement-group-missing

**Replacement strings should reference existing regular expression groups.** $3 in a replacement for a pattern with two groups stays as the text $3.

SonarQube: S6328

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = x.replace(/(a)(b)/, '$3');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = x.replace(/(a)(b)/, '$2');
```

## js/regex-exec-over-match

**"RegExp.exec()" should be preferred over "String.match()".** exec says what is done: run one regex over one string.

SonarQube: S6594

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = 'abc';
const m = s.match(/a(b)/);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = 'abc';
const m = /a(b)/.exec(s);
```

## js/switch-without-default

**"switch" statements should have "default" clauses.** Without a default, an unexpected value is silently ignored.

Off by default: add `"js/switch-without-default"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S131

Noncompliant:

```javascript
switch (a) { case 1: f(); break; case 2: g(); break; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
switch (a) { case 1: f(); break; case 2: g(); break; default: h(); }
```

## js/switch-instead-of-if

**"if" statements should be preferred over "switch" when simpler.** A switch with one or two cases is an if in disguise.

SonarQube: S1301

Noncompliant:

```javascript
switch (a) { case 1: f(); break; default: g(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (a === 1) { f(); } else { g(); }
```

## js/immediately-returned-variable

**Local variables should not be declared and then immediately returned or thrown.** The variable adds a name for a value that is used once, on the next line.

Off by default: add `"js/immediately-returned-variable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1488

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f() { const r = g(); return r; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { return g(); }
```

## js/sort-without-compare

**"Array.prototype.sort()" and "Array.prototype.toSorted()" should use a compare function.** The default order compares elements as strings: 10 sorts before 9.

SonarQube: S2871

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = [10, 9, 1].sort();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = [10, 9, 1].sort((a, b) => a - b);
```

## js/delete-on-array

**"delete" should not be used on arrays.** delete leaves a hole and keeps the length.

SonarQube: S2870

Noncompliant:

```javascript
delete items[2];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
items.splice(2, 1);
```

## js/new-on-non-constructor

**"new" should only be used with functions and classes.** An arrow function cannot be a constructor.

SonarQube: S2999

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const make = () => ({}); const o = new make();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function Make() {} const o = new Make();
```

## js/string-comparison

**Comparison operators should not be used with strings.** < and > compare strings by code unit, not alphabetically.

Off by default: add `"js/string-comparison"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3003

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (a < 'm') { f(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (a.localeCompare('m') < 0) { f(); }
```

## js/string-plus-non-string

**Strings and non-strings should not be added.** '1' + 2 is '12', which is rarely what was meant.

Off by default: add `"js/string-plus-non-string"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3402

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const x = '1' + 2;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const x = `1${2}`;
```

## js/prototype-assignment

**Class methods should be used instead of "prototype" assignments.** The class syntax says the same with less.

Off by default: add `"js/prototype-assignment"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3525

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function A() {} A.prototype.f = function () {};
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { f() {} }
```

## js/require-instead-of-import

**"import" should be used to include external code.** require is the module system of an older Node.

Off by default: add `"js/require-instead-of-import"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3533

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const fs = require('fs');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import fs from 'fs';
```

## js/arithmetic-on-non-numbers

**Arithmetic operations should not result in "NaN".** Arithmetic on an object, array, function or undefined is NaN.

Off by default: add `"js/arithmetic-on-non-numbers"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3757

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const x = {} * 2;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const x = 3 * 2;
```

## js/arithmetic-on-strings-or-booleans

**Arithmetic operators should only have numbers as operands.** 'a' - 1 is NaN, true * 2 is 2.

Off by default: add `"js/arithmetic-on-strings-or-booleans"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3760

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const x = 'a' - 1;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const x = Number(a) - 1;
```

## js/comparison-of-non-numbers

**Values not convertible to numbers should not be used in numeric comparisons.** {} > 1 is false whatever the object holds.

Off by default: add `"js/comparison-of-non-numbers"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3758

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if ({} > 1) { f(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (n > 1) { f(); }
```

## js/inconsistent-return

**Functions should use "return" consistently.** Some paths return a value, others return nothing.

Off by default: add `"js/inconsistent-return"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3801

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 1; } return; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 1; } return 0; }
```

## js/inconsistent-return-types

**Functions should always return the same type.** A function returning a string on one path and a number on another is hard to use.

SonarQube: S3800

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 'x'; } return 1; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 'x'; } return 'y'; }
```

## js/for-in-over-array

**"for in" should not be used with iterables.** for...in visits indexes as strings, and inherited keys.

Off by default: add `"js/for-in-over-array"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4139

Noncompliant:

```javascript
for (const i in [1, 2, 3]) { f(i); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
for (const i of [1, 2, 3]) { f(i); }
```

## js/constructor-async

**Constructors should not contain asynchronous operations.** A constructor cannot wait, so the object is used before it is ready.

SonarQube: S7059

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { fetch('/x'); } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { static async create() { await fetch('/x'); return new A(); } }
```

## js/foreach-to-for-of

**Use "for...of" loops instead of "forEach" method calls.** for...of allows break, await and return.

Off by default: add `"js/foreach-to-for-of"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7728

Noncompliant:

```javascript
items.forEach((i) => f(i));
```

Compliant:

```javascript
for (const i of items) { f(i); }
```

## js/top-level-await

**Top-level await should be preferred over promise chains and async wrappers.** In a module, an async IIFE is what await at the top level replaces.

SonarQube: S7785

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import x from 'x'; (async () => { await x(); })();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import x from 'x'; await x();
```

## js/prefer-starts-ends-with

**Ends of strings should be checked with "startsWith()" and "endsWith()".** indexOf and slice comparisons say it the long way.

SonarQube: S6557

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (s.indexOf('a') === 0) { f(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (s.startsWith('a')) { f(); }
```

## js/fluent-return-this

**Prefer the return type "this" in fluent interfaces.** A method returning its own class type loses the subclass.

SonarQube: S6565

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class A { set(): A { return this; } }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class A { set(): this { return this; } }
```

## js/boxed-return-type

**Primitive return types should be used.** Number, String and Boolean are the wrapper objects.

Off by default: add `"js/boxed-return-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4324

Noncompliant:

```typescript
function f(): Number { return 1; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
function f(): number { return 1; }
```

## js/meaningless-intersection

**Type intersections should use meaningful types.** string & number has no values.

SonarQube: S4335

Noncompliant:

```typescript
type T = string & number;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type T = string & { brand: 'a' };
```

## js/undefined-for-optional-parameter

**"undefined" should not be passed as the value of optional parameters.** An omitted argument already means undefined.

SonarQube: S4623

Noncompliant:

```typescript
function f(a: number, b?: number) {} f(1, undefined);
```

Compliant:

```typescript
function f(a: number, b?: number) {} f(1);
```

## js/arguments-in-wrong-order

**Parameters should be passed in the correct order.** Passing the arguments swapped is a typo that compiles.

SonarQube: S2234

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function area(width, height) {} const width = 1; const height = 2; area(height, width);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function area(width, height) {} const width = 1; const height = 2; area(width, height);
```

## js/selector-parameter

**Methods should not contain selector parameters.** A boolean flag that picks between two behaviours is two methods.

SonarQube: S2301

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(flag = false) { if (flag) { g(); } else { h(); } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { g(); }
```

## js/test-without-assertion

**Tests should include assertions.** A test that asserts nothing passes whatever the code does.

SonarQube: S2699

Noncompliant:

```javascript
it('works', () => { run(); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
it('works', () => { expect(run()).toBe(1); });
```

## js/assertion-incompatible-types

**Assertions comparing incompatible types should not be made.** expect(1).toBe('1') can never pass.

SonarQube: S5845

Noncompliant:

```javascript
expect(1).toBe('1');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
expect(1).toBe(1);
```

## js/template-autoescape-disabled

**Auto-escaping in HTML template engines should not be disabled.** Without escaping, any value can inject markup.

SonarQube: S5247

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const html = Handlebars.compile(t, { noEscape: true });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const html = Handlebars.compile(t);
```

## js/find-for-existence

**Prefer .some() over .find() when only checking existence.** find() returns the element; if only its presence matters, some() says so.

SonarQube: S7754

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(items) { const hit = items.find((i) => i.ok); if (hit) { return 1; } return 0; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(items) { if (items.some((i) => i.ok)) { return 1; } return 0; }
```

## js/unused-assignment

**Unused assignments should be removed.** A value that is overwritten or never read was computed for nothing.

SonarQube: S1854

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f() { let x = g(); x = 2; return x; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { let x = g(); return x; }
```

## js/deprecated-api

**Deprecated APIs should not be used.** Deprecated APIs are on their way out.

SonarQube: S1874

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = 'abc'.substr(1, 2);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = 'abc'.slice(1, 3);
```

## js/deprecated-buffer-constructor

**Deprecated APIs should not be used.** new Buffer() can expose uninitialized memory and is deprecated.

SonarQube: S1874

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const b = new Buffer(10);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const b = Buffer.alloc(10);
```

## js/null-dereference

**Properties of variables with "null" or "undefined" values should not be accessed.** The branch that just checked for null reads a property of it.

SonarQube: S2259

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(x) { if (x === null) { return x.name; } return 1; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(x) { if (x !== null) { return x.name; } return 1; }
```

## js/gratuitous-condition

**Boolean expressions should not be gratuitous.** A condition that is always true or false, or repeats one already known, never decides anything.

SonarQube: S2589

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { if (a) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { g(); } }
```

## js/arrow-function-style

**Braces and parentheses should be used consistently with arrow functions.** (x) => { return x; } says it the long way.

Off by default: add `"js/arrow-function-style"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3524

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const f = (x) => x * 2;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const f = x => x * 2;
```

## js/array-index-not-a-count

**Array indexes should be numeric.** Writing arr['name'] adds a property that array methods ignore.

SonarQube: S3579

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = []; a['name'] = 1;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = []; a[0] = 1;
```

## js/builtin-argument-type

**Arguments to built-in functions should match documented types.** Math.max('abc') is NaN.

SonarQube: S3782

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const m = Math.max('abc', 1);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const m = Math.max(2, 1);
```

## js/undeclared-variable

**Variables should be defined before being used.** An identifier that nothing declares is a ReferenceError, or a global created by accident.

Off by default: add `"js/undeclared-variable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3827

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f() { return total + 1; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(total) { return total + 1; }
```

## js/promise-rejection-in-try

**Promise rejections should not be caught by "try" blocks.** A catch block cannot see the rejection of a promise that is neither awaited nor returned.

SonarQube: S4822

Noncompliant:

```javascript
async function f() { try { g().then(h); } catch (e) { log(e); } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
async function f() { try { await g().then(h); } catch (e) { log(e); } }
```

## js/test-with-sleep

**Tests should be stable.** A fixed delay makes a test slow and flaky.

SonarQube: S5973

Noncompliant:

```javascript
it('waits', () => { setTimeout(done, 500); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
it('waits', async () => { await ready(); });
```

## js/similar-tests

**Similar tests should be grouped in a single Parameterized test.** Three tests that differ only in literals are one test with three rows.

SonarQube: S5976

Noncompliant:

```javascript
it('a', () => { expect(f(1)).toBe(2); }); it('b', () => { expect(f(2)).toBe(3); }); it('c', () => { expect(f(3)).toBe(4); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
it.each([[1, 2], [2, 3], [3, 4]])('f(%i)', (a, b) => { expect(f(a)).toBe(b); });
```

## js/chai-two-reasons

**Chai assertions should have only one reason to succeed.** not.throw(Error) passes when anything else is thrown too.

SonarQube: S6092

Noncompliant:

```javascript
expect(fn).to.not.throw(TypeError);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
expect(fn).to.not.throw();
```

## js/missing-to-string

**Objects and classes converted or coerced to strings should define a "toString()" method.** `${obj}` prints [object Object].

SonarQube: S6551

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = `value: ${{ a: 1 }}`;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = `value: ${JSON.stringify({ a: 1 })}`;
```

## js/wallet-phrase-hardcoded

**Wallet phrases should not be hard-coded.** A recovery phrase in source code hands over the wallet.

SonarQube: S7639

Standards: [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const mnemonic = 'abandon ability able about above absent absorb abstract absurd abuse access accident';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const mnemonic = process.env.MNEMONIC;
```

## js/function-can-move-up

**Functions should be moved to the highest possible scope.** A function that uses nothing from the function around it is recreated on every call.

SonarQube: S7721

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function outer(a) { function helper(x) { return x * 2; } return helper(a); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function helper(x) { return x * 2; } function outer(a) { return helper(a); }
```

## js/assertion-outside-test

**Assertions should be placed inside test cases or hooks.** An assertion in a describe body runs while the tests are being collected.

SonarQube: S8784

Noncompliant:

```javascript
describe('a', () => { expect(1).toBe(1); it('b', () => {}); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
describe('a', () => { it('b', () => { expect(1).toBe(1); }); });
```

## js/props-without-prop-types

**React components should validate prop types.** A prop nobody declared can be anything.

Off by default: add `"js/props-without-prop-types"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6774

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function Hello(props) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function Hello(props) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; } Hello.propTypes = {};
```

## js/default-prop-is-required

**All "defaultProps" should have non-required PropTypes.** A required prop never uses its default.

SonarQube: S6775

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function A() { return <p />; } A.propTypes = { a: PropTypes.string.isRequired }; A.defaultProps = { a: 'x' };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function A() { return <p />; } A.propTypes = { a: PropTypes.string }; A.defaultProps = { a: 'x' };
```

## js/headers-reference-missing-cell

**Table cells should reference their headers.** headers must name ids that exist.

SonarQube: S5260

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = <table><tr><th id="a">A</th></tr><tr><td headers="b">1</td></tr></table>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = <table><tr><th id="a">A</th></tr><tr><td headers="a">1</td></tr></table>;
```

## js/aria-attribute-role-mismatch

**DOM elements with ARIA role should only have supported properties.** aria-checked on a button is ignored by assistive technology.

SonarQube: S6811

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = <div role="button" aria-checked="true" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = <div role="button" aria-pressed="true" />;
```

## js/default-type-argument-redundant

**Default type parameters should be omitted.** Repeating the default adds nothing.

Off by default: add `"js/default-type-argument-redundant"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4157

Noncompliant:

```typescript
interface Box<T = string> { v: T } let b: Box<string>;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
interface Box<T = string> { v: T } let b: Box;
```

## js/union-alias

**Type aliases should be used.** The same union written three times belongs in a type alias.

SonarQube: S4323

Noncompliant:

```typescript
let a: 'x' | 'y' | 'z'; let b: 'x' | 'y' | 'z';
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type T = 'x' | 'y' | 'z'; let a: T; let b: T;
```

## js/redundant-cast

**Redundant casts and non-null assertions should be avoided.** The compiler already knows the type.

Off by default: add `"js/redundant-cast"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4325

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const a = 'x' as string;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const a = 'x';
```

## js/props-not-readonly

**React props should be read-only.** Props are never written to; readonly says so.

SonarQube: S6759

Noncompliant:

```tsx
interface P { name: string } function Hello(props: P) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; }
```

Compliant:

```tsx
interface P { readonly name: string } function Hello(props: P) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; }
```

## js/props-unused

**Unused React typed props should be removed.** A declared prop that the component never reads is dead weight.

SonarQube: S6767

Noncompliant:

```tsx
interface P { a: string; b: string } function Hello({ a }: P) { return <p>{a}</p>; }
```

Compliant:

```tsx
interface P { a: string } function Hello({ a }: P) { return <p>{a}</p>; }
```

## js/vue-props-untyped

**Vue component props should declare a type.** A prop listed by name accepts anything.

SonarQube: S8957

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps(['title']);
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps<{ title: string }>();
```

## js/vue-required-prop-with-default

**Vue props with a default value should not be required.** A required prop is always passed, so its default never applies.

SonarQube: S8950

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps({ size: { type: Number, required: true, default: 3 } });
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps({ size: { type: Number, default: 3 } });
```

## js/vue-prop-mutated

**Vue component props should not be mutated directly.** Props belong to the parent; writing to them breaks one-way data flow.

SonarQube: S8951

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps<{ n: number }>(); props.n = 2;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const props = defineProps<{ n: number }>(); const n = ref(props.n); n.value = 2;
```

## js/vue-event-not-declared

**Vue component events should be explicitly declared.** An undeclared event is invisible to tooling and falls through to the root element.

SonarQube: S8961

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const emit = defineEmits(['save']); emit('close');
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const emit = defineEmits(['save', 'close']); emit('close');
```

## js/vue-ref-untyped

**Vue ref and shallowRef calls should be typed.** ref(null) is typed as null, so nothing can be assigned later.

SonarQube: S8962

Noncompliant:

```typescript
import { ref } from 'vue'; const user = ref(null);
```

Compliant:

```typescript
import { ref } from 'vue'; const user = ref<User | null>(null);
```

## js/proptypes-unused

**Unused React typed props should be removed.** A prop type nobody reads is dead weight.

SonarQube: S6767

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function Hello(props) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; } Hello.propTypes = { name: PropTypes.string, age: PropTypes.number };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function Hello(props) { return <p>{props.name}</p>; } Hello.propTypes = { name: PropTypes.string };
```

## js/increment-in-expression

**Increment (++) and decrement (--) operators should not be used in a method call or mixed with other operators in an expression.** x++ inside a larger expression hides a side effect.

Off by default: add `"js/increment-in-expression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S881

Noncompliant:

```javascript
f(i++);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
f(i); i++;
```

## js/parameter-reassigned

**Initial values of parameters, caught exceptions, and loop variables should not be ignored.** Overwriting a parameter loses the value the caller passed.

SonarQube: S1226

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { a = 1; return a; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { const b = 1; return a + b; }
```

## js/table-for-layout

**HTML "<table>" should not be used for layout purposes.** A table that only positions content misleads screen readers.

SonarQube: S5257

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = <table role="presentation"><tbody /></table>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = <table><tbody /></table>;
```

## js/static-assets-after-session

**Static Assets should not serve session cookies.** A session middleware ahead of express.static sets cookies on every image and script.

SonarQube: S8441

Standards: [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const app = express(); app.use(session({ secret: s })); app.use(express.static('public'));
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const app = express(); app.use(express.static('public')); app.use(session({ secret: s }));
```

## js/login-without-session-regeneration

**A new session should be created during user authentication.** Keeping the pre-login session id allows session fixation.

SonarQube: S5876

Standards: [CWE-384](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/384.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function h(req, res) { req.login(user, function () { res.end(); }); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function h(req, res) { req.session.regenerate(function () { req.login(user, function () { res.end(); }); }); }
```

## js/overloads-not-grouped

**Method overloads should be grouped together.** Overloads separated by other members are easy to miss.

Off by default: add `"js/overloads-not-grouped"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4136

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class A { f(a: string): void; g(): void {} f(a: number): void; f(a: any) {} }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class A { f(a: string): void; f(a: number): void; f(a: any) {} g(): void {} }
```

## js/prefer-as-const

**"as const" assertions should be preferred.** 'a' as 'a' repeats the literal.

SonarQube: S6590

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const a = 'x' as 'x';
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const a = 'x' as const;
```

## js/angular-standalone-false

**Components, Directives, and Pipes should use standalone architecture.** standalone: false keeps a declaration tied to an NgModule.

Off by default: add `"js/angular-standalone-false"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7648

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Component({ selector: 'a', standalone: false }) class A {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({ selector: 'a' }) class A {}
```

## js/angular-outputs-metadata

**The "outputs" metadata property should not be used in Angular components and directives.** The @Output decorator or output() function is checked by the compiler.

SonarQube: S7654

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Component({ selector: 'a', outputs: ['changed'] }) class A {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({ selector: 'a' }) class A { changed = output(); }
```

## js/angular-lifecycle-misplaced

**Angular lifecycle methods should be used in the correct context.** ngOnInit on a service is never called.

SonarQube: S7641

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Injectable() class S { ngOnInit() {} }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({ selector: 'a' }) class C { ngOnInit() {} }
```

## js/var-used-before-declaration

**Variables declared with "var" should be declared before they are used.** var is hoisted, so an earlier use reads undefined.

Off by default: add `"js/var-used-before-declaration"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1526

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f() { g(x); var x = 1; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { var x = 1; g(x); }
```

## js/jsx-key-unstable

**JSX list components keys should match up between renders.** A key that changes every render makes React remount every item.

SonarQube: S6486

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = items.map((i) => <li key={Math.random()}>{i}</li>);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = items.map((i) => <li key={i.id}>{i.name}</li>);
```

## js/jsx-inline-spacing

**Spacing between inline elements should be explicit.** A line break between JSX children is dropped, gluing the words together.

SonarQube: S6772

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = <p><b>Hi</b>
  there</p>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = <p><b>Hi</b>{' '}
  there</p>;
```

## py/no-eval

**eval() and exec() should not be used.** eval()/exec() run arbitrary code and are a common injection vector.

Standards: [CWE-95](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/95.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
eval(user_input)
```

Compliant:

```python
ast.literal_eval(user_input)
```

## py/subprocess-shell

**Commands should not be run through a shell.** shell=True passes the command through /bin/sh, enabling command injection.

Standards: [CWE-78](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
subprocess.run(['ls', '-l'])
```

## py/pickle-load

**Untrusted data should not be unpickled.** pickle.load()/loads() can execute arbitrary code embedded in the data.

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
data = pickle.loads(blob)
```

Compliant:

```python
data = json.loads(blob)
```

## py/mutable-default

**Default arguments should not be mutable.** Default values are evaluated once, so a list/dict/set default is shared between calls.

SonarQube: S5717 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(items=[]):
    return items
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(items=None):
    return items or []
```

## py/assert-tuple

**assert should not be called on a tuple.** `assert (cond, msg)` asserts a non-empty tuple, which is always true.

SonarQube: S5905

Noncompliant:

```python
assert (x > 0, 'x must be positive')
```

Compliant:

```python
assert x > 0, 'x must be positive'
```

## py/bare-except

**Bare except clauses should not be used.** `except:` also catches SystemExit and KeyboardInterrupt. Catch a specific exception.

SonarQube: S5754 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

## py/none-comparison

**None should be compared with 'is'.** `== None` can be overridden by __eq__; use `is None` / `is not None`.

Noncompliant:

```python
if x == None:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be built from strings.** Formatting values into a query lets an attacker change what it does; pass them as parameters.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = %s" % user_id)
```

Compliant:

```python
cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = %s", (user_id,))
```

## py/cookie-not-secure

**Cookies should have the secure flag.** Without `secure`, the cookie is also sent over plain HTTP.

SonarQube: S2092

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), [CWE-315](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/315.html), [CWE-614](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/614.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, secure=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, secure=True)
```

## py/cookie-not-httponly

**Cookies should have the HttpOnly flag.** Without HttpOnly, scripts on the page can read the cookie.

SonarQube: S3330

Standards: [CWE-1004](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1004.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, httponly=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, httponly=True)
```

## py/tls-unverified

**Server certificates should be verified.** Turning verification off lets anyone on the network pose as the server.

SonarQube: S4830

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
requests.get(url, verify=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
requests.get(url)
```

## py/hostname-unverified

**Server hostnames should be verified.** Without a hostname check, a valid certificate for any other site is accepted.

SonarQube: S5527

Standards: [CWE-297](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/297.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
ctx.check_hostname = False
```

Compliant:

```python
ctx.check_hostname = True
```

## py/cleartext-url

**Clear-text protocols should not be used.** http, ftp and telnet send data, including credentials, unencrypted. Loopback and private addresses are not reported.

SonarQube: S5332 (related)

Standards: [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html), [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
api = 'http://api.example-shop.io/v1'
```

Compliant:

```python
api = 'https://api.example-shop.io/v1'
local = 'http://localhost:8000'
```

## py/random-secret

**Pseudorandom generators should not produce secrets.** random is predictable; tokens and passwords built from it can be guessed. Use the secrets module.

SonarQube: S2245 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html), [CWE-338](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/338.html), [CWE-1241](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1241.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
session_token = random.randint(0, 10**9)
```

Compliant:

```python
session_token = secrets.token_hex(16)
jitter = random.random()
```

## py/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD5 and SHA-1 have known collisions and are unsuitable for security purposes.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.md5(data)
```

Compliant:

```python
h = hashlib.sha256(data)
```

## py/weak-cipher

**Cipher algorithms should be robust.** DES, RC2, RC4, Blowfish and similar ciphers are broken or have small blocks.

SonarQube: S5547 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
cipher = DES.new(key, DES.MODE_ECB)
```

Compliant:

```python
cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_GCM)
```

## py/xxe

**XML parsers should not be vulnerable to XXE attacks.** Resolving external entities lets a crafted document read local files or reach internal hosts.

SonarQube: S2755

Standards: [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html), [CWE-827](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/827.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
parser = etree.XMLParser(resolve_entities=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
parser = etree.XMLParser(resolve_entities=False)
```

## py/world-permissions

**File permissions should not be world-accessible.** Mode bits for 'other' let every user on the machine read or change the file.

SonarQube: S2612

Standards: [CWE-266](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/266.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
os.chmod(path, 0o777)
```

Compliant:

```python
os.chmod(path, 0o600)
```

## py/public-tmp

**Temporary files should not be created in publicly writable directories.** Other users can pre-create or swap files in /tmp, so a predictable path can be hijacked.

SonarQube: S5443

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
path = '/tmp/report.csv'
```

Compliant:

```python
path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix='.csv')[1]
```

## py/mktemp

**Insecure temporary file creation methods should not be used.** tempfile.mktemp returns a name without creating the file, which leaves a window for an attacker.

SonarQube: S5445

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
name = tempfile.mktemp()
```

Compliant:

```python
fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp()
```

## py/weak-tls-version

**Weak SSL/TLS protocols should not be used.** SSLv2, SSLv3, TLS 1.0 and TLS 1.1 have known attacks.

SonarQube: S4423

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLSv1)
```

Compliant:

```python
ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_CLIENT)
```

## py/weak-key-size

**Cryptographic keys should be generated with strong parameters.** RSA and DSA keys under 2048 bits can be factored with modest resources.

SonarQube: S4426

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
key = RSA.generate(1024)
```

Compliant:

```python
key = RSA.generate(3072)
```

## py/jwt-unverified

**JWTs should be signed and verified.** Decoding without verification accepts tokens anyone could have forged.

SonarQube: S5659

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
claims = jwt.decode(token, key, verify=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
claims = jwt.decode(token, key, algorithms=['HS256'])
```

## py/autoescape-disabled

**Auto-escaping in HTML template engines should not be disabled.** Without auto-escaping, any variable rendered into a page can inject script.

SonarQube: S5247

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
env = Environment(autoescape=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
env = Environment(autoescape=True)
```

## py/csrf-disabled

**CSRF protections should not be disabled.** Exempting a view, or turning the check off, lets other sites make requests as the signed-in user.

SonarQube: S4502 (related)

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
@csrf_exempt
def pay(request):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def pay(request):
    pass
```

## py/debug-enabled

**Debugging features should not be enabled in production.** Debug mode exposes stack traces and, in Flask, an interactive console.

SonarQube: S4507 (related)

Standards: [CWE-215](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/215.html), [CWE-489](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/489.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A10:2025 Mishandling of Exceptional Conditions, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
app.run(debug=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
app.run()
```

## py/cors-any-origin

**CORS policies should be restricted to trusted origins.** Allowing every origin lets any site read the responses on behalf of a signed-in user.

SonarQube: S5122 (related)

Standards: [CWE-346](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/346.html), [CWE-942](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/942.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
CORS(app, origins='*')
```

Compliant:

```python
CORS(app, origins=['https://app.example.com'])
```

## py/hardcoded-credential

**Credentials should not be hard-coded.** A password or key in source is visible to everyone with the code and stays in its history.

SonarQube: S2068, S6437 (related), S6418 (related)

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
password = 'Tr0ub4dor&3'
```

Compliant:

```python
password = os.environ['DB_PASSWORD']
```

## py/bind-all-interfaces

**Web servers should not bind to all network interfaces.** 0.0.0.0 makes the service reachable from every network the machine is on.

SonarQube: S8392

Standards: [CWE-668](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/668.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
app.run(host='0.0.0.0')
```

Compliant:

```python
app.run(host='127.0.0.1')
```

## py/flask-secret-key

**Flask secret keys should not be disclosed.** A secret key in source lets anyone forge session cookies.

SonarQube: S6779

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
app.secret_key = 'dev-secret-123'
```

Compliant:

```python
app.secret_key = os.environ['SECRET_KEY']
```

## py/jwt-literal-secret

**JWT secret keys should not be disclosed.** A signing key in source lets anyone mint valid tokens.

SonarQube: S6781

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
token = jwt.encode(payload, 'my-secret', algorithm='HS256')
```

Compliant:

```python
token = jwt.encode(payload, SECRET, algorithm='HS256')
```

## py/aws-access-key

**Long-term AWS access keys should not be used directly in code.** An access key ID in source identifies a credential that can be abused if the code leaks.

SonarQube: S7625

Standards: [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
key = 'AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE'
```

Compliant:

```python
key = os.environ['AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID']
```

## py/empty-db-password

**A secure password should be used when connecting to a database.** An empty password means anyone who can reach the database can use it.

SonarQube: S2115

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
conn = psycopg2.connect(host=h, password='')
```

Compliant:

```python
conn = psycopg2.connect(host=h, password=pw)
```

## py/torch-load-unsafe

**Models should not be loaded with torch.load without restricting deserialization.** torch.load unpickles its input, which can run arbitrary code from an untrusted file.

SonarQube: S6985 (related)

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), [CWE-913](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/913.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
model = torch.load(path)
```

Compliant:

```python
model = torch.load(path, weights_only=True)
```

## py/self-assignment

**Variables should not be self-assigned.** Assigning a value to itself does nothing and usually hides a typo.

SonarQube: S1656

Noncompliant:

```python
x = x
```

Compliant:

```python
x = y
```

## py/identical-operands

**Identical expressions should not be used on both sides of a binary operator.** `a == a` is always true and `a - a` always zero: one side is almost certainly a typo.

SonarQube: S1764

Noncompliant:

```python
if a > a:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a > b:
    pass
```

## py/duplicate-condition

**Related if/elif statements should not have the same condition.** The second branch can never run.

SonarQube: S1862

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
elif a:
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
elif b:
    g()
```

## py/identical-branches

**All branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** If every branch does the same thing, the condition decides nothing.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
else:
    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
else:
    g()
```

## py/single-iteration-loop

**Loops with at most one iteration should be refactored.** A loop whose body always ends in a jump runs its body once.

SonarQube: S1751

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    if ok(x):
        return x
```

## py/unreachable-code

**All code should be reachable.** Statements after a return, raise, break or continue never run.

SonarQube: S1763

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    return 1
```

## py/jump-in-finally

**Jump statements should not occur in finally blocks.** A return, break or continue in finally discards any exception that is propagating.

SonarQube: S1143

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    try:
        g()
    finally:
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    try:
        g()
    finally:
        cleanup()
    return 1
```

## py/bare-raise-outside-handler

**Bare raise statements should only be used in except blocks.** With no exception being handled, a bare raise fails with RuntimeError.

SonarQube: S5747

Noncompliant:

```python
raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    raise
```

## py/unreachable-except

**All except blocks should be able to catch exceptions.** An except clause after a catch-all one never runs.

SonarQube: S1045

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except Exception:
    pass
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
except Exception:
    pass
```

## py/exception-not-raised

**Exceptions should not be created without being raised.** `ValueError('x')` on its own builds an object and drops it.

SonarQube: S3984

Noncompliant:

```python
ValueError('bad')
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('bad')
```

## py/raise-non-exception

**Raised exceptions must derive from BaseException.** Only exception classes and instances can be raised.

SonarQube: S5632

Noncompliant:

```python
raise 'failed'
```

Compliant:

```python
raise RuntimeError('failed')
```

## py/init-returns-value

**__init__ should not return a value.** A constructor that returns something other than None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S2734

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.x = 1
```

## py/return-outside-function

**return and yield should not be used outside functions.** They are syntax errors at module or class level.

SonarQube: S2711

Noncompliant:

```python
return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
```

## py/misleading-operator

**Non-existent operators like =+ should not be used.** `x =+ 1` assigns +1, which is almost never what was meant.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```python
x =+ 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x += 1
```

## py/open-mode

**The open builtin should be called with a valid mode.** An invalid mode string raises ValueError at runtime.

SonarQube: S5828

Noncompliant:

```python
f = open(path, 'rw')
```

Compliant:

```python
f = open(path, 'r+')
```

## py/dict-duplicate-key

**Dictionary literals should not have duplicate keys.** Later values silently replace earlier ones.

SonarQube: S5780

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {'a': 1, 'a': 2}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {'a': 1, 'b': 2}
```

## py/set-duplicate-value

**Set literals should not have duplicate values.** The repeated value does nothing.

SonarQube: S5781

Noncompliant:

```python
s = {1, 2, 1}
```

Compliant:

```python
s = {1, 2, 3}
```

## py/float-equality

**Floating point numbers should not be tested for equality.** Rounding makes two mathematically equal results differ in the last bits.

SonarQube: S1244

Noncompliant:

```python
if total == 0.1:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if math.isclose(total, 0.1):
    pass
```

## py/assert-constant

**Assertions should not succeed unconditionally.** `assert True` checks nothing.

SonarQube: S5914

Noncompliant:

```python
assert True
```

Compliant:

```python
assert ready
```

## py/length-comparison

**Collection size comparisons should make sense.** A length is never negative, so `len(x) < 0` is always false and `len(x) >= 0` always true.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```python
if len(x) < 0:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if len(x) == 0:
    pass
```

## py/numpy-nan-equality

**Equality checks should not be made against numpy.nan.** NaN is not equal to anything, itself included, so the check is always false.

SonarQube: S6725

Noncompliant:

```python
if x == np.nan:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if np.isnan(x):
    pass
```

## py/isclose-without-abs-tol

**The abs_tol parameter should be provided when comparing to zero.** With the default relative tolerance only, nothing but zero is close to zero.

SonarQube: S6727

Noncompliant:

```python
math.isclose(x, 0)
```

Compliant:

```python
math.isclose(x, 0, abs_tol=1e-9)
```

## py/dict-fromkeys-mutable

**Mutable default values should not be used with dict.fromkeys().** Every key shares the same list or dict.

SonarQube: S8508

Noncompliant:

```python
d = dict.fromkeys(keys, [])
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {k: [] for k in keys}
```

## py/enum-dataclass

**Enum classes should not be decorated with @dataclass.** The decorator generates methods that clash with Enum's.

SonarQube: S8490

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class Color(Enum):
    RED = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Color(Enum):
    RED = 1
```

## py/typevar-variance

**TypeVars should not be both covariant and contravariant.** The two are mutually exclusive.

SonarQube: S8515

Noncompliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T', covariant=True, contravariant=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T', covariant=True)
```

## py/stopiteration-in-generator

**StopIteration should not be raised inside generators.** Since Python 3.7 it becomes RuntimeError.

SonarQube: S8493

Noncompliant:

```python
def g():
    yield 1
    raise StopIteration
```

Compliant:

```python
def g():
    yield 1
    return
```

## py/property-without-return

**Property methods should have a return statement.** A property that returns nothing always reads as None.

SonarQube: S8504

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def size(self):
        self.x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def size(self):
        return 1
```

## py/async-blocking-sleep

**Non-blocking sleep functions should be used in asynchronous code.** time.sleep stops the whole event loop.

SonarQube: S7488

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    time.sleep(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await asyncio.sleep(1)
```

## py/async-blocking-subprocess

**Async functions should not contain synchronous subprocess calls.** A blocking subprocess call stops the event loop until the process ends.

SonarQube: S7487

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    subprocess.run(['ls'])
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec('ls')
```

## py/async-blocking-http

**Async functions should not contain synchronous HTTP client calls.** requests blocks the event loop while waiting for the response.

SonarQube: S7499

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    requests.get(url)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await client.get(url)
```

## py/async-blocking-open

**Async functions should not contain synchronous file operations.** open() blocks the event loop on slow disks and network mounts.

SonarQube: S7493

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    data = open(p).read()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    data = await aiofiles_read(p)
```

## py/async-blocking-input

**Async functions should not contain input() calls.** input() waits for the user and blocks everything else.

SonarQube: S7501

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    name = input()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    name = await ask()
```

## py/async-blocking-os

**Async functions should not contain synchronous OS calls.** os.system and os.popen block until the command finishes.

SonarQube: S7489

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    os.system('ls')
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await run('ls')
```

## py/task-not-saved

**Asyncio tasks should be saved to prevent premature garbage collection.** The event loop keeps only a weak reference to a task, so it can be collected before it finishes.

SonarQube: S7502

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    asyncio.create_task(work())
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    task = asyncio.create_task(work())
```

## py/torch-module-init

**Subclasses of torch.nn.Module should call the initializer.** Without super().__init__(), the module's parameters aren't registered.

SonarQube: S6978

Noncompliant:

```python
class Net(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        self.fc = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Net(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.fc = 1
```

## py/fastapi-reload-object

**FastAPI applications should be passed as import strings when using reload.** uvicorn can only reload an app it can import by name.

SonarQube: S8397

Noncompliant:

```python
uvicorn.run(app, reload=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
uvicorn.run('main:app', reload=True)
```

## py/flask-header-keyerror

**HTTP headers should be accessed safely.** A missing header makes `request.headers['X']` raise KeyError, and the client sees a 500.

SonarQube: S8371

Noncompliant:

```python
token = request.headers['X-Token']
```

Compliant:

```python
token = request.headers.get('X-Token')
```

## py/doubled-prefix-operator

**Doubled prefix operators not and ~ should not be used.** `not not x` and `~~x` cancel out, or are a clumsy way of casting.

SonarQube: S2761

Noncompliant:

```python
y = not not x
```

Compliant:

```python
y = bool(x)
```

## py/needless-pass

**pass should not be used needlessly.** A block that has other statements doesn't need pass.

SonarQube: S2772

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
```

## py/loop-else-without-break

**Loops without break should not have else clauses.** else on a loop runs when the loop wasn't broken out of, so without a break it always runs.

SonarQube: S2836

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    f(x)
else:
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    if ok(x):
        break
else:
    g()
```

## py/nested-conditional

**Conditional expressions should not be nested.** A ternary inside a ternary is hard to read.

SonarQube: S3358

Noncompliant:

```python
x = a if p else (b if q else c)
```

Compliant:

```python
if p:
    x = a
elif q:
    x = b
else:
    x = c
```

## py/redundant-jump

**Jump statements should not be redundant.** A return at the end of a function, or a continue at the end of a loop, changes nothing.

SonarQube: S3626

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    return
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
```

## py/empty-function

**Functions and methods should not be empty.** An empty body gives no hint whether it is unfinished or deliberate.

SonarQube: S1186

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    # Deliberately empty: subclasses override.
    pass
```

## py/empty-nested-block

**Nested blocks of code should not be left empty.** An empty if, loop or except hides whether the case was forgotten.

SonarQube: S108

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    log.warning('ignored')
```

## py/generic-exception-raised

**Exception and BaseException should not be raised.** Callers can't catch a generic exception without catching everything.

SonarQube: S112

Noncompliant:

```python
raise Exception('bad')
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('bad')
```

## py/duplicated-string

**String literals should not be duplicated.** A literal repeated across the file is easy to change in one place and miss in the others.

SonarQube: S1192

Noncompliant:

```python
a = 'connection failed'
b = 'connection failed'
c = 'connection failed'
```

Compliant:

```python
MSG = 'connection failed'
a = MSG
b = MSG
c = MSG
```

## py/inverted-boolean

**Boolean checks should not be inverted.** `not a == b` is clearer as `a != b`.

SonarQube: S1940

Noncompliant:

```python
if not a == b:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a != b:
    pass
```

## py/redundant-parentheses

**Unnecessary parentheses should be removed.** Parentheses around a whole condition add noise.

SonarQube: S1110

Noncompliant:

```python
if (x > 1):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x > 1:
    pass
```

## py/mergeable-if

**Mergeable if statements should be combined.** An if whose only content is another if can use `and`.

SonarQube: S1066

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    if b:
        f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a and b:
    f()
```

## py/constant-condition

**Constants should not be used as conditions.** The branch is either always or never taken.

SonarQube: S5797

Noncompliant:

```python
if True:
    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if ready:
    f()
```

## py/statement-without-effect

**Non-empty statements should change control flow or have a side effect.** An expression whose value is thrown away does nothing.

SonarQube: S905

Noncompliant:

```python
x == 1
```

Compliant:

```python
assert x == 1
```

## py/builtin-shadowed

**Builtins should not be shadowed by local variables.** Reusing `list` or `id` as a name hides the builtin for the rest of the scope.

SonarQube: S5806

Noncompliant:

```python
list = [1, 2]
```

Compliant:

```python
items = [1, 2]
```

## py/method-without-self

**Instance and class methods should have a self or cls parameter.** Without it, calling the method on an instance fails.

SonarQube: S5719

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run():
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
```

## py/implicit-string-concatenation

**Implicit string concatenations should not be confusing.** In a list or call, a missing comma silently joins two strings.

SonarQube: S5799

Noncompliant:

```python
names = ['a' 'b', 'c']
```

Compliant:

```python
names = ['a', 'b', 'c']
```

## py/startswith-slice

**startswith and endswith should be used instead of string slicing.** `s[:3] == 'abc'` hides what it checks and builds a new string.

SonarQube: S6659

Noncompliant:

```python
if s[:3] == 'abc':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if s.startswith('abc'):
    pass
```

## py/type-comparison

**isinstance() should be preferred to direct type comparisons.** `type(x) == T` fails for subclasses.

SonarQube: S6660

Noncompliant:

```python
if type(x) == int:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, int):
    pass
```

## py/empty-literal

**Literal syntax should be preferred for empty collections.** `[]` and `{}` are faster and clearer than `list()` and `dict()`.

SonarQube: S7498

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = []
```

## py/generator-in-constructor

**Comprehensions should be used instead of constructors around generator expressions.** `list(x for x in y)` builds a generator to throw it away.

SonarQube: S7494

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list(a for a in b)
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

## py/comprehension-copy

**Comprehensions only used to copy should be replaced.** `[x for x in y]` is `list(y)`.

SonarQube: S7500

Noncompliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = list(b)
```

## py/list-in-for

**list() should be avoided when iterating over an iterable.** Copying the iterable first costs memory for nothing, unless the loop changes it.

SonarQube: S7504

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in list(items):
    f(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in items:
    f(x)
```

## py/redundant-collection-call

**Redundant collection functions should be avoided.** `sorted(list(x))` converts twice.

SonarQube: S7508

Noncompliant:

```python
x = sorted(list(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = sorted(y)
```

## py/reversed-sorted

**sorted() should not be passed to reversed().** `sorted(x, reverse=True)` does it in one step.

SonarQube: S7510

Noncompliant:

```python
x = reversed(sorted(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = sorted(y, reverse=True)
```

## py/set-of-sorted

**sorted should not be wrapped directly inside set.** A set has no order, so sorting first is wasted.

SonarQube: S7516

Noncompliant:

```python
x = set(sorted(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = set(y)
```

## py/reversed-wrapper

**Passing a reversed iterable to set(), sorted() or reversed() is pointless.** The order is thrown away or restored.

SonarQube: S7511

Noncompliant:

```python
x = set(reversed(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = set(y)
```

## py/dict-keys-iteration

**The .keys() method should not be called when testing or iterating over a dictionary.** A dictionary already tests and iterates its keys.

SonarQube: S8521

Noncompliant:

```python
if k in d.keys():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if k in d:
    pass
```

## py/list-first-element

**list(...)[0] should not be used to get the first element.** It builds a whole list for one item.

SonarQube: S8519

Noncompliant:

```python
first = list(items)[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
first = next(iter(items))
```

## py/sorted-for-extreme

**sorted() should not be used with indexing to find a minimum or maximum.** Sorting is O(n log n); min() and max() are O(n).

SonarQube: S8517

Noncompliant:

```python
lowest = sorted(xs)[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
lowest = min(xs)
```

## py/sum-concatenates-lists

**sum() should not be used with an empty list to concatenate lists.** It copies the growing list on every step.

SonarQube: S8520

Noncompliant:

```python
flat = sum(lists, [])
```

Compliant:

```python
flat = list(itertools.chain.from_iterable(lists))
```

## py/startswith-or

**startswith and endswith calls should use tuples instead of multiple calls.** `s.startswith(('a', 'b'))` replaces two calls.

SonarQube: S8513

Noncompliant:

```python
ok = s.startswith('a') or s.startswith('b')
```

Compliant:

```python
ok = s.startswith(('a', 'b'))
```

## py/loop-variable-reused

**Loop variables should not be reused in nested loops.** The inner loop overwrites the outer variable.

SonarQube: S8510

Noncompliant:

```python
for i in a:
    for i in b:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
for i in a:
    for j in b:
        pass
```

## py/unittest-specific-assertion

**The most specific unittest assertion should be used.** assertEqual shows both values when it fails; assertTrue(a == b) shows only False.

SonarQube: S5906

Noncompliant:

```python
self.assertTrue(a == b)
```

Compliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(a, b)
```

## py/skip-without-reason

**A reason should be provided when skipping a test.** Without a reason, nobody knows when the skip can go.

SonarQube: S1607

Noncompliant:

```python
@unittest.skip
def test_a():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@unittest.skip('flaky on CI')
def test_a():
    pass
```

## py/numpy-deprecated-alias

**Deprecated NumPy aliases of built-in types should not be used.** np.int, np.float and similar were removed in NumPy 1.24.

SonarQube: S6730

Noncompliant:

```python
x = np.int(3)
```

Compliant:

```python
x = int(3)
```

## py/numpy-randomstate

**numpy.random.Generator should be preferred to RandomState.** Generator has better statistical quality and is faster.

SonarQube: S6711

Noncompliant:

```python
rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
```

Compliant:

```python
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
```

## py/pandas-inplace

**inplace=True should not be used when modifying a Pandas DataFrame.** It often copies anyway, and it blocks method chaining.

SonarQube: S6734

Noncompliant:

```python
df.drop('a', axis=1, inplace=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
df = df.drop('a', axis=1)
```

## py/numpy-where-condition

**np.nonzero should be preferred over np.where with only a condition.** where(cond) is nonzero(cond) under a name that suggests it selects between values.

SonarQube: S6729

Noncompliant:

```python
idx = np.where(mask)
```

Compliant:

```python
idx = np.nonzero(mask)
```

## py/numpy-array-generator

**Passing a list to np.array should be preferred over passing a generator.** np.array can't consume a generator and creates a 0-d object array.

SonarQube: S6714

Noncompliant:

```python
a = np.array(x for x in xs)
```

Compliant:

```python
a = np.array([x for x in xs])
```

## py/pytz-timezone-argument

**pytz.timezone should not be passed to the datetime constructor.** The result is an odd historical offset, not the zone's real one.

SonarQube: S6887

Noncompliant:

```python
d = datetime(2024, 1, 1, tzinfo=pytz.timezone('Europe/Amsterdam'))
```

Compliant:

```python
d = datetime(2024, 1, 1, tzinfo=ZoneInfo('Europe/Amsterdam'))
```

## py/pytz-import

**zoneinfo should be preferred to pytz.** zoneinfo is in the standard library since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S6890

Noncompliant:

```python
import pytz
```

Compliant:

```python
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
```

## py/naive-utc-datetime

**Timezone-aware datetime objects should be preferred.** utcnow() returns a naive datetime that looks like local time.

SonarQube: S6903

Noncompliant:

```python
now = datetime.utcnow()
```

Compliant:

```python
now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
```

## py/typing-union

**Union type expressions should be preferred over typing.Union.** `int | str` is shorter and needs no import.

SonarQube: S6546

Noncompliant:

```python
x: Union[int, str] = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x: int | str = 1
```

## py/django-null-string-field

**null=True should not be used on string-based fields.** Strings then have two empty values, '' and NULL.

SonarQube: S6553

Noncompliant:

```python
name = models.CharField(max_length=10, null=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
name = models.CharField(max_length=10)
```

## py/django-locals-render

**locals() should not be passed to a Django render() function.** It exposes every local variable to the template.

SonarQube: S6556

Noncompliant:

```python
render(request, 'a.html', locals())
```

Compliant:

```python
render(request, 'a.html', {'a': a})
```

## py/django-modelform-all

**Fields of a Django ModelForm should be defined explicitly.** '__all__' exposes any field added to the model later.

SonarQube: S6559

Noncompliant:

```python
fields = '__all__'
```

Compliant:

```python
fields = ['name', 'email']
```

## py/typevar-name

**TypeVar names should match their assigned variable.** A mismatch confuses readers and type checkers.

SonarQube: S8507

Noncompliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('U')
```

Compliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T')
```

## py/duplicate-base-class

**Classes should not inherit from the same base class multiple times.** The second listing raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S8509

Noncompliant:

```python
class A(B, B):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A(B, C):
    pass
```

## py/duplicate-class-field

**Class fields should not be defined multiple times.** Only the last assignment survives.

SonarQube: S8512

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    x = 1
    x = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    x = 1
    y = 2
```

## py/torch-variable

**torch.tensor should be used instead of torch.autograd.Variable.** Variable has been merged into Tensor since PyTorch 0.4.

SonarQube: S6979

Noncompliant:

```python
v = torch.autograd.Variable(t)
```

Compliant:

```python
v = torch.tensor(t)
```

## py/dataloader-workers

**The num_workers parameter should be specified for DataLoader.** The default loads data on the main process.

SonarQube: S6983

Noncompliant:

```python
loader = DataLoader(ds)
```

Compliant:

```python
loader = DataLoader(ds, num_workers=4)
```

## py/aws-region-hardcoded

**AWS region should not be set with a hardcoded string.** A literal region makes the code awkward to deploy elsewhere.

SonarQube: S6262

Noncompliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3', region_name='eu-west-1')
```

Compliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3')
```

## py/re-sub-literal

**str.replace should be preferred to re.sub.** When the pattern has no special characters, replace() is simpler and faster.

SonarQube: S5361

Noncompliant:

```python
s = re.sub('foo', 'bar', t)
```

Compliant:

```python
s = t.replace('foo', 'bar')
```

## py/defaultdict-keyword

**defaultdict should not be initialized with default_factory as a keyword.** defaultdict takes the factory as its first positional argument.

SonarQube: S7507

Noncompliant:

```python
d = defaultdict(default_factory=list)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = defaultdict(list)
```

## py/dict-comprehension-static-key

**Dictionary comprehensions should not use a static key.** Every iteration overwrites the same entry.

SonarQube: S7506

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {'k': v for v in vs}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {v: 1 for v in vs}
```

## py/dict-fromkeys-comprehension

**Populating a dictionary with a constant value should be done with dict.fromkeys.** A comprehension that maps every key to the same constant is fromkeys.

SonarQube: S7519

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {k: 0 for k in keys}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = dict.fromkeys(keys, 0)
```

## py/map-filter-lambda

**Generators and comprehensions should be preferred over map and filter with a lambda.** A comprehension says the same without the lambda.

SonarQube: S7505

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list(map(lambda a: a + 1, xs))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a + 1 for a in xs]
```

## py/any-all-list

**List comprehensions should not be used with any() and all().** A list is built completely before any() can stop at the first hit.

SonarQube: S7492

Noncompliant:

```python
ok = any([x > 1 for x in xs])
```

Compliant:

```python
ok = any(x > 1 for x in xs)
```

## py/literal-in-constructor

**Creation of collections with literals or comprehensions should not be wrapped.** `list([...])` copies a list it just made.

SonarQube: S7496

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list([a for a in b])
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

## py/empty-collection-membership

**Membership tests should not use empty collections.** Nothing is a member of an empty collection.

SonarQube: S8503

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in []:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x in allowed:
    pass
```

## py/logging-error-in-except

**logging.exception() should be used instead of logging.error() in exception handlers.** exception() logs the traceback too.

SonarQube: S8572

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    logger.error('failed')
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
```
