```python
    logger.exception('failed')
```

## py/python2-print

**The print statement should not be used.** It is Python 2 syntax.

SonarQube: PrintStatementUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
print 'hello'
```

Compliant:

```python
print('hello')
```

## py/python2-exec

**The exec statement should not be used.** It is Python 2 syntax, and exec runs arbitrary code.

SonarQube: ExecStatementUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
exec 'x = 1'
```

Compliant:

```python
exec('x = 1')
```

## py/python2-inequality

**<> should not be used to test inequality.** It is Python 2 syntax.

SonarQube: InequalityUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
if a <> b:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a != b:
    pass
```

## py/except-only-reraises

**except clauses should do more than re-raise.** An except that only raises again changes nothing, unless it shields a later clause.

SonarQube: S2737

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    log()
    raise
```

## py/all-not-strings

**Only strings should be listed in __all__.** Any other entry makes `from m import *` fail.

SonarQube: S2823

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
__all__ = [f]
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
__all__ = ['f']
```

## py/iter-returns-non-iterator

**__iter__ should return an iterator.** Returning a list or other container makes iter() raise TypeError.

SonarQube: S2876

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __iter__(self):
        return [1, 2]
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __iter__(self):
        return iter([1, 2])
```

## py/is-with-literal

**Identity comparisons should not be used with cached types.** `x is 'a'` and `x is 1` depend on interning, which is an implementation detail.

SonarQube: S5795

Noncompliant:

```python
if x is 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x == 'a':
    pass
```

## py/is-with-new-object

**New objects should not be created only to check their identity.** `x is []` is always False: the literal is a fresh object.

SonarQube: S5796

Noncompliant:

```python
if x is []:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if not x:
    pass
```

## py/identity-dissimilar-types

**Identity operators should not be used with dissimilar types.** Operands of different kinds never share an identity.

SonarQube: S3403

Noncompliant:

```python
if 1 is 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/inconsistent-return

**Functions should use return consistently.** Some paths return a value and others a bare return, so callers get None unexpectedly.

SonarQube: S3801

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1
    return
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1
    return 0
```

## py/return-in-generator

**return with a value should not be used in an async generator.** An async generator cannot return a value: it is a SyntaxError.

SonarQube: S2712

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    yield 1
    return 2
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    yield 1
    return
```

## py/collection-replaced

**Collection content should not be replaced unconditionally.** The first assignment to the same key is overwritten before it is read.

SonarQube: S4143

Noncompliant:

```python
d['k'] = 1
d['k'] = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
d['k'] = 2
```

## py/dead-store

**Unused assignments should be removed.** The value assigned first is overwritten before anything reads it.

SonarQube: S1854

Noncompliant:

```python
x = compute()
x = other()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = other()
```

## py/exception-cause-literal

**Exception causes must be exceptions or None.** `raise A from 'text'` fails with TypeError.

SonarQube: S5707

Noncompliant:

```python
raise ValueError('x') from 'reason'
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('x') from err
```

## py/except-boolean-expression

**Boolean expressions of exceptions should not be used in except statements.** `except A or B` catches only A: or evaluates to its first operand.

SonarQube: S5714

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError or TypeError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (ValueError, TypeError):
    pass
```

## py/bare-raise-in-finally

**Bare raise statements should not be used in finally blocks.** Outside a handler a bare raise fails, and after one it hides the real error.

SonarQube: S5704

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
finally:
    raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
finally:
    cleanup()
```

## py/exit-reraises

**__exit__ should not re-raise the exception it receives.** Returning a falsy value already lets the exception propagate.

SonarQube: S5706

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __exit__(self, t, v, tb):
        raise v
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __exit__(self, t, v, tb):
        return False
```

## py/exception-base-class

**Custom exception classes should inherit from Exception.** A class with no base cannot be raised or caught.

SonarQube: S5709

Noncompliant:

```python
class AppError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class AppError(Exception):
    pass
```

## py/not-implemented-error

**Special methods should return NotImplemented, not raise NotImplementedError.** Operators fall back to the other operand only when NotImplemented is returned.

SonarQube: S5712

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        raise NotImplementedError
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        return NotImplemented
```

## py/special-method-arity

**Special methods should have an expected number of parameters.** Python calls them with a fixed signature.

SonarQube: S5722

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self, extra):
        return 0
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 0
```

## py/property-arity

**Property getter, setter and deleter methods should have the expected number of parameters.** A getter takes self, a setter self and the value.

SonarQube: S5724

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def x(self, y):
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def x(self):
        return 1
```

## py/call-non-callable

**Calls should not be made to non-callable values.** Calling a literal raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5756

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 'abc'()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 'abc'
```

## py/in-on-non-container

**in and not in should be used on objects supporting them.** A number or None has no members to test.

SonarQube: S5642

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in 5:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x in (5,):
    pass
```

## py/subscript-on-scalar

**Item operations should be done on objects supporting them.** Indexing a number or None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5644

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 5[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [5][0]
```

## py/iterate-scalar

**Iteration should use an Iterable object.** Looping over a number or None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S3862

Noncompliant:

```python
for i in 5:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
for i in range(5):
    pass
```

## py/incompatible-operands

**Operators should be used on compatible types.** Adding a string and a number, or doing arithmetic on None, raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5607

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 'a' + 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 'a' + str(1)
```

## py/unhashable-member

**Set members and dictionary keys should be hashable.** Lists, dicts and sets raise TypeError as keys.

SonarQube: S6662

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {[1]: 'a'}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {(1,): 'a'}
```

## py/float-index

**Sequence indexes must have an __index__ method.** A float cannot index a sequence.

SonarQube: S6663

Noncompliant:

```python
x = items[1.5]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = items[1]
```

## py/string-format-count

**String formatting should not lead to runtime errors.** Fewer arguments than placeholders raises TypeError or IndexError.

SonarQube: S2275

Noncompliant:

```python
msg = '%s and %s' % (a,)
```

Compliant:

```python
msg = '%s and %s' % (a, b)
```

## py/fstring-without-fields

**String formatting should be used correctly.** An f-string with no field is just a string.

SonarQube: S3457

Noncompliant:

```python
msg = f'hello'
```

Compliant:

```python
msg = 'hello'
```

## py/infinite-recursion

**Recursion should not be infinite.** A function that calls itself with no branch can never stop.

SonarQube: S2190

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(n):
    return f(n - 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(n):
    if n == 0:
        return 0
    return f(n - 1)
```

## py/overwritten-parameter

**Function parameters initial values should not be ignored.** The first thing the function does is replace the argument it was given.

SonarQube: S1226

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    x = 1
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    y = x + 1
    return y
```

## py/private-method-unused

**Unused class-private methods should be removed.** Nothing in the class calls it, and name mangling hides it from outside.

SonarQube: S1144

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __hidden(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __hidden(self):
        pass
    def run(self):
        self.__hidden()
```

## py/private-attribute-unread

**Unread private attributes should be removed.** The value is written and never read back.

SonarQube: S4487

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.__x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.__x = 1
    def x(self):
        return self.__x
```

## py/identical-functions

**Functions and methods should not have identical implementations.** Two copies of a body drift apart.

SonarQube: S4144

Noncompliant:

```python
def a():
    x = 1
    return x
def b():
    x = 1
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def a():
    x = 1
    return x
def b():
    return a()
```

## py/duplicated-branch

**Two branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** Merge the conditions instead of repeating the body.

SonarQube: S1871

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    x = 1
    y = 2
elif b:
    x = 1
    y = 2
else:
    z = 3
```

Compliant:

```python
if a or b:
    x = 1
    y = 2
else:
    z = 3
```

## py/loop-variable-closure

**Functions and lambdas should not reference variables defined in enclosing loops.** The closure sees the variable's final value, not the one from its iteration.

SonarQube: S1515

Noncompliant:

```python
fs = []
for i in r:
    fs.append(lambda: i)
```

Compliant:

```python
fs = []
for i in r:
    fs.append(lambda i=i: i)
```

## py/class-name

**Class names should comply with a naming convention.** PascalCase makes classes recognisable.

SonarQube: S101

Noncompliant:

```python
class my_class:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class MyClass:
    pass
```

## py/method-name

**Method names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S100

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def doThing(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def do_thing(self):
        pass
```

## py/function-name

**Function names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S1542

Noncompliant:

```python
def doThing():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def do_thing():
    pass
```

## py/field-name

**Field names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S116

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.myField = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.my_field = 1
```

## py/local-name

**Local variable and function parameter names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S117

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(myArg):
    return myArg
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(my_arg):
    return my_arg
```

## py/self-parameter-name

**self should be the first argument to instance methods.** Another name confuses readers and tools.

SonarQube: S5720

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(this):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
```

## py/cls-parameter-name

**The first argument to class methods should follow the naming convention.** Use cls.

SonarQube: S2710

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @classmethod
    def make(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @classmethod
    def make(cls):
        pass
```

## py/wildcard-import

**Wildcard imports should not be used.** They hide where names come from and can shadow each other.

SonarQube: S2208

Noncompliant:

```python
from os import *
```

Compliant:

```python
from os import path
```

## py/unused-import

**Unnecessary imports should be removed.** An import nothing uses only slows start-up.

SonarQube: S1128

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
print(os.name)
```

## py/unused-local

**Unused local variables should be removed.** A variable that is never read is dead code or a typo.

SonarQube: S1481

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    x = compute()
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    x = compute()
    return x
```

## py/unused-parameter

**Unused function parameters should be removed.** Callers pass a value that is ignored.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a):
    return a
```

## py/too-many-returns

**Functions should not contain too many return statements.** Many exits make the flow hard to follow.

SonarQube: S1142

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x == 1:
        return 1
    if x == 2:
        return 2
    if x == 3:
        return 3
    return 4
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x
```

## py/nesting-depth

**Control flow statements should not be nested too deeply.** Deep nesting hides the main path.

SonarQube: S134

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    if b:
        if c:
            if d:
                if e:
                    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a and b:
    f()
```

## py/function-complexity

**Cyclomatic Complexity of functions should not be too high.** Every branch is another path to test.

SonarQube: FunctionComplexity

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b, c, d, e, g, h, i, j, k):
    if a: pass
    if b: pass
    if c: pass
    if d: pass
    if e: pass
    if g: pass
    if h: pass
    if i: pass
    if j: pass
    if k: pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a):
    if a:
        return 1
    return 0
```

## py/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hardcoded.** A fixed address has to be changed in the code when the network moves.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```python
host = '192.168.1.10'
```

Compliant:

```python
host = os.environ['HOST']
```

## py/noqa-comment

**Uses of noqa comments should be tracked.** Each one silences a check.

SonarQube: S1309

Noncompliant:

```python
import os  # noqa
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
```

## py/trailing-comment

**Comments should not be located at the end of lines of code.** They push lines past the screen edge and are easy to miss.

SonarQube: S139 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 1  # the count
```

Compliant:

```python
# the count
x = 1
```

## py/semicolon-statements

**Statements should be on separate lines.** Several statements on one line are easy to misread.

SonarQube: OneStatementPerLine

Noncompliant:

```python
a = 1; b = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
a = 1
b = 2
```

## py/increment-operator

**Increment and decrement operators should not be used.** `++x` is two unary plus signs and does nothing.

SonarQube: PreIncrementDecrement

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 1
++x
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 1
x += 1
```

## py/lambda-assigned

**Assignments of lambdas to variables should be replaced by function definitions.** A def has a name in tracebacks and can carry a docstring.

SonarQube: S6661

Noncompliant:

```python
f = lambda x: x + 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x + 1
```

## py/hint-any

**Any should not be used as a type hint.** It switches off type checking for that value.

SonarQube: S6542

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: Any):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: int):
    pass
```

## py/hint-bare-generic

**Type hints of generic types should specify their type parameters.** A bare list or dict says nothing about its contents.

SonarQube: S6543

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: list):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: list[int]):
    pass
```

## py/hint-typing-alias

**Built-in generic types should be preferred over the typing module in type hints.** List[int] is list[int] since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S6545

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: List[int]):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: list[int]):
    pass
```

## py/nested-fstring

**f-strings should not be nested too deeply.** Three levels of quotes and braces are unreadable.

SonarQube: S6799

Noncompliant:

```python
s = f"{f'{f"{x}"}'}"
```

Compliant:

```python
inner = f'{x}'
s = f'{inner}'
```

## py/dataclass-annotation

**Dataclass attributes should use type annotations.** Without an annotation the attribute is a class variable, not a field.

SonarQube: S8514

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: int = 1
```

## py/dataclass-mutable-default

**Mutable values should not be used as default values in dataclass attributes.** dataclass rejects list, dict and set defaults at class creation.

SonarQube: S8685

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: list = []
```

Compliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: list = field(default_factory=list)
```

## py/pydantic-optional-default

**Optional Pydantic fields should have explicit default values.** In Pydantic v2 an Optional field without a default is still required.

SonarQube: S8396

Noncompliant:

```python
class M(BaseModel):
    x: Optional[int]
```

Compliant:

```python
class M(BaseModel):
    x: Optional[int] = None
```

## py/django-model-str

**Django models should define a __str__ method.** Without it the admin and shell show 'Model object (1)'.

SonarQube: S6554

Noncompliant:

```python
class P(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField()
```

Compliant:

```python
class P(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField()
    def __str__(self):
        return self.name
```

## py/decorator-order

**The @receiver (Django) and @route (Flask) decorators should be the outermost decorators.** Decorators applied above them never reach the registered function.

SonarQube: S6552

Noncompliant:

```python
@login_required
@app.route('/')
def home():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/')
@login_required
def home():
    pass
```

## py/fastapi-path-parameter

**FastAPI path parameters should be included in route function signatures.** FastAPI fails to pass the value, and the endpoint returns 422.

SonarQube: S8411

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/items/{item_id}')
def read():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/items/{item_id}')
def read(item_id):
    pass
```

## py/fastapi-annotated

**FastAPI dependencies should use Annotated type hints.** Annotated keeps the default free for a real default and works with type checkers.

SonarQube: S8410

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(db=Depends(get_db)):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(db: Annotated[Db, Depends(get_db)]):
    pass
```

## py/bs4-parser

**BeautifulSoup parser should be explicitly specified.** Without one the result depends on which parsers are installed.

SonarQube: S8905

Noncompliant:

```python
soup = BeautifulSoup(html)
```

Compliant:

```python
soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'html.parser')
```

## py/bs4-deprecated

**Deprecated Beautiful Soup methods should be replaced with their modern equivalents.** The camelCase names were renamed in Beautiful Soup 4.

SonarQube: S8900

Noncompliant:

```python
links = soup.findAll('a')
```

Compliant:

```python
links = soup.find_all('a')
```

## py/logging-formatted-message

**Logging statements should follow Python best practices.** Formatting the message up front costs time even when the level is off.

SonarQube: S8554

Noncompliant:

```python
logger.info(f'user {name}')
```

Compliant:

```python
logger.info('user %s', name)
```

## py/test-fail-in-try

**Dedicated exception assertions should be used instead of try-except with fail().** assertRaises states the intent and reports it better.

SonarQube: S8714

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
    self.fail('no error')
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
    f()
```

## py/raises-multiple-statements

**Only one method invocation is expected when testing runtime exceptions.** Any other statement in the block could be the one that raises.

SonarQube: S5778

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    a = make()
    a.run()
```

Compliant:

```python
a = make()
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    a.run()
```

## py/assert-in-raises

**Assertions should not be made at the end of blocks expecting an exception.** Code after the raising call never runs.

SonarQube: S5915

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
    assert flag
```

Compliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
assert flag
```

## py/raises-too-generic

**Tests should check which exception is thrown.** Exception matches every failure, including test bugs.

SonarQube: S5958

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    run()
```

Compliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
```

## py/async-timeout-parameter

**Asynchronous functions should not accept timeout parameters.** Callers can cancel with the event loop's own timeouts.

SonarQube: S7483

Noncompliant:

```python
async def fetch(url, timeout=5):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
async def fetch(url):
    pass
```

## py/async-without-await

**Async functions should use async features.** Without await, async for or async with there is nothing asynchronous.

SonarQube: S7503

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    return await g()
```

## py/checkpoint-sleep-zero

**Checkpoints should be used instead of sleep(0).** trio and anyio have a dedicated checkpoint call.

SonarQube: S7491

Noncompliant:

```python
await trio.sleep(0)
```

Compliant:

```python
await trio.lowlevel.checkpoint()
```

## py/items-unused-half

**Using .items() to iterate over a dictionary should be avoided if possible.** If the key or the value is never used, iterate the keys or values.

SonarQube: S7512

Noncompliant:

```python
for k, v in d.items():
    print(k)
```

Compliant:

```python
for k in d:
    print(k)
```

## py/dict-lookup-in-loop

**Iteration over a dictionary key value pairs should be done with the items() method call.** Looking each key up again costs a hash per iteration.

SonarQube: S7517

Noncompliant:

```python
for k in d:
    print(d[k])
```

Compliant:

```python
for k, v in d.items():
    print(v)
```

## py/enumerate-index-lookup

**enumerate() values should be unpacked instead of using index lookups.** The loop already has the element.

SonarQube: S8518

Noncompliant:

```python
for i, x in enumerate(items):
    print(items[i])
```

Compliant:

```python
for i, x in enumerate(items):
    print(x)
```

## py/set-discard

**set.discard() should be used instead of checking membership before removal.** discard does not raise when the item is absent.

SonarQube: S8492

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in s:
    s.remove(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
s.discard(x)
```

## py/set-update-loop

**set.update() should be used instead of a for-loop with add().** One call replaces the loop.

SonarQube: S8502

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    s.add(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
s.update(xs)
```

## py/datetime-range

**Constructor attributes of date and time objects should be in the range of possible values.** Month 13 or day 32 raises ValueError.

SonarQube: S6882

Noncompliant:

```python
d = datetime(2020, 13, 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = datetime(2020, 12, 1)
```

## py/pandas-merge-args

**When using pandas.merge or pandas.join, the parameters on, how and validate should be provided.** The defaults hide intent and let duplicate keys through.

SonarQube: S6735

Noncompliant:

```python
out = pd.merge(a, b)
```

Compliant:

```python
out = pd.merge(a, b, on='k', how='inner', validate='one_to_one')
```

## py/read-csv-dtype

**dtype parameter should be provided when using pandas.read_csv or pandas.read_table.** Guessing types per chunk is slow and inconsistent.

SonarQube: S6740

Noncompliant:

```python
df = pd.read_csv('a.csv')
```

Compliant:

```python
df = pd.read_csv('a.csv', dtype={'id': 'int64'})
```

## py/dataframe-values

**The DataFrame.to_numpy() method should be preferred to the DataFrame.values attribute.** values has no defined result type for mixed columns.

SonarQube: S6741

Noncompliant:

```python
a = df.values
```

Compliant:

```python
a = df.to_numpy()
```

## py/ecb-mode

**Encryption algorithms should be used with secure mode and padding scheme.** ECB encrypts equal blocks to equal ciphertext and leaks structure.

SonarQube: S5542

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_ECB)
```

Compliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_GCM, nonce=n)
```

## py/static-iv

**Cipher Block Chaining IVs should be unpredictable.** A fixed IV makes equal plaintexts encrypt to equal ciphertexts.

SonarQube: S3329

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, b'0123456789abcdef')
```

Compliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, os.urandom(16))
```

## py/static-salt

**Password hashing functions should use an unpredictable salt.** A fixed salt lets one precomputed table crack every password.

SonarQube: S2053

Standards: [CWE-759](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/759.html), [CWE-760](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/760.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pw, b'salt', 100000)
```

Compliant:

```python
h = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pw, os.urandom(16), 100000)
```

## py/ldap-anonymous-bind

**LDAP connections should be authenticated.** An anonymous bind lets anyone read the directory.

SonarQube: S4433

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
conn.simple_bind_s()
```

Compliant:

```python
conn.simple_bind_s(dn, secret)
```

## py/password-fast-hash

**Passwords should not be stored in plaintext or with a fast hashing algorithm.** MD5 and SHA-2 are cheap to brute-force.

SonarQube: S5344

Standards: [CWE-256](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/256.html), [CWE-916](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/916.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
```

Compliant:

```python
h = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode(), bcrypt.gensalt())
```

## py/jump-outside-loop

**break and continue should not be used outside a loop.** Outside a loop they are a SyntaxError, and in a loop's else clause they belong to the enclosing loop.

SonarQube: S1716

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    break
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    for x in xs:
        break
```

## py/silly-equality

**Unnecessary equality checks should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal, so the comparison has a fixed result.

SonarQube: S2159

Noncompliant:

```python
if 1 == 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x == 'a':
    pass
```

## py/ignored-pure-call

**Return values from functions without side effects should not be ignored.** Calling len() or sorted() and dropping the result does nothing.

SonarQube: S2201

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    sorted(xs)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    return sorted(xs)
```

## py/method-could-be-static

**Methods and properties that don't access instance data should be static.** A method that never uses self does not need an instance.

Off by default: add `"py/method-could-be-static"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2325

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def double(self, x):
        return x * 2
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @staticmethod
    def double(x):
        return x * 2
```

## py/invariant-return

**Functions returns should not be invariant.** If every return gives the same constant, the return value carries no information.

SonarQube: S3516

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 0
    return 0
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 0
    return 1
```

## py/result-of-no-return-function

**The output of functions that don't return anything should not be used.** The function always yields None.

SonarQube: S3699

Noncompliant:

```python
def log(x):
    print(x)
r = log(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
def double(x):
    return x * 2
r = double(1)
```

## py/route-mixed-methods

**HTTP routes should restrict allowed HTTP methods.** One route that serves GET and a state-changing verb makes CSRF and caching mistakes easy.

SonarQube: S3752

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def x():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET'])
def x():
    pass
```

## py/undefined-name

**Variables, classes and functions should be either defined or imported.** Using a name nothing defines raises NameError.

SonarQube: S5953 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
print(missing_name)
```

Compliant:

```python
print(len('a'))
```

## py/private-class-unused

**Unused private nested classes should be removed.** Nothing in the class uses it, and name mangling hides it from outside.

SonarQube: S3985

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    class __Hidden:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    class __Hidden:
        pass
    def make(self):
        return self.__Hidden()
```

## py/nested-definition-unused

**Unused scope-limited definitions should be removed.** A function or class defined inside another and never used is dead code.

SonarQube: S5603

Noncompliant:

```python
def outer():
    def helper():
        pass
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def outer():
    def helper():
        pass
    return helper()
```

## py/walrus-as-statement

**Walrus operator should not make code confusing.** As a statement, := is just a harder-to-read assignment.

SonarQube: S5685

Noncompliant:

```python
(x := f())
```

Compliant:

```python
x = f()
```

## py/except-non-exception

**Caught Exceptions must derive from BaseException.** Catching a string or number raises TypeError once an exception is raised.

SonarQube: S5708

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except 'error':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

## py/except-parent-and-child

**A subclass should not be in the same except statement as a parent class.** The parent already catches the subclass.

SonarQube: S5713

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (Exception, ValueError):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (ValueError, KeyError):
    pass
```

## py/none-comparison-constant

**Comparison to None should not be constant.** The result never changes.

SonarQube: S5727

Noncompliant:

```python
if [] is None:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/all-undefined-name

**Only defined names should be listed in __all__.** An undefined entry makes `from m import *` fail.

SonarQube: S5807

Noncompliant:

```python
__all__ = ['Missing']
```

Compliant:

```python
class Missing:
    pass
__all__ = ['Missing']
```

## py/assert-incompatible-types

**Assertions comparing incompatible types should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal.

SonarQube: S5845

Noncompliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(1, 'a')
```

Compliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(1, x)
```

## py/confusing-type-check

**Type checks shouldn't be confusing.** isinstance needs a class, and a type compared with a string is never equal.

SonarQube: S5864

Noncompliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, 'str'):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, str):
    pass
```

## py/return-type-mismatch

**Function return types should be consistent with their type hint.** The literal returned is not what the signature promises.

SonarQube: S5886

Noncompliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 1
```

## py/annotation-mismatch

**Values assigned to variables should match their type annotations.** The literal does not fit the annotation.

SonarQube: S5890

Noncompliant:

```python
x: int = 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
x: int = 1
```

## py/test-name-case

**Test methods should be discoverable.** unittest only collects methods that start with a lowercase test.

SonarQube: S5899

Noncompliant:

```python
import unittest
class T(unittest.TestCase):
    def Test_a(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
import unittest
class T(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a(self):
        pass
```

## py/test-returns-early

**Tests should be skipped explicitly.** A bare return makes a test pass without checking anything.

SonarQube: S5918

Noncompliant:

```python
def test_a():
    return
    assert False
```

Compliant:

```python
import unittest
@unittest.skip('not ready')
def test_a():
    assert False
```

## py/field-named-after-class

**A field should not duplicate the name of its containing class.** It reads like a constructor call.

SonarQube: S1700

Noncompliant:

```python
class Point:
    point = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Point:
    origin = 1
```

## py/names-differ-by-case

**Methods and field names should not differ only by capitalization.** They are easy to confuse.

SonarQube: S1845

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
    def Run(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
    def stop(self):
        pass
```

## py/jsonresponse-unsafe

**The safe flag should be set to False when serializing non-dictionary objects.** JsonResponse raises TypeError for a list unless safe=False.

SonarQube: S6560

Noncompliant:

```python
return JsonResponse([1, 2])
```

Compliant:

```python
return JsonResponse([1, 2], safe=False)
```

## py/random-state-missing

**Results that depend on random number generation should be reproducible.** Without a seed, every run gives different results.

SonarQube: S6709

Noncompliant:

```python
X_train, X_test = train_test_split(X)
```

Compliant:

```python
X_train, X_test = train_test_split(X, random_state=0)
```

## py/pandas-long-chain

**pandas.pipe method should be preferred over long chains of instructions.** Long chains hide intermediate steps.

SonarQube: S6742

Noncompliant:

```python
out = df.a().b().c().d().e().f()
```

Compliant:

```python
out = df.pipe(step_one).pipe(step_two)
```

## py/generic-class-syntax

**Generic classes should be defined using the type parameter syntax.** Python 3.12 declares type parameters on the class.

SonarQube: S6792

Noncompliant:

```python
class A(Generic[T]):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A[T]:
    pass
```

## py/type-alias-annotation

**Type aliases should be declared with a type statement.** Python 3.12 has a dedicated statement.

SonarQube: S6794

Noncompliant:

```python
X: TypeAlias = int
```

Compliant:

```python
type X = int
```

## py/error-handler-without-status

**Flask error handlers should set HTTP status code.** Returning only a body sends status 200 for an error.

SonarQube: S6863

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.errorhandler(404)
def missing(e):
    return 'not found'
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.errorhandler(404)
def missing(e):
    return 'not found', 404
```

## py/time-format-mix

**The 12-hour format should be used with the AM/PM marker.** %I without %p is ambiguous, and %p with %H is ignored.

SonarQube: S6883

Noncompliant:

```python
s = now.strftime('%I:%M')
```

Compliant:

```python
s = now.strftime('%I:%M %p')
```

## py/weekmask-invalid

**Numpy weekmask should have a valid value.** It must be seven 0/1 flags, or day abbreviations.

SonarQube: S6900

Noncompliant:

```python
n = np.busday_count(a, b, weekmask='11111')
```

Compliant:

```python
n = np.busday_count(a, b, weekmask='1111100')
```

## py/tf-gather-validate-indices

**The validate_indices argument should not be set for tf.gather.** It is deprecated and ignored.

SonarQube: S6925

Noncompliant:

```python
y = tf.gather(x, i, validate_indices=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
y = tf.gather(x, i)
```

## py/tf-reduction-axis

**The reduction axis should be specified when using reduction operations.** Without an axis everything is reduced to a scalar, which is rarely meant.

SonarQube: S6929

Noncompliant:

```python
y = tf.reduce_sum(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
y = tf.reduce_sum(x, axis=0)
```

## py/tf-function-recursive

**tf.function should not be recursive.** A traced function cannot call itself.

SonarQube: S6908

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(n):
    return f(n - 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(n):
    return n
```

## py/tf-function-side-effects

**Python side effects should not be used inside a tf.function.** They run once during tracing, not on every call.

SonarQube: S6928

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    print(x)
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    tf.print(x)
    return x
```

## py/tf-variable-in-function

**tf.Variable objects should be singletons when created inside of a tf.function.** A new variable is created on every trace.

SonarQube: S6918

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    v = tf.Variable(1)
    return v
```

Compliant:

```python
v = tf.Variable(1)
@tf.function
def f(x):
    return v
```

## py/sklearn-pipeline-memory

**memory parameter should be specified for Scikit-Learn Pipeline.** Caching avoids refitting unchanged steps.

SonarQube: S6969

Noncompliant:

```python
p = Pipeline([('s', s)])
```

Compliant:

```python
p = Pipeline([('s', s)], memory='cache')
```

## py/ml-hyperparameters

**Important hyperparameters should be specified for machine learning estimators.** Library defaults rarely suit a given problem.

SonarQube: S6973

Noncompliant:

```python
m = RandomForestClassifier()
```

Compliant:

```python
m = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200)
```

## py/estimator-init-attribute

**Subclasses of BaseEstimator should not set attributes ending with _ in __init__.** Scikit-learn reserves them for values learned in fit.

SonarQube: S6974

Noncompliant:

```python
class E(BaseEstimator):
    def __init__(self):
        self.coef_ = None
```

Compliant:

```python
class E(BaseEstimator):
    def __init__(self):
        self.alpha = 1
```

## py/torch-state-without-mode

**model.eval() or model.train() should be called after loading the state of a PyTorch model.** Dropout and batch norm behave differently in each mode.

SonarQube: S6982

Noncompliant:

```python
model.load_state_dict(state)
use(model)
```

Compliant:

```python
model.load_state_dict(state)
model.eval()
```

## py/async-polling-loop

**Events should be used instead of sleep in asynchronous loops.** Polling wakes up for nothing and reacts late.

SonarQube: S7484

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    while not done:
        await asyncio.sleep(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await done_event.wait()
```

## py/sleep-forever

**Long sleep durations should use sleep_forever() instead of arbitrary intervals.** A huge sleep is a forever-wait in disguise.

SonarQube: S7486

Noncompliant:

```python
await trio.sleep(86400)
```

Compliant:

```python
await trio.sleep_forever()
```

## py/cancel-scope-without-checkpoint

**Cancellation scopes should contain checkpoints.** A scope can only cancel code at an await.

SonarQube: S7490

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    with trio.fail_after(1):
        work()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    with trio.fail_after(1):
        await work()
```

## py/cancellation-swallowed

**Cancellation exceptions should be re-raised after cleanup.** Swallowing it breaks timeouts and task groups.

SonarQube: S7497

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    await f()
except asyncio.CancelledError:
    cleanup()
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    await f()
except asyncio.CancelledError:
    cleanup()
    raise
```

## py/nursery-single-start

**TaskGroup/Nursery should not be used for a single start call.** A group of one is just a call.

SonarQube: S7513

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await g()
```

## py/nursery-control-flow

**Control flow statements should not be used inside TaskGroup or Nursery blocks.** A return or break inside cancels sibling tasks in surprising ways.

SonarQube: S7514

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
    return 1
```

## py/sync-with-async-manager

**async with should be used for asynchronous resource management.** A plain `with` never calls the async enter and exit.

SonarQube: S7515

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    with aiohttp.ClientSession() as s:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as s:
        pass
```

## py/not-implemented-boolean

**NotImplemented should not be used in boolean contexts.** It is truthy, and a DeprecationWarning since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S7931

Noncompliant:

```python
if NotImplemented:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def __eq__(self, o):
    return NotImplemented
```

## py/compression-namespace

**Compression modules should be imported from the compression namespace.** Python 3.14 moved them under compression.

Off by default: add `"py/compression-namespace"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7941

Noncompliant:

```python
import gzip
```

Compliant:

```python
from compression import gzip
```

## py/post-reads-query-args

**Query parameters should not be used in Flask POST requests.** POST data belongs in the body.

SonarQube: S8370

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['POST'])
def x():
    return request.args.get('a')
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['POST'])
def x():
    return request.form.get('a')
```

## py/preprocess-request-ignored

**Flask preprocess_request return values should be handled.** A returned response means the request is already answered.

SonarQube: S8375

Noncompliant:

```python
app.preprocess_request()
```

Compliant:

```python
rv = app.preprocess_request()
```

## py/send-file-metadata

**send_file should specify mimetype or download_name when used with file-like objects.** Without a name Flask cannot guess either.

SonarQube: S8385

Noncompliant:

```python
return send_file(io.BytesIO(data))
```

Compliant:

```python
return send_file(io.BytesIO(data), mimetype='text/plain')
```

## py/upload-with-body

**FastAPI file upload endpoints should use Form() with Pydantic validators instead of Body() or Depends().** File uploads are multipart, so Body() and Depends() can't carry the other fields.

SonarQube: S8389

Standards: [CWE-20](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/20.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
def up(f: UploadFile, meta: Meta = Body()):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def up(f: UploadFile, meta: str = Form()):
    pass
```

## py/no-content-with-body

**Endpoints returning 204 status should have an empty response body.** A 204 response must not carry a body.

SonarQube: S8400

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', status_code=204)
def x():
    return {'a': 1}
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', status_code=204)
def x():
    return None
```

## py/router-included-late

**Child routers should be included before parent router registration.** A router copies its routes when it is included.

SonarQube: S8401

Noncompliant:

```python
app.include_router(parent)
parent.include_router(child)
```

Compliant:

```python
parent.include_router(child)
app.include_router(parent)
```

## py/testclient-data

**TestClient requests should use the content parameter for bytes or text.** data= is for form fields.

SonarQube: S8405

Noncompliant:

```python
client.post('/x', data='raw')
```

Compliant:

```python
client.post('/x', content='raw')
```

## py/redundant-response-model

**FastAPI routes should not specify redundant response_model parameters.** The return annotation already says it.

SonarQube: S8409

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', response_model=Item)
def x() -> Item:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x() -> Item:
    pass
```

## py/include-router-prefix

**Router prefixes should be defined during APIRouter initialization.** Declaring it with the router keeps it in one place.

SonarQube: S8413

Noncompliant:

```python
app.include_router(router, prefix='/v1')
```

Compliant:

```python
router = APIRouter(prefix='/v1')
app.include_router(router)
```

## py/cors-not-last

**CORSMiddleware should be added last in the middleware chain.** Middleware added later runs first, so errors from outer layers skip CORS headers.

SonarQube: S8414

Noncompliant:

```python
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware)
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware)
```

Compliant:

```python
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware)
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware)
```

## py/http-exception-undocumented

**HTTPException responses should be documented in endpoint metadata.** Without responses= the OpenAPI document hides the error.

SonarQube: S8415

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x():
    raise HTTPException(404)
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', responses={404: {}})
def x():
    raise HTTPException(404)
```

## py/slots-assignment

**Attributes should only be assigned if they are declared in __slots__.** Other names raise AttributeError.

SonarQube: S8494

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    __slots__ = ('a',)
    def __init__(self):
        self.b = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    __slots__ = ('a',)
    def __init__(self):
        self.a = 1
```

## py/tuple-length-inconsistent

**Functions should return tuples of consistent length.** Callers unpack a fixed number of values.

SonarQube: S8495

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1, 2
    return 1, 2, 3
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1, 2
    return 3, 4
```

## py/comparison-methods-incomplete

**Comparison methods should be defined completely.** Defining only some of <, <=, > and >= gives surprising errors.

SonarQube: S8500

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __lt__(self, o):
        return True
```

Compliant:

```python
import functools
@functools.total_ordering
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        return True
    def __lt__(self, o):
        return True
```

## py/singledispatch-mixup

**@singledispatch and @singledispatchmethod should not be confused.** The wrong one dispatches on self.

SonarQube: S8505

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @singledispatch
    def f(self, x):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @singledispatchmethod
    def f(self, x):
        pass
```

## py/mro-conflict

**Multiple inheritance should not create Method Resolution Order conflicts.** A base listed before its own subclass makes class creation fail.

SonarQube: S8511

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    pass
class B(A):
    pass
class C(A, B):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    pass
class B(A):
    pass
class C(B, A):
    pass
```

## py/groupby-iterator-reused

**Group iterators from itertools.groupby should not be reused.** A group is consumed when the next key is requested, and only once.

SonarQube: S8516

Noncompliant:

```python
for k, g in groupby(xs):
    a = list(g)
    b = list(g)
```

Compliant:

```python
for k, g in groupby(xs):
    a = list(g)
```

## py/bs4-html-string

**HTML elements should be created with new_tag() instead of inserting raw HTML strings.** Inserting a string makes escaped text, not an element.

SonarQube: S8903

Noncompliant:

```python
soup.body.append('<b>hi</b>')
```

Compliant:

```python
tag = soup.new_tag('b')
soup.body.append(tag)
```

## py/bs4-find-unchecked

**BeautifulSoup elements should be checked for None before accessing.** find() returns None when nothing matches.

SonarQube: S8904

Noncompliant:

```python
title = soup.find('h1').text
```

Compliant:

```python
h = soup.find('h1')
title = h.text if h else ''
```

## py/bs4-class-selector

**CSS selectors should be used when selecting elements with multiple required classes.** class_='a b' matches only that exact attribute string.

SonarQube: S8906

Noncompliant:

```python
els = soup.find_all('div', class_='a b')
```

Compliant:

```python
els = soup.select('div.a.b')
```

## py/hint-missing-return

**Function returns should have type hints.** A signature without a return type tells callers nothing.

Off by default: add `"py/hint-missing-return"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6538

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 1
```

## py/hint-missing-parameter

**Function parameters should have type hints.** An untyped parameter tells callers nothing.

Off by default: add `"py/hint-missing-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6540

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: int):
    return x
```

## py/cdk-bucket-plain-http

**S3 buckets should enforce HTTPS-only access.** Without a policy that denies plain HTTP, requests can travel unencrypted.

SonarQube: S6249

Standards: [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', enforce_ssl=True)
```

## py/cdk-bucket-unversioned

**Amazon S3 buckets should have versioning enabled.** Without versioning an overwrite or delete cannot be undone.

SonarQube: S6252

Standards: [CWE-16](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/16.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', enforce_ssl=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

## py/cdk-bucket-public

**S3 buckets should not grant access to all users or authenticated users.** A public bucket exposes its objects to the whole internet.

SonarQube: S6265

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Site', public_read_access=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Site')
```

## py/cdk-bucket-public-access-not-blocked

**Amazon S3 bucket public access should be fully blocked.** Without a block, a later policy or ACL can still make objects public.

SonarQube: S6281

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', block_public_access=s3.BlockPublicAccess.BLOCK_ALL)
```

## py/cdk-policy-for-everyone

**AWS resource-based policies should not grant public access.** A policy for every principal lets anyone use the resource.

SonarQube: S6270

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(principals=[iam.AnyPrincipal()], actions=a)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(principals=[iam.AccountRootPrincipal()], actions=a)
```

## py/cdk-policy-grants-everything

**Policies should not grant all privileges.** An action of * allows every operation on the resource.

SonarQube: S6302

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['*'], resources=[arn])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=[arn])
```

## py/cdk-policy-all-resources

**IAM policies should not grant access to all account resources.** A resource of * covers every resource in the account.

SonarQube: S6304

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=['*'])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=[arn])
```

## py/cdk-policy-privilege-escalation

**AWS IAM policies should limit the scope of permissions given.** These actions let a principal grant itself more access.

SonarQube: S6317

Standards: [CWE-269](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/269.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['iam:PassRole'], resources=['*'])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=['*'])
```

## py/cdk-volume-unencrypted

**EBS volumes should be encrypted.** An unencrypted volume exposes data if a snapshot or disk leaks.

SonarQube: S6275

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
v = ec2.Volume(self, 'Disk', size=Size.gibibytes(10))
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
v = ec2.Volume(self, 'Disk', size=Size.gibibytes(10), encrypted=True)
```

## py/cdk-database-unencrypted

**Amazon RDS resources should be encrypted at rest.** An unencrypted database exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6303

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.DatabaseInstance(self, 'Db', engine=e)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.DatabaseInstance(self, 'Db', engine=e, storage_encrypted=True)
```

## py/cdk-search-unencrypted

**OpenSearch domains should have encryption at rest enabled.** An unencrypted domain exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6308

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = opensearch.Domain(self, 'Search', version=v)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = opensearch.Domain(self, 'Search', version=v, encryption_at_rest=opensearch.EncryptionAtRestOptions(enabled=True))
```

## py/cdk-notebook-unencrypted

**SageMaker notebook instances should be encrypted at rest.** The notebook volume holds datasets and models.

SonarQube: S6319

Standards: [CWE-312](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/312.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
n = sagemaker.CfnNotebookInstance(self, 'Nb', instance_type=t, role_arn=r)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
n = sagemaker.CfnNotebookInstance(self, 'Nb', instance_type=t, role_arn=r, kms_key_id=k)
```

## py/cdk-admin-port-open

**Administration services access should be restricted to specific IP addresses.** SSH or RDP open to the world invites brute force.

SonarQube: S6321

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg.add_ingress_rule(ec2.Peer.any_ipv4(), ec2.Port.tcp(22))
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg.add_ingress_rule(ec2.Peer.ipv4('10.0.0.0/8'), ec2.Port.tcp(22))
```

## py/cdk-topic-unencrypted

**Amazon SNS topics should be encrypted at rest.** Messages are stored unencrypted otherwise.

SonarQube: S6327

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
t = sns.Topic(self, 'Events')
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
t = sns.Topic(self, 'Events', master_key=key)
```

## py/cdk-publicly-accessible

**Public network access to cloud resources should be disabled.** A public database or cluster is reachable from the internet.

SonarQube: S6329

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-668](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/668.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), [CWE-1327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1327.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.CfnDBInstance(self, 'Db', publicly_accessible=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.CfnDBInstance(self, 'Db', publicly_accessible=False)
```

## py/cdk-queue-unencrypted

**SQS queues should be encrypted.** Messages are stored unencrypted otherwise.

SonarQube: S6330

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
q = sqs.Queue(self, 'Jobs', encryption=sqs.QueueEncryption.UNENCRYPTED)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
q = sqs.Queue(self, 'Jobs')
```

## py/cdk-filesystem-unencrypted

**Amazon EFS file systems should be encrypted.** An unencrypted file system exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6332

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
f = efs.FileSystem(self, 'Fs', vpc=vpc, encrypted=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk

```

## py/cdk-api-method-no-auth

**AWS API Gateway should require authentication.** A method without authorization is callable by anyone.

SonarQube: S6333

Standards: [CWE-306](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/306.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
api.root.add_method('GET', i, authorization_type=apigateway.AuthorizationType.NONE)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
api.root.add_method('GET', i, authorization_type=apigateway.AuthorizationType.IAM)
```

## py/cdk-unrestricted-outbound

**Security groups should not allow unrestricted outbound traffic.** A compromised host can reach any destination.

SonarQube: S6463

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg = ec2.SecurityGroup(self, 'Sg', vpc=vpc, allow_all_outbound=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg = ec2.SecurityGroup(self, 'Sg', vpc=vpc, allow_all_outbound=False)
```

## py/lambda-async-handler

**AWS Lambda handlers must not be an async function.** The runtime calls the handler without an event loop, so it never runs.

SonarQube: S7614

Noncompliant:

```python
async def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

## py/lambda-client-in-handler

**Reusable resources should be initialized at construction time of Lambda functions.** Creating a client on every invocation wastes the warm start.

SonarQube: S6243

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    s3 = boto3.client('s3')
    return {}
```

Compliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3')
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

## py/lambda-reserved-variable

**Reserved environment variable names should not be overridden in Lambda functions.** The runtime sets these, and overriding them breaks it.

SonarQube: S7617

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
os.environ['AWS_REGION'] = 'x'
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
os.environ['APP_REGION'] = 'x'
```

## py/lambda-request-timeout

**Network calls in AWS Lambda functions shouldn't be made without explicit timeout parameters.** A hung call burns the whole invocation budget.

SonarQube: S7618

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    r = requests.get(url)
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    r = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
```

## py/cloudwatch-reserved-namespace

**AWS CloudWatch metrics namespace should not begin with AWS/.** That prefix is reserved for AWS services.

SonarQube: S7609

Noncompliant:

```python
cw.put_metric_data(Namespace='AWS/Custom', MetricData=[])
```

Compliant:

```python
cw.put_metric_data(Namespace='App/Custom', MetricData=[])
```

## py/s3-bucket-owner

**S3 operations should verify bucket ownership using ExpectedBucketOwner parameter.** Without it a request can reach another account's bucket with the same name.

SonarQube: S7608

Standards: [CWE-441](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/441.html), [CWE-693](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/693.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
s3.get_object(Bucket='b', Key='k')
```

Compliant:

```python
s3.get_object(Bucket='b', Key='k', ExpectedBucketOwner=acct)
```

## py/lambda-sync-invoke

**Lambdas should not invoke other lambdas synchronously.** The caller pays for the callee's whole run time.

SonarQube: S6246

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    lambda_client.invoke(FunctionName='f', Payload=b'{}')
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    lambda_client.invoke(FunctionName='f', InvocationType='Event')
```

## py/boto-manual-pagination

**boto3 operations that support pagination should be performed using paginators.** A single call returns only the first page.

SonarQube: S7622

Noncompliant:

```python
r = s3.list_objects_v2(Bucket='b')
```

Compliant:

```python
r = s3.get_paginator('list_objects_v2').paginate(Bucket='b')
```

## py/regex-invalid

**Regular expressions should be syntactically valid.** An invalid pattern raises re.error when it is compiled.

SonarQube: S5856

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a)')
```

## py/regex-empty-group

**Regular expressions should not contain empty groups.** An empty group matches nothing and captures nothing useful.

SonarQube: S6331

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a()b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a(b)')
```

## py/regex-empty-alternative

**Alternation in regular expressions should not contain empty alternatives.** An empty alternative matches everywhere, which is rarely meant.

SonarQube: S6323

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b')
```

## py/regex-single-character-class

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain only one character.** [a] is just a.

SonarQube: S6397

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[a]b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab')
```

## py/regex-useless-group

**Non-capturing groups without quantifier should not be used.** The group groups nothing.

SonarQube: S6395

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:ab)c')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:ab)+c')
```

## py/regex-superfluous-braces

**Superfluous curly brace quantifiers should be avoided.** {1} repeats once.

SonarQube: S6396

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a{1}')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a{2}')
```

## py/regex-concise-syntax

**Regular expression quantifiers and character classes should be used concisely.** \d is shorter than [0-9], and + than {1,}.

SonarQube: S6353

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[0-9]{1,}')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d+')
```

## py/regex-octal-escape

**Octal escape sequences should not be used in regular expressions.** They are easy to mistake for backreferences.

SonarQube: S6537

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\012')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\x0a')
```

## py/regex-backreference-order

**Back references in regular expressions should only refer to capturing groups that are matched before.** A group cannot refer to itself, so the reference never matches.

SonarQube: S6001

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a\\1)')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a)\\1')
```

## py/regex-replacement-groups

**Replacement strings should reference existing regular expression groups.** A missing group raises re.error at runtime.

SonarQube: S6328

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
s = re.sub('(a)', '\\2', t)
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
s = re.sub('(a)', '\\1', t)
```

## py/regex-single-character-alternation

**Single-character alternations in regular expressions should be replaced with character classes.** a|b|c is [abc].

SonarQube: S6035

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b|c')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[abc]')
```

## py/regex-multiple-spaces

**Regular expressions should not contain multiple spaces.** Several spaces in a row are easy to miscount.

SonarQube: S6326

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a   b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a {3}b')
```

## py/regex-duplicate-class-member

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain the same character twice.** The repeated member does nothing.

SonarQube: S5869

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[aa]')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[ab]')
```

## py/regex-grapheme-in-class

**Unicode Grapheme Clusters should be avoided inside regex character classes.** A class matches one code point, so a cluster is split.

SonarQube: S5868

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[e\u0301]')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:e\u0301)')
```

## py/regex-repeat-matches-empty

**Repeated patterns in regular expressions should not match the empty string.** The loop can spin without consuming anything.

SonarQube: S5842

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a*)*')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+)*')
```

## py/regex-anchor-alternation

**Alternatives in regular expressions should be grouped when used with anchors.** In ^a|b|c$ the anchors bind only the first and last alternative.

SonarQube: S5850

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^a|b|c$')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^(?:a|b|c)$')
```

## py/regex-redundant-alternative

**Regex alternatives should not be redundant.** An alternative another one already covers never decides the match.

SonarQube: S5855

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b')
```

## py/regex-possessive-always-fails

**Regex patterns following a possessive quantifier should not always fail.** A possessive quantifier takes everything it can match, leaving nothing for the next item.

SonarQube: S5994

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a*+a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a*+b')
```

## py/regex-unmatchable-boundary

**Regex boundaries should not be used in a way that can never be matched.** Text after $ or before ^ cannot match.

SonarQube: S5996

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a$b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^ab$')
```

## py/regex-contradictory-lookaround

**Regex lookahead assertions should not be contradictory.** The next character is required and forbidden at once.

SonarQube: S6002

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?=a)b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?=a)ab')
```

## py/regex-reluctant-at-end

**Reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions should be followed by an expression that can't match the empty string.** At the end of a pattern a reluctant quantifier matches as little as possible.

SonarQube: S6019

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab.*?')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab.*?c')
```

## py/regex-reluctant-instead-of-class

**Character classes should be preferred over reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions.** `".*?"` backtracks where `"[^"]*"` does not.

SonarQube: S5857

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('"(.*?)"')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('"([^"]*)"')
```

## py/regex-too-complex

**Regular expressions should not be too complicated.** A pattern nobody can read is a pattern nobody can fix.

SonarQube: S5843

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('((a|b)+(c|d)+)+((e|f)+(g|h)+)+((i|j)+(k|l)+)+')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a|b)+')
```

## py/regex-catastrophic-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause catastrophic backtracking.** Nested repetitions that can match the same text take exponential time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S5852

Standards: [CWE-1333](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1333.html)

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+)+b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+b)+')
```

## py/regex-polynomial-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause non-linear backtracking.** Adjacent repetitions over the same characters take polynomial time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S8786

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d*\\d*x')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d+x')
```

## py/missing-docstring

**Docstrings should be defined.** Public modules, classes and functions are documented in their docstring.

Off by default: add `"py/missing-docstring"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1720

Noncompliant:

```python
def run():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
"""Tools."""
def run():
    """Run it."""
    return 1
```

## py/missing-license-header

**Track lack of copyright and license headers.** A header states who owns the file and under which terms it is shared.

Off by default: add `"py/missing-license-header"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1451

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
```

Compliant:

```python
# Copyright 2026 Example Ltd.
import os
```

## py/aws-polling-loop

**AWS waiters should be used instead of custom polling loops.** A waiter polls with the right delays and gives up cleanly.

SonarQube: S7621

Noncompliant:

```python
import boto3
while True:
    s = ec2.describe_instances()
    time.sleep(5)
```

Compliant:

```python
import boto3
ec2.get_waiter('instance_running').wait()
```

## py/lambda-tmp-not-cleaned

**AWS Lambda handlers should clean up temporary files in /tmp directory.** The directory survives between invocations and fills up.

SonarQube: S7620

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    open('/tmp/out', 'w').write('x')
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    open('/tmp/out', 'w').write('x')
    os.remove('/tmp/out')
```

## py/lambda-non-json-return

**AWS Lambda handlers should return only JSON serializable values.** The runtime serializes the return value with json.

SonarQube: S7613

Noncompliant:

```python
import datetime
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {'at': datetime.datetime.now()}
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {'at': 'now'}
```

## py/flask-single-method-route

**Generic route decorators should not be used.** @app.get says the method without a keyword argument.

SonarQube: S8412

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET'])
def x():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x():
    pass
```

## py/flask-view-decorator

**Flask class-based view decorators should be applied using the decorators attribute.** Wrapping as_view() hides the view's name and breaks routing.

SonarQube: S8374

Noncompliant:

```python
app.add_url_rule('/x', view_func=login_required(V.as_view('x')))
```

Compliant:

```python
class V(View):
    decorators = [login_required]
app.add_url_rule('/x', view_func=V.as_view('x'))
```

## py/pandas-datetime-format

**Dates should be formatted correctly when using pandas.to_datetime with dayfirst or yearfirst.** An explicit format makes dayfirst and yearfirst ignored.

SonarQube: S6894

Noncompliant:

```python
d = pd.to_datetime(s, format='%d/%m/%Y', dayfirst=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = pd.to_datetime(s, format='%d/%m/%Y')
```

## py/einops-pattern

**Einops pattern should be valid.** A pattern needs an arrow and balanced parentheses.

SonarQube: S6984

Noncompliant:

```python
import einops
y = einops.rearrange(x, 'b c h w')
```

Compliant:

```python
import einops
y = einops.rearrange(x, 'b c h w -> b (c h) w')
```

## py/special-method-return

**Functions and methods should only return expected values.** Python rejects a __len__ that returns text or a __bool__ that returns a number.

SonarQube: S935

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 1
```

## py/call-argument-mismatch

**The number and name of arguments passed to a function should match its parameters.** A wrong count or name raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S930

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
f(1, 2, 3)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
f(1, 2)
```

## py/builtin-argument-type

**Arguments given to functions should be of an expected type.** A number is not iterable and has no length.

SonarQube: S5655

Noncompliant:

```python
n = len(5)
```

Compliant:

```python
n = len([5])
```

## py/used-before-defined

**Variables, classes and functions should be defined before being used.** At module level a name must exist when the statement runs.

SonarQube: S3827

Noncompliant:

```python
print(later)
later = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
later = 1
print(later)
```

## py/typevar-generic-function

**Generic functions should be defined using the type parameter syntax.** Python 3.12 declares type parameters on the function.

SonarQube: S6796

Noncompliant:

```python
from typing import TypeVar
T = TypeVar('T')
def first(x: list[T]) -> T:
    return x[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
def first[T](x: list[T]) -> T:
    return x[0]
```

## py/keras-input-shape

**The input_shape parameter should not be specified for tf.keras.Model subclasses.** A subclassed model builds on the first call; input_shape is ignored.

SonarQube: S6919

Noncompliant:

```python
class M(tf.keras.Model):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.d = Dense(1, input_shape=(3,))
```

Compliant:

```python
class M(tf.keras.Model):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.d = Dense(1)
```

## py/xml-signature-no-x509

**XML signatures should be validated securely.** Without a certificate check anyone can sign the document.

SonarQube: S6377

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import signxml
XMLVerifier().verify(data, require_x509=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
import signxml
XMLVerifier().verify(data, x509_cert=cert)
```

## java/string-reference-equality

**Strings should be compared with equals().** `==` compares references, not contents, so equal strings may compare unequal.

SonarQube: S4973

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s == "x"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return "x".equals(s); } }
```

## java/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD2, MD5 and SHA-1 are broken for security purposes.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256"); } }
```

## java/empty-catch

**Exceptions should not be silently swallowed.** An empty catch block hides failures. Handle the error or explain with a comment.

SonarQube: S108 (related)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) { log(e); } } }
```

## java/print-stack-trace

**printStackTrace() should not be used.** Stack traces printed to stderr bypass logging and may leak internals.

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Exception e) { e.printStackTrace(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Exception e) { log.error("failed", e); } }
```

## java/system-exit

**System.exit() should not be called.** System.exit() shuts down the whole JVM, which is rarely what library or server code wants.

SonarQube: S1147

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.exit(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new IllegalStateException(); } }
```

## java/system-out

**System.out and System.err should not be used for logging.** Use a logger so output can be filtered, formatted and routed.

SonarQube: S106

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.out.println("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { log.info("x"); } }
```

## java/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be built from strings.** Concatenating values into a query lets an attacker change what it does; pass them as parameters.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stmt.executeQuery("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = " + id); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stmt.executeQuery("SELECT 1"); } }
```

## java/cookie-not-secure

**Cookies should have the secure flag.** Without `secure`, the cookie is also sent over plain HTTP.
