Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s.toLowerCase(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s = s.toLowerCase(); } }
```

## java/servlet-mutable-field

**Servlets should not have mutable instance fields.** One servlet instance serves every request, so its fields are shared state.

SonarQube: S2226

Noncompliant:

```java
class S extends HttpServlet { int hits; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class S extends HttpServlet { final int limit = 3; }
```

## java/proxy-annotation-visibility

**Methods with Spring proxying annotations should be public.** Spring's proxies cannot intercept non-public methods.

SonarQube: S2230

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional public void f() {} }
```

## java/wait-on-thread

**wait, notify and notifyAll should not be called on Thread instances.** Thread uses its own monitor internally, so these calls interfere with join().

SonarQube: S2236

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Thread thread) throws Exception { thread.wait(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object lock) throws Exception { lock.wait(); } }
```

## java/loop-never-runs

**Loop conditions should be true at least once.** A condition that is false on entry means the body never runs.

SonarQube: S2252

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 10; i < 5; i++) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {} } }
```

## java/wait-outside-synchronized

**wait, notify and notifyAll should only be called from synchronized code.** Without the monitor they throw IllegalMonitorStateException.

SonarQube: S2273

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { lock.wait(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { lock.wait(); } } }
```

## java/misleading-operator

**Non-existent operators like =+ should not be used.** `x =+ 1` assigns +1, which is almost never what was meant.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = 0; x =+ 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = 0; x += 1; } }
```

## java/to-array-cast

**Collection.toArray() should be passed an array of the proper type.** Casting the Object[] result to a typed array throws ClassCastException.

SonarQube: S3020

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] a = (String[]) list.toArray(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] a = list.toArray(new String[0]); } }
```

## java/identical-branches

**All branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** If every branch does the same thing, the condition decides nothing.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else { f(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else { g(); } } }
```

## java/default-package-application

**@SpringBootApplication and @ComponentScan should not be used in the default package.** Spring would scan every class on the classpath.

SonarQube: S4602

Noncompliant:

```java
@SpringBootApplication class App {}
```

Compliant:

```java
package com.acme;
@SpringBootApplication class App {}
```

## java/thread-local-never-removed

**ThreadLocal variables should be cleaned up when no longer used.** In a thread pool a value left behind leaks into the next task.

SonarQube: S5164

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static ThreadLocal<String> t = new ThreadLocal<>(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static ThreadLocal<String> t = new ThreadLocal<>(); void done() { t.remove(); } }
```

## java/nested-test-class-without-nested

**JUnit5 inner test classes should be annotated with @Nested.** JUnit 5 only runs non-static inner test classes that are marked @Nested.

SonarQube: S5790

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { class Inner { @Test void a() {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Nested class Inner { @Test void a() {} } }
```

## java/test-silently-ignored

**JUnit5 test methods should not be silently ignored.** JUnit 5 skips private and static test methods without a warning.

SonarQube: S5810

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test private void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/competing-test-annotations

**Test methods should not be annotated with competing annotations.** A method with both @Test and @ParameterizedTest runs twice, or not as intended.

SonarQube: S5967

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test @ParameterizedTest void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @ParameterizedTest void a() {} }
```

## java/compute-if-absent-null

**Map computeIfAbsent() and computeIfPresent() should not be used to add null values.** A mapping function that returns null adds nothing.

SonarQube: S6104

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> null); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> new ArrayList<>()); } }
```

## java/async-in-configuration

**@Async should not be used on methods declared within a @Configuration class.** Configuration classes are proxied in a way that prevents @Async from working.

SonarQube: S6817

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Async void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Component class C { @Async void f() {} }
```

## java/path-variable-not-bound

**Path template variables should be properly bound to method parameters.** A {variable} with no matching @PathVariable parameter is never read.

SonarQube: S6856

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/users/{id}") String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/users/{id}") String f(@PathVariable String id) { return id; } }
```

## java/duplicate-bean-name

**Beans in a @Configuration class should have different names.** Two @Bean methods with one name register one bean and silently override the other.

SonarQube: S6862

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Bean A a() { return null; } @Bean B a(int x) { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Bean A a() { return null; } @Bean B b() { return null; } }
```

## java/pbe-literal-salt

**Password hashing functions should use an unpredictable salt.** A fixed salt lets attackers precompute hashes for every user.

SonarQube: S2053

Standards: [CWE-759](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/759.html), [CWE-760](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/760.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new PBEKeySpec(pw, "salt".getBytes(), 10000, 256); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new PBEKeySpec(pw, randomSalt, 10000, 256); } }
```

## java/custom-crypto

**Custom cryptographic algorithms should not be used.** Home-made ciphers and digests are almost always weaker than the standard ones.

SonarQube: S2257

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class MyDigest extends MessageDigest { MyDigest() { super("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class MyDigest { }
```

## java/ldap-object-deserialization

**Deserialization of Java objects from LDAP should be disabled.** Returning objects from a search lets a malicious directory run code in the client.

SonarQube: S4434

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { controls.setReturningObjFlag(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { controls.setReturningObjFlag(false); } }
```

## java/activemq-untrusted-classes

**ActiveMQConnectionFactory should not be vulnerable to malicious code deserialization.** By default it will instantiate any class named in a message.

SonarQube: S5301

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new ActiveMQConnectionFactory(url); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ActiveMQConnectionFactory f = new ActiveMQConnectionFactory(url); f.setTrustedPackages(List.of("com.acme")); } }
```

## java/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hard-coded.** A literal address breaks when the network changes and can't be overridden per environment.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String host = "203.0.113.7"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String host = System.getenv("HOST");
String local = "127.0.0.1"; } }
```

## java/missing-override

**@Override should be used on overriding and implementing methods.** Without it, a typo in the signature silently creates a new method.

SonarQube: S1161

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Override public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

## java/unused-method-parameter

**Unused method parameters should be removed.** A parameter nothing reads misleads callers.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private void f(int unused) { g(); } void h() { f(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private void f(int used) { g(used); } void h() { f(1); } }
```

## java/ignored-test-without-reason

**JUnit4 @Ignore and JUnit5 @Disabled annotations should be used to disable tests with a reason.** Without a reason, nobody knows when the test can come back.

SonarQube: S1607

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Disabled void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Disabled("flaky on CI") void a() {} }
```

## java/for-update-unrelated

**for loop increment clauses should modify the loops' counters.** An update clause that touches other variables than the ones the condition tests usually never ends the loop.

SonarQube: S1994

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; j++) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {} } }
```

## java/transient-in-plain-class

**Fields in non-serializable classes should not be transient.** transient means nothing in a class that is never serialized.

SonarQube: S2065

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { transient int x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { transient int x; }
```

## java/close-in-finally

**Try-with-resources should be used.** A resource closed in finally leaks if close() itself throws, and the code is longer.

SonarQube: S2093

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { use(in); } finally { in.close(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try (InputStream in = open()) { use(in); } } }
```

## java/log-and-rethrow

**Exceptions should be either logged or rethrown but not both.** The same failure then appears twice in the logs.

SonarQube: S2139

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { log.error("x", e); throw e; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { throw new UncheckedIOException(e); } } }
```

## java/synchronized-read-object

**readObject should not be synchronized.** Deserialization already has exclusive access to the object.

SonarQube: S2675

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private synchronized void readObject(ObjectInputStream in) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private void readObject(ObjectInputStream in) {} }
```

## java/multiline-without-braces

**Multiline blocks should be enclosed in curly braces.** A second statement indented like the first looks guarded by the condition, but isn't.

SonarQube: S2681

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
  void f() {
    if (ok)
      a();
      b();
  }
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
  void f() {
    if (ok) {
      a();
      b();
    }
  }
}
```

## java/sleep-in-test

**Thread.sleep should not be used in tests.** Sleeping makes tests slow and flaky.

SonarQube: S2925, S5973

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() throws Exception { Thread.sleep(100); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { void a() throws Exception { Thread.sleep(100); } }
```

## java/incomplete-assertion

**Assertions should be complete.** assertThat(x) on its own asserts nothing.

SonarQube: S2970

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(actual); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(actual).isEqualTo(1); } }
```

## java/array-copy-loop

**Arrays and lists should not be copied using loops.** System.arraycopy and Arrays.copyOf are faster and clearer.

SonarQube: S3012

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) { b[i] = a[i]; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.arraycopy(a, 0, b, 0, a.length); } }
```

## java/assertion-argument-order

**Assertion arguments should be passed in the correct order.** The expected value goes first, or the failure message is backwards.

SonarQube: S3415

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(result, 4); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(4, result); } }
```

## java/test-class-name

**Test classes should comply with a naming convention.** Build tools only run classes whose names match their pattern.

SonarQube: S3577

Noncompliant:

```java
class Checks { @Test void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class ChecksTest { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/assert-on-parameters

**Asserts should not be used to check the parameters of a public method.** Assertions are disabled by default, so the check silently disappears.

SonarQube: S4274

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void f(int x) { assert x > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public void f(int x) { if (x <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException(); } }
```

## java/composed-request-mapping

**Composed @RequestMapping variants should be preferred.** @GetMapping says it in one word.

SonarQube: S4488

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @RequestMapping(value = "/x", method = RequestMethod.GET) String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/x") String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/jdbc-driver-for-name

**Class.forName() should not load JDBC 4.0+ drivers.** Drivers register themselves, so the call is dead weight.

SonarQube: S4925

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Class.forName("com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Class.forName("com.acme.Plugin"); } }
```

## java/expected-in-test-annotation

**Exception testing via the @Test annotation should be avoided.** @Test(expected = X.class) passes if any line of the test throws X.

SonarQube: S5777

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test(expected = IOException.class) void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/expected-exception-rule

**Exception testing via the JUnit ExpectedException rule should not be mixed.** The rule is deprecated in favour of assertThrows.

SonarQube: S5776

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Rule ExpectedException thrown = ExpectedException.none(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { }
```

## java/jupiter-public-test

**JUnit5 test classes and methods should have default package visibility.** public adds nothing in JUnit 5.

SonarQube: S5786

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test public void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/too-many-assertions

**Test methods should not contain too many assertions.** A test with dozens of assertions fails at the first and hides the rest.

SonarQube: S5961

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(0, x); assertEquals(1, x); assertEquals(2, x); assertEquals(3, x); assertEquals(4, x); assertEquals(5, x); assertEquals(6, x); assertEquals(7, x); assertEquals(8, x); assertEquals(9, x); assertEquals(10, x); assertEquals(11, x); assertEquals(12, x); assertEquals(13, x); assertEquals(14, x); assertEquals(15, x); assertEquals(16, x); assertEquals(17, x); assertEquals(18, x); assertEquals(19, x); assertEquals(20, x); assertEquals(21, x); assertEquals(22, x); assertEquals(23, x); assertEquals(24, x); assertEquals(25, x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(1, x); } }
```

## java/region-not-set

**Region should be set explicitly when creating a new AWS client.** The default region comes from the environment and differs between machines.

SonarQube: S6241

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().build(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).build(); } }
```

## java/credentials-provider-not-set

**Credentials Provider should be set explicitly when creating a new AWS client.** The default provider chain tries many sources and picks different ones in different places.

SonarQube: S6242

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).build(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).credentialsProvider(p).build(); } }
```

## java/aws-region-hardcoded

**AWS region should not be set with a hardcoded String.** A literal region makes the code awkward to deploy elsewhere.

SonarQube: S6262

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Region r = Region.of("eu-west-1"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Region r = Region.of(config.region()); } }
```

## java/value-annotation-literal

**@Value annotation should inject property or SpEL expression.** @Value("text") injects the literal text, which is rarely intended.

SonarQube: S6804

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Value("plain") String s; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Value("${app.name}") String s; }
```

## java/optional-primitive-request-param

**Optional REST parameters should have an object type.** A primitive cannot hold the null that a missing parameter produces.

SonarQube: S6814

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { String f(@RequestParam(required = false) int page) { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { String f(@RequestParam(required = false) Integer page) { return ""; } }
```

## java/controller-should-be-rest

**@Controller should be replaced with @RestController.** When every method has @ResponseBody, @RestController says it once.

SonarQube: S6833

Noncompliant:

```java
@Controller class C { @ResponseBody String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@RestController class C { String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/cacheable-and-cacheput

**@Cacheable and @CachePut should not be combined.** One reads from the cache, the other always writes; together they contradict.

SonarQube: S7179

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @Cacheable("a") @CachePut("a") String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @Cacheable("a") String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/pageable-parameter

**Methods returning Page or Slice must take Pageable as an input parameter.** Spring Data needs the Pageable to know which page to fetch.

SonarQube: S7186

Noncompliant:

```java
interface R { Page<A> find(String name); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface R { Page<A> find(String name, Pageable p); }
```

## java/transaction-callback-signature

**@BeforeTransaction and @AfterTransaction methods must be void and take no arguments.** Spring invokes them without arguments and ignores their result.

SonarQube: S7190

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeTransaction int f() { return 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeTransaction void f() {} }
```

## java/junit4-assert-in-jupiter

**JUnit Jupiter tests should not use JUnit 4 assertions.** Jupiter and JUnit 4 put the message in different positions.

SonarQube: S8715

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.Assert;
class T { }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;
class T { }
```

## java/duplicate-lifecycle-method

**Test classes should not contain multiple lifecycle methods of the same type.** JUnit does not define the order of two @BeforeEach methods.

SonarQube: S8745

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeEach void a() {} @BeforeEach void b() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeEach void a() {} @AfterEach void b() {} }
```

## java/commented-out-code

**Sections of code should not be commented out.** Commented-out code rots, and version control already remembers it.

SonarQube: S125

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
// int x = compute(1);
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
// Compute the total price.
}
```

## java/package-name

**Package names should comply with a naming convention.** Packages are lower case.

SonarQube: S120

Noncompliant:

```java
package com.Acme;
class A { }
```

Compliant:

```java
package com.acme;
class A { }
```

## java/switch-case-falls-through

**Switch cases should end with an unconditional break statement.** A case that runs into the next one is usually a forgotten break.

SonarQube: S128

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a(); case 2: b(); break; default: c(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a(); break; case 2: b(); break; default: c(); } } }
```

## java/local-shadows-field

**Local variables should not shadow class fields.** The field is then unreachable by its plain name.

SonarQube: S1117

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int count; void f() { int count = 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int count; void f() { int total = 1; } }
```

## java/empty-statement

**Empty statements should be removed.** A stray semicolon is either noise or a bug.

SonarQube: S1116

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g();; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); } }
```

## java/assertion-in-run

**JUnit assertions should not be used in run methods.** An assertion that fails in another thread is lost; the test still passes.

SonarQube: S2186

Noncompliant:

```java
class W implements Runnable { public void run() { assertEquals(1, x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class W implements Runnable { public void run() { result = x; } }
```

## java/testcase-without-tests

**TestCases should contain tests.** A JUnit 3 TestCase with no test methods runs nothing.

SonarQube: S2187

Noncompliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { void helper() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { public void testIt() {} }
```

## java/testcase-lifecycle-super

**JUnit test cases should call super methods.** Skipping super.setUp() or super.tearDown() skips the parent fixture.

SonarQube: S2188

Noncompliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { protected void setUp() { init(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { protected void setUp() { super.setUp(); init(); } }
```

## java/wait-outside-loop

**Object.wait(...) should be called inside a loop.** A woken thread must re-check its condition, because wake-ups can be spurious.

SonarQube: S2274

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { lock.wait(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { while (!ready) { lock.wait(); } } } }
```

## java/volatile-compound-operation

**volatile variables should not be used with compound operators.** count++ on a volatile field is a read and a write, so updates are lost.

SonarQube: S3078

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { volatile int count; void f() { count++; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { AtomicInteger count; void f() { count.incrementAndGet(); } }
```

## java/week-year-mismatch

**DateTimeFormatters should not use mismatched year and week numbers.** A calendar year combined with a week number gives the wrong date at the year boundary.

SonarQube: S5917

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DateTimeFormatter f = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-ww"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DateTimeFormatter f = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM"); } }
```

## java/model-attribute-name

**Model attributes should follow the Java identifier naming convention.** A name that is not an identifier cannot be referenced from the view.

SonarQube: S6806

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @ModelAttribute("my-name") Object a() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @ModelAttribute("myName") Object a() { return null; } }
```

## java/hardcoded-path

**URIs should not be hardcoded.** A literal path or file URI breaks on another machine.

SonarQube: S1075

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String p = "/home/app/data"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String p = config.dataDir(); } }
```

## java/redundant-cast

**Redundant casts should not be used.** Casting a value to the type it already has does nothing.

SonarQube: S1905

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = (String) "x"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = "x"; } }
```

## java/serializable-field-type

**Fields in a Serializable class should either be transient or serializable.** A List or Map field is only serializable if the instance behind it is.

SonarQube: S1948

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private List<String> items; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private transient List<String> items; }
```

## java/read-resolve-visibility

**readResolve methods should be inheritable.** A private readResolve is ignored when a subclass is deserialized.

SonarQube: S2062

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private Object readResolve() { return this; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { protected Object readResolve() { return this; } }
```

## java/pointless-math

**Do not perform unnecessary mathematical operations.** Rounding an integer, or taking the floor of one, changes nothing.

SonarQube: S2185

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Math.floor(5); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Math.floor(x); } }
```

## java/test-without-assertion

**Tests should include assertions.** A test that asserts nothing passes whatever the code does.

SonarQube: S2699

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { service.run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(1, service.run()); } }
```

## java/commons-date-truncate

**DateUtils.truncate from Apache Commons Lang should not be used.** It misbehaves around DST changes.

SonarQube: S2718

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Date d = DateUtils.truncate(now, Calendar.DATE); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.now(); } }
```

## java/nullable-primitive

**@CheckForNull or @Nullable should not be used on primitive types.** A primitive can never be null.

SonarQube: S4682

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Nullable int f() { return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Nullable Integer f() { return null; } }
```

## java/assertj-simplifiable

**AssertJ assertions should be simplified to the corresponding dedicated assertion.** hasSize(n) and isEmpty() report the actual contents when they fail; isEqualTo(0) does not.

SonarQube: S5838

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(list.size()).isEqualTo(3); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(list).hasSize(3); } }
```

## java/assertj-chain

**Consecutive AssertJ assertThat statements should be chained.** Two statements on the same subject can be one chain.

SonarQube: S5853

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(x).isNotNull(); assertThat(x).isEqualTo(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(x).isNotNull().isEqualTo(1); } }
```

## java/assertthatthrownby-alone

**AssertJ assertThatThrownBy should not be used alone.** Without a follow-up it only checks that something was thrown.

SonarQube: S5958

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThatThrownBy(() -> f()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThatThrownBy(() -> f()).isInstanceOf(IOException.class); } }
```

## java/mockito-times-one

**Call to Mockito verify should be simplified.** times(1) is the default.

SonarQube: S6068

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { verify(mock, times(1)).run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { verify(mock).run(); } }
```

## java/mockito-qualified

**Mockito core methods should be statically imported.** Mockito.when(...) reads worse than when(...).

SonarQube: S8924

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Mockito.when(x.get()).thenReturn(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { when(x.get()).thenReturn(1); } }
```

## java/proxied-call-through-this

**Methods with Spring proxy should not be called via this.** A call from inside the class bypasses the proxy, so @Transactional and friends do nothing.

SonarQube: S6809

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void a() {} void b() { a(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void a() {} void b() { other.a(); } }
```

## java/select-star

**SQL queries should retrieve only necessary fields.** SELECT * breaks when columns are added and fetches more than is used.

SonarQube: S6905

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String q = "SELECT * FROM users"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String q = "SELECT id, name FROM users"; } }
```

## java/multiple-main-methods

**Only one main method should be defined in a class.** Two entry points make it unclear which one starts the program.

SonarQube: S8446

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) {} public static void main(String a) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) {} }
```

## java/month-numeric-literal

**Month and DayOfWeek enums should be used instead of numeric literals.** LocalDate.of(2024, 3, 1) hides which month 3 is.

SonarQube: S8694

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.of(2024, 3, 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.of(2024, Month.MARCH, 1); } }
```

## java/try-fail-catch

**Dedicated exception assertions should be used instead of try-catch with fail.** assertThrows states the intent and fails with a clear message.

SonarQube: S8714

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); fail(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThrows(IOException.class, () -> f()); } }
```

## java/ignored-status-return

**Return values should not be ignored when they contain the operation status code.** mkdir() and createNewFile() report failure only through their result.

SonarQube: S899

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { dir.mkdirs(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (!dir.mkdirs()) { fail(); } } }
```

## java/instanceof-in-catch

**Exception types should not be tested using instanceof in catch blocks.** Separate catch clauses state the same thing and let the compiler check them.

SonarQube: S1193

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); } catch (Exception e) { if (e instanceof IOException) { g(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); } catch (IOException e) { g(); } } }
```

## java/catch-drops-cause

**Exception handlers should preserve the original exceptions.** A new exception thrown from a handler loses the stack trace unless it carries the cause.

SonarQube: S1166

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); } catch (IOException e) { throw new IllegalStateException("failed"); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); } catch (IOException e) { throw new IllegalStateException("failed", e); } } }
```

## java/error-extended

**java.lang.Error should not be extended.** Error is for the JVM; application failures are exceptions.

SonarQube: S1194

Noncompliant:

```java
class MyError extends Error {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class MyException extends RuntimeException {}
```

## java/throw-null-pointer

**NullPointerException should not be explicitly thrown.** It signals a bug the JVM found; callers can't tell yours from theirs.

SonarQube: S1695

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new NullPointerException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Objects.requireNonNull(x); } }
```

## java/catch-null-pointer

**NullPointerException should not be caught.** Fix the null instead of hiding the crash.

SonarQube: S1696

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); } catch (NullPointerException e) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (x != null) { f(); } } }
```

## java/superfluous-throws

**Exceptions in throws clauses should not be superfluous.** Unchecked exceptions need no declaration, and a listed subclass is covered by its parent.

SonarQube: S1130

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void g() throws IOException, RuntimeException {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void g() throws IOException {} }
```

## java/future-keyword-name

**Restricted identifiers should not be used as identifiers.** var, yield, record and sealed are contextual keywords; reusing them as names breaks on newer Java.

SonarQube: S6213, S1190 (related)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int record = 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int rec = 1; } }
```

## java/clone-without-cloneable

**Classes that override clone should be Cloneable and call super.clone().** Without both, clone() throws or returns an object of the wrong class.

SonarQube: S1182

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public Object clone() { return new A(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Cloneable { public Object clone() throws CloneNotSupportedException { return super.clone(); } }
```

## java/cloneable-without-clone

**Cloneables should implement clone.** Object.clone is protected, so a Cloneable class that doesn't expose it can't be cloned by callers.

SonarQube: S2157

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Cloneable {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Cloneable { public Object clone() { return null; } }
```

## java/url-in-hash

**URL.hashCode and URL.equals should be avoided.** Both resolve the host name, which blocks and gives different answers over time.

SonarQube: S2112

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Set<URL> urls; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Set<URI> urls; }
```

## java/array-to-string

**hashCode and toString should not be called on array instances.** They return the identity, not the content.

SonarQube: S2116

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int[] a) { a.toString(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int[] a) { Arrays.toString(a); } }
```

## java/long-bits-int

**Double.longBitsToDouble should take long as argument.** An int argument is widened and gives a tiny number, not the bit pattern.

SonarQube: S2127

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Double.longBitsToDouble(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Double.longBitsToDouble(1L); } }
```

## java/parse-by-boxing

**Parsing should be used to convert Strings to primitives.** valueOf builds a wrapper object only to unwrap it again.

SonarQube: S2130

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = Integer.valueOf(s).intValue(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = Integer.parseInt(s); } }
```

## java/random-float-as-int

**Methods of Random that return floating point values should not be used in random integer generation.** Casting a double to int to get a random integer is biased and slower than nextInt.

SonarQube: S2140

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = (int) (Math.random() * 10); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = new Random().nextInt(10); } }
```

## java/final-protected

**final classes should not have protected members.** Nothing can subclass a final class, so protected means package.

SonarQube: S2156

Noncompliant:

```java
final class A { protected int x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
final class A { private int x; }
```

## java/atomic-equals

**.equals() should not be used to test the values of Atomic classes.** Atomic classes compare by identity.

SonarQube: S2204

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { AtomicInteger a; boolean f(AtomicInteger b) { return a.equals(b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { AtomicInteger a; boolean f(AtomicInteger b) { return a.get() == b.get(); } }
```

## java/diamond-operator

**The diamond operator should be used.** Repeating the type arguments on both sides adds noise.

SonarQube: S2293

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<String>(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(); } }
```

## java/boolean-selector

**Public methods should not contain selector arguments.** A boolean flag means the method does two things.

Off by default: add `"java/boolean-selector"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2301

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void run(boolean fast) { if (fast) { go(); } else { slow(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public void runFast() { go(); } public void runSlow() { slow(); } }
```

## java/delete-on-exit

**deleteOnExit should not be used.** It leaks memory for the life of the JVM and does not run on a crash.

SonarQube: S2308

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.deleteOnExit(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.delete(); } }
```

## java/empty-file

**Files should not be empty.** A file with no declaration is dead weight.

SonarQube: S2309

Noncompliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
class A { List<String> l; }
```

## java/redundant-modifier

**Redundant modifiers should not be used.** The language already implies them.

SonarQube: S2333

Noncompliant:

```java
interface A { public void f(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface A { void f(); }
```

## java/exposes-internal-state

**Private mutable members should not be stored or returned directly.** A caller can change the object's state through the returned reference.

SonarQube: S2384

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int[] a; int[] get() { return a; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int[] a; int[] get() { return a.clone(); } }
```

## java/field-shadows-parent

**Child class fields should not shadow parent class fields.** The same name for two fields invites reading the wrong one.

SonarQube: S2387

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { int x; }
class C extends P { int x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { int x; }
class C extends P { int y; }
```

## java/thread-as-runnable

**Thread should not be used where a Runnable argument is expected.** A Thread is a Runnable, but passing one wastes it and invites start() confusion.

SonarQube: S2438

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { executor.submit(new Thread(task)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { executor.submit(task); } }
```

## java/synchronize-on-lock

**Synchronizing on a Lock object should be avoided.** Lock has its own lock() method; synchronized uses its monitor instead.

SonarQube: S2442

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Lock l; void f() { synchronized (l) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Lock l; void f() { l.lock(); } }
```

## java/synchronize-on-public-field

**Blocks should be synchronized on private final fields.** Other code can reassign or lock on a non-private field, breaking mutual exclusion.

SonarQube: S2445

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object lock = new Object(); void f() { synchronized (lock) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private final Object lock = new Object(); void f() { synchronized (lock) { g(); } } }
```

## java/thread-in-constructor

**Threads should not be started in constructors.** The new thread can see a half-built object.

SonarQube: S2693

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { A() { new Thread(this::run).start(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { A() {} void begin() { new Thread(this::run).start(); } }
```

## java/instance-writes-static

**Instance methods should not write to static fields.** Every instance changes shared state, which is a race.

SonarQube: S2696

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static int n; void f() { n = 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static int n; static void f() { n = 1; } }
```

## java/literal-assertion

**Literal boolean values and nulls should not be used in assertions.** The assertion's result doesn't depend on the code under test.

SonarQube: S2701

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertTrue(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertTrue(x > 0); } }
```

## java/getter-setter-sync

**Getters and setters should be synchronized in pairs.** Only one side synchronized still allows a stale read or a torn write.

SonarQube: S2886

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int n; public synchronized int getN() { return n; } public void setN(int v) { n = v; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int n; public synchronized int getN() { return n; } public synchronized void setN(int v) { n = v; } }
```

## java/unnecessary-semicolon

**Unnecessary semicolons should be omitted.** An extra ; after a member or type declaration does nothing.

SonarQube: S2959

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int x;; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int x; }
```

## java/unicode-escape

**Escaped Unicode characters should not be used.** \u00e9 is harder to read than é.

Off by default: add `"java/unicode-escape"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2973

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = "\u00e9"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = "é"; } }
```

## java/static-field-updated-in-constructor

**Static fields should not be updated in constructors.** Each new instance rewrites state shared by all of them.

SonarQube: S3010

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static int n; A() { n++; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int n; A() { n++; } }
```

## java/string-builder-empty

**isEmpty should be used to test for the emptiness of StringBuffers and Builders.** It states the intent.

SonarQube: S3033

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(StringBuilder sb) { return sb.length() == 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(StringBuilder sb) { return sb.isEmpty(); } }
```

## java/main-signature

**main should have the right signature.** The JVM only launches public static void main(String[]).

SonarQube: S3051

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void main(String[] args) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] args) {} }
```

## java/default-field-value

**Fields should not be initialized to default values.** The JVM already does it.

SonarQube: S3052

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int n = 0; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int n; }
```

## java/optional-parameter

**Optional should not be used for parameters.** It makes callers wrap values, and null is still possible.

SonarQube: S3553

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { void f(Optional<String> s) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { void f(String s) {} } }
```

## java/files-exists

**Java 8's Files.exists should not be used.** It is slow and its answer is stale on return.

SonarQube: S3725

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean b = Files.exists(p); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { byte[] b = Files.readAllBytes(p); } }
```

## java/raw-type

**Raw types should not be used.** Without type arguments the compiler can't check the contents.

SonarQube: S3740

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List l = new ArrayList(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(); } }
```

## java/this-escapes-constructor

**this should not be exposed from constructors.** Another object sees this before construction finishes.

SonarQube: S3366

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { A(Registry r) { r.add(this); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { A() {} }
```

## java/synchronized-collections

**Synchronized classes Vector, Hashtable, Stack and StringBuffer should not be used.** They lock every call, and still don't make compound operations safe.

Off by default: add `"java/synchronized-collections"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1149

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Vector<String> v = new Vector<>(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> v = new ArrayList<>(); } }
```

## java/public-field

**Class variable fields should not have public accessibility.** Callers can change it without the class noticing.

Off by default: add `"java/public-field"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1104

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public int n; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int n; }
```

## java/public-static-not-final

**public static fields should be constant.** Anyone can reassign it.

SonarQube: S1444

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public static int n; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static final int N = 1; }
```

## java/missing-braces

**Control structures should use curly braces.** A one-line body invites the dangling-statement bug.

Off by default: add `"java/missing-braces"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S121

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (x) f(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (x) { f(); } } }
```

## java/if-else-if-without-else

**if ... else if constructs should end with else clauses.** The last branch is where the unexpected case lands.

Off by default: add `"java/if-else-if-without-else"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S126

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else if (b) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else if (b) { g(); } else { h(); } } }
```

## java/literal-suffix-case

**Literal suffixes should be upper case.** l looks like 1.

SonarQube: S818

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { long n = 10l; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { long n = 10L; } }
```

## java/octal-literal

**Octal values should not be used.** 010 is 8, which surprises readers.

SonarQube: S1314

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = 010; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = 8; } }
```

## java/literal-on-the-right

**String literals should be placed on the left side when checking for equality.** "x".equals(s) can't throw NullPointerException.

SonarQube: S1132

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean b = s.equals("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean b = "x".equals(s); } }
```

## java/constructor-calls-overridable

**Constructors should only call non-overridable methods.** A subclass override runs before the subclass is constructed.

SonarQube: S1699

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { A() { init(); } void init() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { A() { init(); } private void init() {} }
```

## java/too-many-fields

**Classes should not have too many fields.** A class with dozens of fields has several responsibilities.

SonarQube: S1820

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int a1; int a2; int a3; int a4; int a5; int a6; int a7; int a8; int a9; int a10; int a11; int a12; int a13; int a14; int a15; int a16; int a17; int a18; int a19; int a20; int a21; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int a1; }
```

## java/nested-switch

**switch statements and expressions should not be nested.** Nested switches are hard to read.

SonarQube: S1821

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { switch (a) { case 1: switch (b) { case 2: f(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { switch (a) { case 1: g(b); } } }
```

## java/redundant-extends-object

**Extensions and implementations should not be redundant.** Every class extends Object.

SonarQube: S1939

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends Object {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {}
```

## java/ends-with-else-switch-labels

**Comma-separated labels should be used in Switch with colon case.** Empty case groups fall through; one label list says it better.

SonarQube: S6208

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { switch (x) { case 1: case 2: f(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { switch (x) { case 1, 2: f(); break; } } }
```

## java/modulus-equals-one

**Modulus results should not be checked for direct equality.** x % 2 == 1 is false for negative odd numbers.

SonarQube: S2197

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean odd = n % 2 == 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean odd = n % 2 != 0; } }
```

## java/default-locale

**String operations should not rely on the default system locale.** toUpperCase() gives different results in Turkish.

SonarQube: S1449

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = x.toUpperCase(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = x.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT); } }
```

## java/default-charset

**Classes and methods that rely on the default system encoding should not be used.** The result changes from machine to machine.

SonarQube: S1943

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { byte[] b = s.getBytes(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { byte[] b = s.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8); } }
```

## java/suppress-warnings

**Track uses of @SuppressWarnings annotations.** Each one silences a check.

Off by default: add `"java/suppress-warnings"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1309

Noncompliant:

```java
@SuppressWarnings("unchecked") class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {}
```

## java/nopmd-comment

**Track uses of NOPMD suppression comments.** Each one silences a check.

SonarQube: S1310

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int x; // NOPMD
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int x; }
```

## java/checkstyle-off

**Track uses of CHECKSTYLE:OFF suppression comments.** Each one silences a check.

SonarQube: S1315

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int x; // CHECKSTYLE:OFF
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int x; }
```

## java/logger-field

**Loggers should be private static final and should share a naming convention.** A logger shared or reassigned is a leak.

SonarQube: S1312

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Logger log = null; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private static final Logger LOG = null; }
```

## java/eager-fetch

**Avoid using FetchType.EAGER.** It loads the whole object graph on every query.

SonarQube: S6904

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @OneToMany(fetch = FetchType.EAGER) List<B> bs; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @OneToMany(fetch = FetchType.LAZY) List<B> bs; }
```

## java/type-parameter-shadowed

**Type parameters should not shadow other type parameters.** The inner T hides the outer T.

SonarQube: S4977

Noncompliant:

```java
class A<T> { <T> void f(T x) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A<T> { <U> void f(U x) {} }
```

## java/final-upper-bound

**The upper bound of type variables and wildcards should not be final.** A final bound allows only that type.

SonarQube: S4968

Noncompliant:

```java
class A<T extends String> {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A<T extends CharSequence> {}
```

## java/unused-type-parameter

**Unused type parameters should be removed.** A type parameter nothing refers to is noise.

SonarQube: S2326

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { <T> void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { <T> void f(T x) {} }
```

## java/unnecessary-bit-operation

**Unnecessary bit operations should not be performed.** x | 0 and x & -1 return x.

SonarQube: S2437

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int y = x | 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int y = x; } }
```

## java/redundant-close

**close() calls should not be redundant.** try-with-resources already closes the resource.

SonarQube: S4087

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try (Reader r = open()) { r.close(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try (Reader r = open()) { r.read(); } } }
```

## java/servlet-throws

**Exceptions should not be thrown from servlet methods.** An uncaught exception shows the client a stack trace.

SonarQube: S1989

Standards: [CWE-600](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/600.html)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends HttpServlet { void doGet(HttpServletRequest r, HttpServletResponse s) { throw new RuntimeException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends HttpServlet { void doGet(HttpServletRequest r, HttpServletResponse s) { s.sendError(500); } }
```

## java/static-iv

**Counter Mode initialization vectors should not be reused.** A fixed IV in CTR or GCM mode breaks confidentiality.

SonarQube: S6432

Standards: [CWE-323](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/323.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { IvParameterSpec iv = new IvParameterSpec(new byte[16]); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { IvParameterSpec iv = new IvParameterSpec(random); } }
```

## java/regex-invalid

**Regular expressions should be syntactically valid.** An invalid pattern raises re.error when it is compiled.

SonarQube: S5856

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a)"); } }
```

## java/regex-empty-group

**Regular expressions should not contain empty groups.** An empty group matches nothing and captures nothing useful.

SonarQube: S6331

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a()b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a(b)"); } }
```

## java/regex-single-character-class

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain only one character.** [a] is just a.

SonarQube: S6397

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[a]b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("ab"); } }
```

## java/regex-useless-group

**Non-capturing groups without quantifier should not be used.** The group groups nothing.

SonarQube: S6395

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(?:ab)c"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(?:ab)+c"); } }
```

## java/regex-superfluous-braces

**Superfluous curly brace quantifiers should be avoided.** {1} repeats once.

SonarQube: S6396

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a{1}"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a{2}"); } }
```

## java/regex-concise-syntax

**Regular expression quantifiers and character classes should be used concisely.** \d is shorter than [0-9], and + than {1,}.

SonarQube: S6353

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[0-9]{1,}"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\d+"); } }
```

## java/regex-backreference-order

**Back references in regular expressions should only refer to capturing groups that are matched before.** A group cannot refer to itself, so the reference never matches.

SonarQube: S6001

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a\\1)"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a)\\1"); } }
```

## java/regex-single-character-alternation

**Single-character alternations in regular expressions should be replaced with character classes.** a|b|c is [abc].

SonarQube: S6035

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a|b|c"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[abc]"); } }
```

## java/regex-multiple-spaces

**Regular expressions should not contain multiple spaces.** Several spaces in a row are easy to miscount.

SonarQube: S6326

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a   b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a {3}b"); } }
```

## java/regex-duplicate-class-member

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain the same character twice.** The repeated member does nothing.

SonarQube: S5869

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[aa]"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[ab]"); } }
```

## java/regex-grapheme-in-class

**Unicode Grapheme Clusters should be avoided inside regex character classes.** A class matches one code point, so a cluster is split.

SonarQube: S5868

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[e\\u0301]"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(?:e\\u0301)"); } }
```

## java/regex-repeat-matches-empty

**Repeated patterns in regular expressions should not match the empty string.** The loop can spin without consuming anything.

SonarQube: S5842

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a*)*"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a+)*"); } }
```

## java/regex-anchor-alternation

**Alternatives in regular expressions should be grouped when used with anchors.** In ^a|b|c$ the anchors bind only the first and last alternative.

SonarQube: S5850

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("^a|b|c$"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("^(?:a|b|c)$"); } }
```

## java/regex-redundant-alternative

**Regex alternatives should not be redundant.** An alternative another one already covers never decides the match.

SonarQube: S5855

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a|a"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a|b"); } }
```

## java/regex-possessive-always-fails

**Regex patterns following a possessive quantifier should not always fail.** A possessive quantifier takes everything it can match, leaving nothing for the next item.

SonarQube: S5994

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a*+a"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a*+b"); } }
```

## java/regex-unmatchable-boundary

**Regex boundaries should not be used in a way that can never be matched.** Text after $ or before ^ cannot match.

SonarQube: S5996

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("a$b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("^ab$"); } }
```

## java/regex-contradictory-lookaround

**Regex lookahead assertions should not be contradictory.** The next character is required and forbidden at once.

SonarQube: S6002

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(?=a)b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(?=a)ab"); } }
```

## java/regex-reluctant-at-end

**Reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions should be followed by an expression that can't match the empty string.** At the end of a pattern a reluctant quantifier matches as little as possible.

SonarQube: S6019

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("ab.*?"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("ab.*?c"); } }
```

## java/regex-reluctant-instead-of-class

**Character classes should be preferred over reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions.** `".*?"` backtracks where `"[^"]*"` does not.

SonarQube: S5857

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\"(.*?)\""); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\"([^\"]*)\""); } }
```

## java/regex-too-complex

**Regular expressions should not be too complicated.** A pattern nobody can read is a pattern nobody can fix.

SonarQube: S5843

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("((a|b)+(c|d)+)+((e|f)+(g|h)+)+((i|j)+(k|l)+)+"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a|b)+"); } }
```

## java/regex-catastrophic-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause catastrophic backtracking.** Nested repetitions that can match the same text take exponential time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S5852

Standards: [CWE-1333](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1333.html)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a+)+b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a+b)+"); } }
```

## java/regex-polynomial-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause non-linear backtracking.** Adjacent repetitions over the same characters take polynomial time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S8786

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\d*\\d*x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\d+x"); } }
```

## java/regex-ascii-letters

**Unicode-aware versions of character classes should be preferred.** [a-zA-Z] misses accented letters.

Off by default: add `"java/regex-ascii-letters"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5867

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[a-zA-Z]+"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\p{L}+"); } }
```

## java/regex-multiline-empty-lines

**Empty lines should not be tested with regex MULTILINE flag.** With MULTILINE, ^$ also matches an empty string at the end of the input, not just blank lines.

SonarQube: S5846

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("^$", Pattern.MULTILINE); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("^\\s*$", Pattern.MULTILINE); } }
```

## java/regex-canonical-equivalence

**Regexes containing characters subject to normalization should use the CANON_EQ flag.** A precomposed letter and its decomposed form look the same and don't match otherwise.

SonarQube: S5854

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\u00e9"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\u00e9", Pattern.CANON_EQ); } }
```

## java/regex-unicode-case

**Case insensitive Unicode regular expressions should enable the UNICODE_CASE flag.** CASE_INSENSITIVE alone only folds ASCII letters.

SonarQube: S5866

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\u00e9", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\u00e9", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE | Pattern.UNICODE_CASE); } }
```

## java/regex-stack-overflow

**Regular expressions should not overflow the stack.** Java matches a repeated alternation recursively, so long input throws StackOverflowError.

SonarQube: S5998

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("(a|b)*"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("[ab]*"); } }
```

## java/regex-control-escape

**The regex escape sequence \cX should only be used with characters in the @-_ range.** Other characters give a control character nobody expects.

SonarQube: S6070

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\c1"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\x01"); } }
```

## java/too-many-returns

**Methods should not have too many return statements.** Many exits make the flow hard to follow.

Off by default: add `"java/too-many-returns"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1142

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { if (x == 1) { return 1; } if (x == 2) { return 2; } if (x == 3) { return 3; } return 4; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { return x; } }
```

## java/nesting-depth

**Control flow statements should not be nested too deeply.** Deep nesting hides the main path.

Off by default: add `"java/nesting-depth"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S134

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { if (b) { if (c) { if (d) { g(); } } } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a && b) { g(); } } }
```

## java/method-complexity

**Methods should not be too complex.** Every branch is another path to test.

Off by default: add `"java/method-complexity"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1541

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int a) { if (a == 1) {} if (a == 2) {} if (a == 3) {} if (a == 4) {} if (a == 5) {} if (a == 6) {} if (a == 7) {} if (a == 8) {} if (a == 9) {} if (a == 10) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int a) { if (a == 1) {} } }
```

## java/expression-complexity

**Expressions should not be too complex.** Four boolean operators in one expression are hard to read.

Off by default: add `"java/expression-complexity"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1067

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean a, boolean b, boolean c, boolean d, boolean e) { return a && b || c && d || e; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean a, boolean b) { return a && b; } }
```

## java/switch-case-too-long

**switch case clauses should not have too many lines of code.** A long case belongs in its own method.

SonarQube: S1151

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a();
 b();
 c();
 d();
 e();
 f();
 g();
 break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a(); break; } } }
```

## java/anonymous-class-too-long

**Anonymous classes should not have too many lines.** A long anonymous class should have a name.

SonarQube: S1188

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object o = new Object() {
 int a;
 int b;
 int c;
 int d;
 int e;
 int f;
 int g;
 int h;
 int i;
 int j;
 int k;
 int l;
 int m;
 int n;
 int o;
 int p;
 int q;
 int r;
 int s;
 int t;
 int u;
 }; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object o = new Object() { int a; }; }
```

## java/lambda-too-long

**Lambdas should not have too many lines.** A long lambda hides what it does.

SonarQube: S5612

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Runnable r = () -> {
 a();
 b();
 c();
 d();
 e();
 f();
 g();
 h();
 i();
 j();
 k();
 }; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Runnable r = () -> { a(); }; }
```

## java/inner-class-too-long

**Inner classes should not have too many lines of code.** A long inner class is a class of its own.

Off by default: add `"java/inner-class-too-long"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2972

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { class B {
 int a;
 int b;
 int c;
 int d;
 int e;
 int f;
 int g;
 int h;
 int i;
 int j;
 int k;
 int l;
 int m;
 int n;
 int o;
 int p;
 int q;
 int r;
 int s;
 int t;
 int u;
 int v;
 int w;
 int x;
 int y;
 int z;
 } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { class B { int a; } }
```

## java/too-many-methods

**Classes should not have too many methods.** A class with dozens of methods has several responsibilities.

Off by default: add `"java/too-many-methods"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1448

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void a1() {} void a2() {} void a3() {} void a4() {} void a5() {} void a6() {} void a7() {} void a8() {} void a9() {} void a10() {} void a11() {} void a12() {} void a13() {} void a14() {} void a15() {} void a16() {} void a17() {} void a18() {} void a19() {} void a20() {} void a21() {} void a22() {} void a23() {} void a24() {} void a25() {} void a26() {} void a27() {} void a28() {} void a29() {} void a30() {} void a31() {} void a32() {} void a33() {} void a34() {} void a35() {} void a36() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void a1() {} }
```

## java/statements-on-one-line

**Statements should be on separate lines.** Several statements on one line are easy to misread.

SonarQube: S122

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { a(); b();
 } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 a();
 b();
 } }
```

## java/equality-in-loop-condition

**Equality operators should not be used in for loop termination conditions.** If the counter skips the bound, the loop never ends.

SonarQube: S888

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int n) { for (int i = 0; i != n; i += 2) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i += 2) { g(); } } }
```

## java/varargs-declared

**Functions should not be defined with a variable number of arguments.** Varargs hide the arity and defeat overloading.

Off by default: add `"java/varargs-declared"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S923

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(String... names) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(String[] names) {} }
```

## java/ternary-operator

**The ternary operator should not be used.** Nested or long ternaries are hard to read.

Off by default: add `"java/ternary-operator"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1774

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(boolean b) { return b ? 1 : 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(boolean b) { if (b) { return 1; } return 2; } }
```

## java/floating-point-equality

**Floating point numbers should not be tested for equality.** Rounding makes two mathematically equal results differ in the last bits.

SonarQube: S1244

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(double a, double b) { return a == b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(double a, double b) { return Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-9; } }
```

## java/float-increment

**Increment and decrement operators should not be used with floating point variables.** x++ on a double adds exactly 1, which drifts for large values.

SonarQube: S8346

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(double d) { d++; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(double d) { d += 1.0; } }
```

## java/deprecated-documentation

**Deprecated elements should have both the annotation and the Javadoc tag.** One without the other leaves tools or readers uninformed.

SonarQube: S1123

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { /** @deprecated use g */ @Deprecated void f() {} }
```

## java/serial-version-uid

**Serializable classes should have a serialVersionUID.** Without one the compiler computes it, and any change breaks old data.

SonarQube: S2057

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L; }
```

## java/comparator-serializable

**Comparators should be Serializable.** A TreeMap with a non-serializable comparator can't be serialized.

SonarQube: S2063

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Comparator<String> {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Comparator<String>, Serializable {}
```

## java/main-throws

**main should not throw anything.** An uncaught exception from main kills the program with a stack trace.

SonarQube: S2096

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) throws Exception {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) {} }
```

## java/string-switch

**Switches should be used for sequences of simple String tests.** A chain of equals comparisons is a switch.

SonarQube: S2196

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(String s) { if (s.equals("a")) { x(); } else if (s.equals("b")) { y(); } else if (s.equals("c")) { z(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(String s) { switch (s) { case "a": x(); break; default: break; } } }
```

## java/wildcard-import

**Wildcard imports should not be used.** They hide where names come from.

SonarQube: S2208

Noncompliant:

```java
import java.util.*;
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
```

## java/utility-class-instantiated

**Classes with only static methods should not be instantiated.** There is nothing for an instance to hold.

SonarQube: S2440

Noncompliant:

```java
class U { static void f() {} }
class A { void g() { new U(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class U { static void f() {} }
class A { void g() { U.f(); } }
```

## java/final-class-private-constructors

**Classes without public constructors should be final.** Nothing outside can extend it.

SonarQube: S2974

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private A() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
final class A { private A() {} }
```

## java/static-field-name

**Static non-final field names should comply with a naming convention.** camelCase tells readers it isn't a constant.

SonarQube: S3008

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static int My_Count; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static int myCount; }
```

## java/static-imports-many

**Classes should not have too many static imports.** Many static imports hide where names come from.

Off by default: add `"java/static-imports-many"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3030

Noncompliant:

```java
import static a.A.a1;
import static a.A.a2;
import static a.A.a3;
import static a.A.a4;
import static a.A.a5;
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
import static a.A.a1;
class A {}
```

## java/controller-scope

**Spring @Controller classes should not use @Scope.** Controllers are singletons by design.

SonarQube: S3750

Noncompliant:

```java
@Controller @Scope("session") class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
@Controller class A {}
```

## java/input-stream-bulk-read

**read(byte[],int,int) should be overridden.** The default calls read() once per byte.

SonarQube: S4929

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends InputStream { public int read() { return -1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends InputStream { public int read() { return -1; } public int read(byte[] b, int o, int l) { return -1; } }
```

## java/output-stream-bulk-write

**write(byte[],int,int) should be overridden.** The default calls write() once per byte.

SonarQube: S4349

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends OutputStream { public void write(int b) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends OutputStream { public void write(int b) {} public void write(byte[] b, int o, int l) {} }
```

## java/signed-byte-read

**InputStream.read() implementation should not return a signed byte.** read() must return 0 to 255, or -1.

SonarQube: S4517

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends InputStream { public int read() { byte b = next(); return b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends InputStream { public int read() { byte b = next(); return b & 0xFF; } }
```

## java/boxed-boolean-condition

**Avoid using boxed Boolean types directly in boolean expressions.** A null Boolean throws NullPointerException when unboxed.

SonarQube: S5411

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Boolean b) { if (b) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Boolean b) { if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(b)) { g(); } } }
```

## java/assert-multiple-calls

**Only one method invocation is expected when testing runtime exceptions.** Any other call in the lambda could be the one that throws.

SonarQube: S5778

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThrows(X.class, () -> { a(); b(); }); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { a(); assertThrows(X.class, () -> b()); } }
```

## java/assert-in-try

**Assertion methods should not be used within the try block of a try-catch catching an Error.** The AssertionError is caught, so the test passes.

SonarQube: S5779

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { try { assertEquals(1, x()); } catch (AssertionError e) { log(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { try { x(); } catch (AssertionError e) { log(); } assertEquals(1, y()); } }
```

## java/lifecycle-annotation

**Methods setUp() and tearDown() should be correctly annotated starting with JUnit4.** JUnit 4 ignores them without @Before and @After.

SonarQube: S5826

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void setUp() {} @Test void t() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Before void setUp() {} @Test void t() {} }
```

## java/assertj-context-late

**AssertJ methods setting the assertion context should come before an assertion.** as() after the assertion has no effect on it.

SonarQube: S5833

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(x).isEqualTo(1).as("msg"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(x).as("msg").isEqualTo(1); } }
```

## java/assertj-empty-collection

**AssertJ assertions allMatch and doesNotContain should also test for emptiness.** They pass on an empty collection.

SonarQube: S5841

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(list).allMatch(x -> x > 0); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(list).isNotEmpty().allMatch(x -> x > 0); } }
```

## java/assert-incompatible-types

**Assertions comparing incompatible types should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal.

SonarQube: S5845

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertEquals(1, "a"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertEquals(1, x()); } }
```

## java/mockito-annotations-uninitialized

**Annotated Mockito objects should be initialized.** @Mock fields stay null unless Mockito sets them up.

SonarQube: S5979

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Mock B b; @Test void t() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class) class A { @Mock B b; @Test void t() {} }
```

## java/mockito-matchers-mixed

**Mockito argument matchers should be used on all parameters.** Mixing matchers and raw values raises InvalidUseOfMatchersException.

SonarQube: S6073

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { verify(m).call(any(), 5); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { verify(m).call(any(), eq(5)); } }
```

## java/dangling-javadoc

**Dangling Javadoc comments should be removed.** A Javadoc comment that documents nothing is dead text.

SonarQube: S8491

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 /** note */
 g();
 } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 // note
 g();
 } }
```

## java/pattern-compiled-in-method

**Regex patterns should not be created needlessly.** Compiling on every call wastes time.

Off by default: add `"java/pattern-compiled-in-method"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4248

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return Pattern.compile("a+").matcher(s).matches(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private static final Pattern P = Pattern.compile("a+"); }
```

## java/large-number-underscores

**Underscores should be used to make large numbers readable.** 1000000 is easier to read as 1_000_000.

Off by default: add `"java/large-number-underscores"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2148

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int n = 1000000; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int n = 1_000_000; }
```

## java/test-method-name

**Test methods should comply with a naming convention.** Names that read as sentences are easy to scan.

Off by default: add `"java/test-method-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3578

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void Test_One() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Test void testOne() {} }
```

## java/abstract-class-name

**Abstract class names should comply with a naming convention.** Abstract and the class name together say what it is.
