SonarQube: S118

Noncompliant:

```java
abstract class Base {}
```

Compliant:

```java
abstract class AbstractBase {}
```

## java/abstract-class-mixed

**An abstract class should have both abstract and concrete methods.** With only one kind it is an interface or a plain class.

SonarQube: S1694

Noncompliant:

```java
abstract class A { abstract void f(); abstract void g(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
abstract class A { abstract void f(); void g() {} }
```

## java/public-api-undocumented

**Public types, methods and fields should be documented with Javadoc.** Callers read the Javadoc, not the code.

Off by default: add `"java/public-api-undocumented"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1176

Noncompliant:

```java
public class A { public void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { /** Doc. */ public class A { /** Doc. */ public void f() {} } } }
```

## java/private-field-used-locally

**Private fields only used as local variables in methods should become local variables.** A field one method writes and reads is a local variable in disguise.

SonarQube: S1450

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int tmp; void f() { tmp = 1; g(tmp); } void h() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int tmp = 1; g(tmp); } void h() {} }
```

## java/private-method-not-static

**private and final methods that don't access instance data should be static.** They don't need an instance.

Off by default: add `"java/private-method-not-static"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2325

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int twice(int x) { return x * 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private static int twice(int x) { return x * 2; } }
```

## java/configuration-field-injection

**Factory method injection should be used in @Configuration classes.** Autowired fields in a configuration class are injected before @Bean methods run, and hide the dependencies.

SonarQube: S3305

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration class A { @Autowired B b; }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Configuration class A { @Bean C c(B b) { return new C(b); } }
```

## java/bean-method-name

**Bean names should adhere to the naming conventions.** The method name becomes the bean name.

SonarQube: S6830

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Bean B My_Bean() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Bean B myBean() { return null; } }
```

## java/bean-method-called-directly

**@Bean methods for Singleton should not be invoked in @Configuration when proxyBeanMethods is false.** Without the proxy each direct call builds a new instance.

SonarQube: S6838

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false) class A { @Bean B b() { return new B(); } @Bean C c() { return new C(b()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false) class A { @Bean B b() { return new B(); } @Bean C c(B b) { return new C(b); } }
```

## java/spel-unbalanced

**SpEL expression should have a valid syntax.** An unclosed #{ or ${ is a startup error.

SonarQube: S6857

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Value("#{systemProperties['x']") String x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Value("#{systemProperties['x']}") String x; }
```

## java/cache-on-interface

**@Cache* annotations should only be applied on concrete classes.** Proxy-based caching does not see annotations on interfaces under CGLIB.

SonarQube: S7180

Noncompliant:

```java
interface A { @Cacheable("x") String f(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface A { String f(); }
```

## java/debug-enabled

**Debugging features should not be enabled in production.** Debug output shows internals to anyone who can read it.

SonarQube: S4507

Standards: [CWE-215](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/215.html), [CWE-489](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/489.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A10:2025 Mishandling of Exceptional Conditions, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
@EnableWebSecurity(debug = true) class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
@EnableWebSecurity class A {}
```

## java/autoescape-disabled

**Auto-escaping in HTML template engines should not be disabled.** Without it every value is an XSS vector.

SonarQube: S5247

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Mustache.compiler().escapeHTML(false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Mustache.compiler(); } }
```

## java/upload-size-unlimited

**HTTP request content length should be limited.** Unbounded uploads exhaust memory and disk.

SonarQube: S5693

Standards: [CWE-400](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/400.html), [CWE-770](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/770.html)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { resolver.setMaxUploadSize(-1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { resolver.setMaxUploadSize(1000000); } }
```

## java/server-version-disclosed

**Web application technologies should not disclose version information.** The version tells attackers which exploits to try.

SonarQube: S5689

Standards: [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { config.setSendServerVersion(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { config.setSendServerVersion(false); } }
```

## java/security-rule-order

**HttpSecurity URL patterns should be correctly ordered.** Rules after anyRequest() are never reached.

SonarQube: S4601

Standards: [CWE-285](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/285.html), [CWE-287](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/287.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.authorizeRequests().anyRequest().authenticated().antMatchers("/open").permitAll(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.authorizeRequests().antMatchers("/open").permitAll().anyRequest().authenticated(); } }
```

## java/password-fast-hash

**Passwords should not be stored in plaintext or with a fast hashing algorithm.** MD5 and SHA-2 are cheap to brute-force.

SonarQube: S5344

Standards: [CWE-256](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/256.html), [CWE-916](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/916.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String h = DigestUtils.sha256Hex(password); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String h = encoder.encode(password); } }
```

## java/receiver-without-permission

**Android broadcast receivers should not be registered without a permission.** Any app can send the broadcast.

SonarQube: S5322

Standards: [CWE-925](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/925.html), [CWE-926](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/926.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { registerReceiver(receiver, filter); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { registerReceiver(receiver, filter, PERMISSION, null); } }
```

## java/keystore-without-user-auth

**Android KeyStore keys should require user authentication.** A key anyone with the device can use protects nothing.

SonarQube: S6288

Standards: [CWE-522](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/522.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { KeyGenParameterSpec s = new KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder("k", 3).setBlockModes("GCM").build(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { KeyGenParameterSpec s = new KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder("k", 3).setUserAuthenticationRequired(true).build(); } }
```

## java/biometric-without-crypto

**Biometric authentication should be cryptographically bound.** Without a CryptoObject the result can be bypassed by hooking the callback.

SonarQube: S6293

Standards: [CWE-1390](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1390.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { biometricPrompt.authenticate(info); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { biometricPrompt.authenticate(info, cryptoObject); } }
```

## java/database-key-literal

**Mobile database encryption keys should not be disclosed.** A key in the app is a key in every copy of the app.

SonarQube: S6301

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), [CWE-321](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/321.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { db.getWritableDatabase("secret"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { db.getWritableDatabase(key); } }
```

## java/webview-javascript-interface

**Native methods should not be exposed to untrusted WebView content.** A bridge object is callable from any script the page loads.

SonarQube: S7409

Standards: [CWE-749](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/749.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { webView.addJavascriptInterface(bridge, "app"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { webView.loadUrl(url); } }
```

## java/persistent-device-id

**Avoid using persistent unique identifiers.** They track a user across apps and resets.

SonarQube: S7435

Standards: [CWE-213](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/213.html)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String id = tm.getImei(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(); } }
```

## java/exact-alarm

**Exact alarms should not be abused.** They wake the device and cost battery.

Off by default: add `"java/exact-alarm"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6891

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { alarm.setExact(0, 1L, intent); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { alarm.set(0, 1L, intent); } }
```

## java/sealed-permits-redundant

**Permitted types of a sealed class should be omitted if they are declared in the same file.** The compiler infers them.

SonarQube: S6217

Noncompliant:

```java
sealed class A permits B {}
final class B extends A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
sealed class A {}
final class B extends A {}
```

## java/record-serial-version-zero

**serialVersionUID field should not be set to 0L in records.** Records ignore it, and 0L suggests it was copied blindly.

SonarQube: S6219

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int x) implements Serializable { private static final long serialVersionUID = 0L; }
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int x) implements Serializable { }
```

## java/record-serialization-hooks

**Members ignored during record serialization should not be used.** Records are serialized by their components; these hooks are never called.

SonarQube: S6209

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int x) implements Serializable { private void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream o) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int x) implements Serializable { }
```

## java/unused-exception-parameter

**Unused exception parameter should use the unnamed variable pattern.** Java 22 allows _ for a name nobody reads.

Off by default: add `"java/unused-exception-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7467

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException _) { h(); } } }
```

## java/redundant-explicit-type

**Local-Variable Type Inference should be used.** The type is already on the right-hand side.

Off by default: add `"java/redundant-explicit-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6212

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { var sb = new StringBuilder(); } }
```

## java/concatenated-text-block

**String multiline concatenation should be replaced with Text Blocks.** Three lines joined with + and \n are a text block.

SonarQube: S6126

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = "a\n" + "b\n" + "c"; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
 a
 b
 c"""; }
```

## java/if-chain-to-switch

**Use switch instead of if-else chain to compare a variable against multiple cases.** A chain of comparisons with one variable is a switch.

SonarQube: S6880

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { if (x == 1) { a(); } else if (x == 2) { b(); } else if (x == 3) { c(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1 -> a(); case 2 -> b(); default -> c(); } } }
```

## java/clamp

**Use built-in Math.clamp methods.** Math.min(Math.max(..)) says it indirectly.

SonarQube: S6885

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int v = Math.min(Math.max(x, 0), 10); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int v = Math.clamp(x, 0, 10); } }
```

## java/redundant-time-conversion

**Redundant time instantiation patterns should be simplified.** LocalDateTime.now().toLocalDate() is LocalDate.now().

SonarQube: S8695

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDateTime.now().toLocalDate(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.now(); } }
```

## java/value-based-identity

**Value-based classes should be compared using their value.** == on Optional or a java.time object compares identities, which are not meaningful.

SonarQube: S8696

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Optional<String> a, Optional<String> b) { return a == b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Optional<String> a, Optional<String> b) { return a.equals(b); } }
```

## java/clock-in-test

**The system clock should not be used in unit tests.** A test that reads the clock is flaky.

Off by default: add `"java/clock-in-test"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8692

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { Instant.now(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { Instant.now(clock); } }
```

## java/scoped-value-ignored

**ScopedValue.where results should not be ignored.** The binding only exists inside the returned carrier's run or call.

SonarQube: S8432

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ScopedValue.where(KEY, 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ScopedValue.where(KEY, 1).run(task); } }
```

## java/startup-method

**Methods annotated with @Startup should be non-static, non-producer, and parameter-free.** Quarkus can't call them otherwise.

SonarQube: S8911

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Startup static void init() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Startup void init() {} }
```

## java/label-in-switch

**switch statements should not contain non-case labels.** A label that looks like a case but isn't compiles silently.

SonarQube: S1219

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: done: g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: g(); } } }
```

## java/parameter-overwritten

**Method parameters' initial values should not be ignored.** The first thing the method does is replace the argument it was given.

SonarQube: S1226

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { x = 1; return x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { int y = x + 1; return y; } }
```

## java/enum-key-map

**Maps with keys that are enum values should use the EnumMap implementation.** EnumMap is faster and smaller.

SonarQube: S1640

Noncompliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { Object m = new HashMap<Color, String>(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { Object m = new EnumMap<Color, String>(Color.class); }
```

## java/annotation-container

**Annotation repetitions should not be wrapped.** Repeatable annotations can be written one after another.

SonarQube: S1710

Noncompliant:

```java
@Roles({@Role("a"), @Role("b")}) class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
@Role("a") @Role("b") class A {}
```

## java/dead-store

**Unused assignments should be removed.** The value assigned first is overwritten before anything reads it.

SonarQube: S1854

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x; x = compute(); x = other(); g(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x; x = other(); g(x); } }
```

## java/silly-equality

**Unnecessary equality checks should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal.

SonarQube: S2159

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f() { return 1 == "a"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int x) { return x == 1; } }
```

## java/multiplication-overflow

**Math operands should be cast before assignment.** The multiplication is done in int and only then widened, so it overflows first.

SonarQube: S2184

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { long f(int a, int b) { long r = a * b; return r; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { long f(int a, int b) { long r = (long) a * b; return r; } }
```

## java/byte-shift

**Raw byte values should not be used in bitwise operations in combination with shifts.** A byte is sign-extended, so the high bits are wrong.

SonarQube: S3034

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(byte b) { return b << 8; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(byte b) { return (b & 0xFF) << 8; } }
```

## java/builder-never-read

**StringBuilder data should be used.** Building text nobody reads is wasted work.

SonarQube: S3063

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(); sb.append("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f() { StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(); sb.append("x"); return sb.toString(); } }
```

## java/lock-on-value-based

**Value-based classes should not be used for locking.** Boxed numbers and Optional may be shared or interned.

SonarQube: S3436

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Integer n = 0; void f() { synchronized (n) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object lock = new Object(); void f() { synchronized (lock) { g(); } } }
```

## java/varargs-array

**Arrays should not be created for varargs parameters.** The compiler builds the array.

SonarQube: S3878

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = String.format("%s", new Object[] {x}); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = String.format("%s", x); } }
```

## java/getter-wrong-field

**Getters and setters should access the expected fields.** A getter that returns another field is a copy-paste bug.

SonarQube: S4275

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int a; private int b; int getA() { return b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int a; private int b; int getA() { return a; } }
```

## java/equals-nonnull-parameter

**equals method parameters should not be marked @Nonnull.** equals(null) must return false, not throw.

SonarQube: S4454

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(@Nonnull Object o) { return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
```

## java/substring-index-of

**String offset-based methods should be preferred for finding substrings from offsets.** indexOf(x, from) avoids the copy.

SonarQube: S4635

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = s.substring(3).indexOf("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = s.indexOf("x", 3); } }
```

## java/guava-replaceable

**Java features should be preferred to Guava.** The JDK has Optional, Function, Predicate and Supplier.

SonarQube: S4738

Noncompliant:

```java
import com.google.common.base.Optional;
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.Optional;
```

## java/checked-exception-test-body

**Only one method invocation is expected when testing checked exceptions.** Any other call in the try block could be the one that throws.

SonarQube: S5783

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { try { a(); b(); fail(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { a(); try { b(); fail(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

## java/collect-with-add

**collect should be used with Streams instead of list::add.** forEach(list::add) mutates shared state.

Off by default: add `"java/collect-with-add"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2203

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stream.forEach(list::add); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { list = stream.collect(Collectors.toList()); } }
```

## java/jdbc-update-in-loop

**Use batch Processing in JDBC.** One round trip per row is slow.

SonarQube: S6912

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ps.executeUpdate(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ps.executeBatch(); } }
```

## java/throws-many-checked

**Public methods should throw at most one checked exception.** Callers have to handle each one.

Off by default: add `"java/throws-many-checked"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1160

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void f() throws IOException, SQLException {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public void f() throws IOException {} }
```

## java/throws-checked

**Checked exceptions should not be thrown.** They force every caller to handle them.

Off by default: add `"java/throws-checked"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1162

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new IOException("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new UncheckedIOException("x", e); } }
```

## java/qualified-name

**Simple class names should be used.** A fully qualified name in code is noise.

Off by default: add `"java/qualified-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1942

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { java.util.List l = null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List l = null; } }
```

## java/one-type-per-file

**Files should contain only one top-level class or interface each.** Several types per file are hard to find.

Off by default: add `"java/one-type-per-file"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1996

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {}
class B {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {}
```

## java/package-private-field

**Member variable visibility should be specified.** A missing modifier is package visibility by accident.

Off by default: add `"java/package-private-field"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2039

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int n; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int n; }
```

## java/boolean-method-name

**The names of methods with boolean return values should start with is or has.** Callers read isReady() as a question.

Off by default: add `"java/boolean-method-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2047

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean ready() { return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean isReady() { return true; } }
```

## java/local-constant-name

**Local constants should follow naming conventions for constants.** final locals named like variables hide that they never change.

Off by default: add `"java/local-constant-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4174

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { final int max = 3; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { final int MAX = 3; } }
```

## java/junit4-import

**Migrate your tests from JUnit4 to the new JUnit5 annotations.** JUnit 4 is in maintenance.

Off by default: add `"java/junit4-import"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5793

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
```

## java/operator-precedence

**Limited dependence should be placed on operator precedence.** a && b || c needs parentheses to be read at a glance.

Off by default: add `"java/operator-precedence"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S864

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean a, boolean b, boolean c) { return a && b || c; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean a, boolean b, boolean c) { return (a && b) || c; } }
```

## java/increment-in-expression

**Increment and decrement operators should not be used in a method call or mixed with other operators in an expression.** i++ inside an expression is easy to misread.

Off by default: add `"java/increment-in-expression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S881

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int i) { g(i++); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int i) { g(i); i++; } }
```

## java/magic-number

**Magic numbers should not be used.** A bare 86400 in code has no name.

Off by default: add `"java/magic-number"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S109

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { return x * 86400; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final int DAY = 86400; int f(int x) { return x * DAY; } }
```

## java/test-assertion-message

**Test assertions should include messages.** A failure without a message needs a debugger.

Off by default: add `"java/test-assertion-message"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2698

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertTrue(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertTrue(x, "x holds"); } }
```

## java/brace-on-its-own-line

**An open curly brace should be located at the end of a line.** The brace belongs with the statement it opens.

Off by default: add `"java/brace-on-its-own-line"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1105

Noncompliant:

```java
class A
{
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
}
```

## java/brace-at-line-end

**An open curly brace should be located at the beginning of a line.** Allman style puts every brace on its own line.

Off by default: add `"java/brace-at-line-end"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1106

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A
{
}
```

## java/else-on-another-line

**Close curly brace and the next else, catch and finally keywords should be located on the same line.** Cuddled else keeps the chain together.

Off by default: add `"java/else-on-another-line"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1107

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) {
 g();
}
else {
 h();
} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) {
 g();
} else {
 h();
} } }
```

## java/else-on-the-same-line

**Close curly brace and the next else, catch and finally keywords should be on two different lines.** Allman style puts the keyword on its own line.

Off by default: add `"java/else-on-the-same-line"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1108

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) {
 g();
} else {
 h();
} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) {
 g();
}
else {
 h();
} } }
```

## java/closing-brace-not-first

**A close curly brace should be located at the beginning of a line.** A brace after code is easy to miss.

Off by default: add `"java/closing-brace-not-first"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1109

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 g(); }
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 g();
}
}
```

## java/loop-bound-changes

**for loop stop conditions should be invariant.** Changing the bound inside the body makes the loop's length hard to predict.

SonarQube: S127

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { n--; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { g(); } } }
```

## java/enum-set

**Sets with elements that are enum values should be replaced with EnumSet.** EnumSet is faster and smaller.

SonarQube: S1641

Noncompliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { Object s = new HashSet<Color>(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { Object s = EnumSet.noneOf(Color.class); }
```

## java/identity-with-equals

**== and != should not be used when equals is overridden.** Two equal objects can be different instances.

SonarQube: S1698

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
class A { boolean f(P a, P b) { return a == b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
class A { boolean f(P a, P b) { return a.equals(b); } }
```

## java/functional-interface-redefined

**Standard functional interfaces should not be redefined.** java.util.function already has this shape.

Off by default: add `"java/functional-interface-redefined"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1711

Noncompliant:

```java
interface Maker { String get(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface Maker { String get(); String other(); }
```

## java/wait-on-condition

**Object.wait should not be called on objects that implement Condition.** Condition has await() and signal() for this.

SonarQube: S1844

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Condition c) { c.wait(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Condition c) { c.await(); } }
```

## java/static-member-via-instance

**static members should be accessed statically.** Calling a static method on an instance suggests it is not.

SonarQube: S2209

Noncompliant:

```java
class U { static void f() {} }
class A { void g(U u) { u.f(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class U { static void f() {} }
class A { void g() { U.f(); } }
```

## java/lambda-explicit-types

**Types should be used in lambdas.** Repeating the parameter types adds noise.

Off by default: add `"java/lambda-explicit-types"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2211

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Runnable r = null; java.util.function.Function<String, Integer> f = (String s) -> s.length(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { java.util.function.Function<String, Integer> f = s -> s.length(); } }
```

## java/inner-class-static

**Inner classes which do not reference their owning classes should be static.** A non-static inner class carries a hidden reference to its outer instance.

Off by default: add `"java/inner-class-static"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2694

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int n; class B { int m; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int n; static class B { int m; } }
```

## java/get-class-loader

**JEE applications should not getClassLoader.** Application servers manage class loaders themselves.

Off by default: add `"java/get-class-loader"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3032

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ClassLoader c = A.class.getClassLoader(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ClassLoader c = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader(); } }
```

## java/string-index-bounds

**Indexes passed to String operations should be within the string's bounds.** A negative or inverted index throws StringIndexOutOfBoundsException.

SonarQube: S3039

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String t = s.substring(-1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String t = s.substring(1); } }
```

## java/loops-over-same-collection

**Multiple loops over the same set should be combined.** Two passes over one collection can be one.

Off by default: add `"java/loops-over-same-collection"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3047

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (String s : l) { a(s); } for (String s : l) { b(s); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (String s : l) { a(s); b(s); } } }
```

## java/collection-never-read

**Collection contents should be used.** A collection that is filled and never read is dead code.

SonarQube: S4030

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(); l.add("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(); l.add("x"); return l; } }
```

## java/member-order

**The members of an interface or class declaration should appear in a pre-defined order.** Fields, then constructors, then methods.

Off by default: add `"java/member-order"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1213

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {} int n; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int n; void f() {} }
```

## java/missing-license-header

**Track lack of copyright and license headers.** A header states who owns the file and under which terms it is shared.

Off by default: add `"java/missing-license-header"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1451

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { /* Copyright 2026 Example Ltd. */
class A {} } }
```

## java/ternary-boxed-mix

**Dissimilar primitive wrappers should not be used with the ternary operator without explicit casting.** Integer and Double branches unbox and promote, so a null throws and an int silently becomes a double.

SonarQube: S2154

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Number f(boolean b, Integer i, Double d) { return b ? i : d; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Number f(boolean b, Integer i, Integer j) { return b ? i : j; } }
```

## java/persistence-annotation-mix

**Do not mix field and getter persistence annotations.** JPA uses the placement of @Id to pick field or property access, and ignores the other.

SonarQube: S2206

Noncompliant:

```java
@Entity class A { @Id Long id; @Column String getName() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Entity class A { @Id Long id; @Column String name; }
```

## java/session-attribute-not-serializable

**Non-serializable objects should not be stored in HttpSession instances.** Session replication and passivation need serializable values.

SonarQube: S2441

Noncompliant:

```java
class P {}
class A { void f(HttpSession s) { s.setAttribute("p", new P()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P implements Serializable {}
class A { void f(HttpSession s) { s.setAttribute("p", new P()); } }
```

## java/session-attributes-not-completed

**@Controller classes that use @SessionAttributes must call setComplete on their SessionStatus.** Without it the attributes live until the session ends.

SonarQube: S3753

Noncompliant:

```java
@Controller @SessionAttributes("x") class A { void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Controller @SessionAttributes("x") class A { void f(SessionStatus s) { s.setComplete(); } }
```

## java/unindented-conditional

**A conditionally executed single line should be denoted by indentation.** A statement at the same indent as its if looks unconditional.

SonarQube: S3973

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 if (a)
 g();
 } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
 if (a)
  g();
 } }
```

## java/collection-constructor-as-function

**Collection constructors should not be used as java.util.function.Function.** ArrayList::new used as a Function takes the argument as the initial capacity.

SonarQube: S5329

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stream.map(ArrayList::new); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stream.map(x -> new ArrayList<>(x)); } }
```

## java/list-remove-in-loop

**List.remove() should not be used in ascending for loops.** Removing shifts the elements, so the next one is skipped.

SonarQube: S5413

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (int i = 0; i < l.size(); i++) { l.remove(i); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (int i = l.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) { l.remove(i); } } }
```

## java/index-of-range

**String.indexOf should be used with correct ranges.** A negative from-index is treated as 0, which hides a bug.

SonarQube: S6915

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = s.indexOf("x", -1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = s.indexOf("x", 0); } }
```

## java/subclass-adds-fields-not-equals

**Subclasses that add fields to classes that override equals should also override equals.** Two instances can compare equal while differing in the new fields.

SonarQube: S2160

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
class C extends P { int extra; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
class C extends P { int extra; public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
```

## java/infinite-loop

**Loops should not be infinite.** A loop with no way out hangs the thread.

SonarQube: S2189

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { while (true) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { while (true) { if (g()) { break; } } } }
```

## java/zero-denominator

**Zero should not be a possible denominator.** Integer division by zero throws ArithmeticException.

SonarQube: S3518

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { return x / 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { return x / 2; } }
```

## java/invariant-return

**Methods returns should not be invariant.** If every return gives the same constant, the value carries no information.

SonarQube: S3516

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(boolean b) { if (b) { return 0; } return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(boolean b) { if (b) { return 0; } return 1; } }
```

## java/redundant-assignment

**Assignments should not be redundant.** The variable already holds that value.

SonarQube: S4165

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { x = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { x = x + 1; } }
```

## java/map-get-then-put

**Map.get and value test should be replaced with single method call.** computeIfAbsent does both in one step and one lookup.

SonarQube: S3824

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Map<String, String> m, String k) { String v = m.get(k); if (v == null) { v = "x"; m.put(k, v); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Map<String, String> m, String k) { String v = m.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> "x"); } }
```

## java/optional-get-unchecked

**Optional value should only be accessed after calling isPresent().** get() on an empty Optional throws NoSuchElementException.

SonarQube: S3655

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(Optional<String> o) { return o.get(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(Optional<String> o) { return o.orElse(""); } }
```

## java/null-dereference

**Null pointers should not be dereferenced.** The variable is null on this path.

SonarQube: S2259

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = null; s.length(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = "a"; s.length(); } }
```

## java/resource-not-closed

**Resources should be closed.** An unclosed stream leaks a file handle.

SonarQube: S2095

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { InputStream in = new FileInputStream("x"); in.read(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream("x")) { in.read(); } } }
```

## java/constant-condition

**Conditionally executed code should be reachable.** A condition that is always true or false makes one branch dead.

SonarQube: S2583

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (false) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(boolean b) { if (b) { g(); } } }
```

## java/gratuitous-boolean

**Boolean expressions should not be gratuitous.** A literal operand makes the other side pointless.

SonarQube: S2589

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean b) { return b && true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean b) { return b; } }
```

## java/lock-not-released

**Locks should be released on all paths.** An exception between lock() and unlock() leaves the lock held.

SonarQube: S2222

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Lock l; void f() { l.lock(); g(); l.unlock(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Lock l; void f() { l.lock(); try { g(); } finally { l.unlock(); } } }
```

## java/stream-intermediate-unused

**Intermediate Stream methods should not be left unused.** filter() and map() build a pipeline that never runs without a terminal operation.

SonarQube: S3958

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { l.stream().filter(x -> true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { l.stream().filter(x -> true).count(); } }
```

## java/stream-reused

**Consumed Stream pipelines should not be reused.** A stream can be consumed once.

SonarQube: S3959

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { Stream<String> s = l.stream(); s.count(); s.forEach(x -> {}); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { l.stream().count(); l.stream().forEach(x -> {}); } }
```

## java/min-max-constant

**Min and max used in combination should not always return the same value.** With the bounds reversed the result is always the same constant.

SonarQube: S3065

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int v = Math.min(Math.max(x, 10), 5); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int v = Math.min(Math.max(x, 5), 10); } }
```

## java/inheritance-too-deep

**Inheritance tree of classes should not be too deep.** A deep chain hides where behaviour lives.

SonarQube: S110

Noncompliant:

```java
class A1 {}
class A2 extends A1 {}
class A3 extends A2 {}
class A4 extends A3 {}
class A5 extends A4 {}
class A6 extends A5 {}
class A7 extends A6 {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A1 {}
class A2 extends A1 {}
```

## java/static-via-subclass

**static base class members should not be accessed via derived types.** Writing the subclass hides where the member lives.

SonarQube: S3252

Noncompliant:

```java
class B { static void s() {} }
class D extends B {}
class A { void f() { D.s(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class B { static void s() {} }
class D extends B {}
class A { void f() { B.s(); } }
```

## java/abstract-method-redeclared

**Abstract methods should not be redundant.** The interface already declares it.

SonarQube: S3038

Noncompliant:

```java
interface I { void f(); }
abstract class A implements I { public abstract void f(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface I { void f(); }
abstract class A implements I { }
```

## java/compareto-overloaded

**compareTo should not be overloaded.** Comparable<T> calls compareTo(T); an overload with another type is never called.

SonarQube: S4351

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Comparable<A> { public int compareTo(Object o) { return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Comparable<A> { public int compareTo(A o) { return 0; } }
```

## java/hash-key-without-hashcode

**Classes that don't define hashCode() should not be used in hashes.** The default hashCode is the identity, so equal keys miss.

Off by default: add `"java/hash-key-without-hashcode"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2141

Noncompliant:

```java
class P {}
class A { Set<P> s; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { public int hashCode() { return 1; } }
class A { Set<P> s; }
```

## java/deprecated-call

**@Deprecated code should not be used.** Deprecated code is on its way out.

Off by default: add `"java/deprecated-call"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1874

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated void old() {} void f() { old(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void neu() {} void f() { neu(); } }
```

## java/removal-call

**@Deprecated code marked for removal should never be used.** It will not compile in a coming release.

SonarQube: S5738

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated(forRemoval = true) void old() {} void f() { old(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void neu() {} void f() { neu(); } }
```

## java/tree-key-not-comparable

**Types used as keys in Maps should implement Comparable.** A TreeMap throws ClassCastException on a key it can't order.

Off by default: add `"java/tree-key-not-comparable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6411

Noncompliant:

```java
class P {}
class A { Object m = new TreeMap<P, String>(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P implements Comparable<P> { public int compareTo(P o) { return 0; } }
class A { Object m = new TreeMap<P, String>(); }
```

## java/object-stream-append

**Files opened in append mode should not be used with ObjectOutputStream.** Each append writes a new stream header, which readers choke on.

SonarQube: S2689

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ObjectOutputStream o = new ObjectOutputStream(new FileOutputStream("f", true)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ObjectOutputStream o = new ObjectOutputStream(new FileOutputStream("f")); } }
```

## java/non-null-set-to-null

**@NonNull values should not be set to null.** The annotation promises a value, and null breaks it.

SonarQube: S2637

Noncompliant:

```java
@NonNull String s = null;
```

Compliant:

```java
@NonNull String s = "a";
```

## java/xml-parser-xxe

**XML parsers should not be vulnerable to XXE attacks.** An XML parser with external entities lets a document read local files.

SonarQube: S2755

Standards: [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html), [CWE-827](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/827.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DocumentBuilderFactory f = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DocumentBuilderFactory f = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance(); f.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true); } }
```

## java/xml-xinclude

**XML parsers should not allow inclusion of arbitrary files.** XInclude pulls files into the document.

SonarQube: S6373

Standards: [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html), [CWE-827](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/827.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.setXIncludeAware(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.setXIncludeAware(false); } }
```

## java/xml-entity-expansion

**XML parsers should not be vulnerable to Denial of Service attacks.** Turning off secure processing lets a small document expand into gigabytes.

SonarQube: S6376

Standards: [CWE-776](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/776.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { f.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true); } }
```

## java/xml-signature-validation

**XML signatures should be validated securely.** Without secure validation a crafted signature can exhaust memory or read files.

SonarQube: S6377

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ctx.setProperty("org.jcp.xml.dsig.secureValidation", false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ctx.setProperty("org.jcp.xml.dsig.secureValidation", true); } }
```

## java/entity-bound-to-request

**Database Operations should not be vulnerable to mass assignment.** Binding a request straight onto an entity lets a caller set any field.

SonarQube: S4684

Standards: [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
@Entity class User { String role; }
class A { @PostMapping("/u") void save(@RequestBody User u) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class UserDto { String name; }
class A { @PostMapping("/u") void save(@RequestBody UserDto u) {} }
```

## java/ejb-thread

**JEE applications should not use threads or synchronization.** The container manages threads and concurrency for its components.

SonarQube: S2654

Noncompliant:

```java
@Stateless class A { void f() { new Thread(r).start(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Stateless class A { void f() { executor.submit(r); } }
```

## java/serialization-parent-constructor

**The non-serializable super class of a Serializable class should have a no-argument constructor.** Deserialization calls it to build the non-serializable part.

SonarQube: S2055

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { P(int x) {} }
class C extends P implements Serializable { C() { super(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { P() {} P(int x) {} }
class C extends P implements Serializable { C() { super(1); } }
```

## java/write-non-serializable

**writeObject argument must implement Serializable.** ObjectOutputStream throws NotSerializableException at runtime.

SonarQube: S2118

Noncompliant:

```java
class P {}
class A { void f(ObjectOutputStream o) { o.writeObject(new P()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P implements Serializable {}
class A { void f(ObjectOutputStream o) { o.writeObject(new P()); } }
```

## java/runtime-annotation-check

**Reflection should not be used to check non-runtime annotations.** An annotation without RUNTIME retention is invisible to reflection.

SonarQube: S2109

Noncompliant:

```java
@interface Marker {}
class A { boolean f(Class<?> c) { return c.isAnnotationPresent(Marker.class); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME) @interface Marker {}
class A { boolean f(Class<?> c) { return c.isAnnotationPresent(Marker.class); } }
```

## java/transaction-modes-differ

**Methods should not call same-class methods with incompatible @Transactional values.** A call inside the class bypasses the proxy, so the second annotation is ignored.

SonarQube: S2229

Noncompliant:

```java
@Service class A { @Transactional void f() { g(); } @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.REQUIRES_NEW) void g() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Service class A { @Transactional void f() { g(); } @Transactional void g() {} }
```

## java/arguments-in-wrong-order

**Parameters should be passed in the correct order.** Passing a, b to a method declared (b, a) swaps their meaning.

SonarQube: S2234

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void m(int width, int height) {} void f(int width, int height) { m(height, width); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void m(int width, int height) {} void f(int width, int height) { m(width, height); } }
```

## java/static-init-uses-subclass

**Classes should not access their own subclasses during class initialization.** The subclass's initializer runs before the parent's finishes.

SonarQube: S2390

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { static final P DEFAULT = new C(); }
class C extends P {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { static P make() { return new C(); } }
class C extends P {}
```

## java/lazy-static-unsynchronized

**Lazy initialization of static fields should be synchronized.** Two threads can both see null and both initialize.

Off by default: add `"java/lazy-static-unsynchronized"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2444

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static Object inst; static Object get() { if (inst == null) { inst = new Object(); } return inst; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static Object inst; static synchronized Object get() { if (inst == null) { inst = new Object(); } return inst; } }
```

## java/double-checked-publication

**Assignment of lazy-initialized members should be the last step with double-checked locking.** Other threads can see the half-built object.

SonarQube: S3064

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Foo inst; Foo get() { if (inst == null) { synchronized (this) { if (inst == null) { inst = new Foo(); inst.init(); } } } return inst; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Foo inst; Foo get() { if (inst == null) { synchronized (this) { if (inst == null) { Foo f = new Foo(); f.init(); inst = f; } } } return inst; } }
```

## java/wait-under-several-locks

**wait should not be called when multiple locks are held.** wait releases only one lock, so the others stay held and can deadlock.

SonarQube: S3046

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (a) { synchronized (b) { b.wait(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (b) { b.wait(); } } }
```

## java/write-object-only-synchronized

**writeObject should not be the only synchronized code in a class.** State written under a lock that nothing else takes is not protected.

SonarQube: S3042

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private synchronized void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream o) {} void g() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private synchronized void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream o) {} synchronized void g() {} }
```

## java/visible-for-testing-used

**Class members annotated with @VisibleForTesting should not be accessed from production code.** The annotation says the member is public only for tests.

SonarQube: S5803

Noncompliant:

```java
class B { @VisibleForTesting void hook() {} }
class A { void f(B b) { b.hook(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class B { void hook() {} }
class A { void f(B b) { b.hook(); } }
```

## java/collection-call-type

**Inappropriate Collection calls should not be made.** Asking a List<String> whether it contains an int is always false.

SonarQube: S2175

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(List<String> l) { return l.contains(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(List<String> l) { return l.contains("a"); } }
```

## java/for-each-narrowing

**An iteration on a Collection should be performed on the type handled by the Collection.** The loop variable is cast on every pass and fails when the type is wrong.

SonarQube: S4838

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<Object> l) { for (String s : l) { g(s); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (String s : l) { g(s); } } }
```

## java/primitive-array-stream

**Arrays.stream should be used for primitive arrays.** Stream.of(intArray) gives a stream of one array.

SonarQube: S3631

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object f(int[] a) { return Stream.of(a); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object f(int[] a) { return Arrays.stream(a); } }
```

## java/float-arithmetic

**Math should not be performed on floats.** float has about 7 digits, so sums lose precision quickly.

Off by default: add `"java/float-arithmetic"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2164

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { float f(float a, float b) { return a + b; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { double f(double a, double b) { return a + b; } }
```

## java/hash-capacity

**Hash-based collections with known capacity should be initialized with the proper related size.** new HashMap<>(n) resizes before holding n entries, because the load factor is 0.75.

SonarQube: S6485

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Map<String, String> m = new HashMap<>(list.size()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Map<String, String> m = HashMap.newHashMap(list.size()); } }
```

## java/class-coupling

**Classes should not depend on an excessive number of classes.** A class that touches dozens of types does too much.

SonarQube: S6539, S1200

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { B1 a1; B2 a2; B3 a3; B4 a4; B5 a5; B6 a6; B7 a7; B8 a8; B9 a9; B10 a10; B11 a11; B12 a12; B13 a13; B14 a14; B15 a15; B16 a16; B17 a17; B18 a18; B19 a19; B20 a20; B21 a21; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { B1 a1; }
```

## java/redundant-nullability

**Redundant nullability annotations should be removed.** A primitive can't be null.

Off by default: add `"java/redundant-nullability"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6665

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(@Nullable int x) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) {} }
```

## java/collect-redundant

**Stream.collect calls should not be redundant.** A dedicated terminal operation does the same.

Off by default: add `"java/collect-redundant"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4266

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { long n = stream.collect(Collectors.counting()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { long n = stream.count(); } }
```

## java/file-delete

**java.nio.Files#delete should be preferred.** File.delete() returns false on failure instead of saying why.

SonarQube: S4042

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(File file) { file.delete(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Path p) throws IOException { Files.delete(p); } }
```

## java/hex-of-byte

**Integer.toHexString should not be used to build hexadecimal strings.** A negative byte prints as ffffff80, and a small one loses its leading zero.

SonarQube: S4425

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(byte b) { return Integer.toHexString(b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(byte b) { return String.format("%02x", b); } }
```

## java/enum-equals

**Enum values should be compared with ==.** equals on an enum is the same check, slower and null-unsafe on one side.

Off by default: add `"java/enum-equals"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4551

Noncompliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { boolean f(Color c) { return c.equals(Color.RED); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
enum Color { RED }
class A { boolean f(Color c) { return c == Color.RED; } }
```

## java/text-block-escapes

**Escape sequences should not be used in text blocks.** A text block can hold the line break and the quote as they are.

SonarQube: S5665

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
 a\nb
 """; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
 a
 b
 """; }
```

## java/text-block-indent

**Whitespace for text block indent should be consistent.** Mixed tabs and spaces change the stripped indent unpredictably.

SonarQube: S5664

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
	a
  b
  """; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
  a
  b
  """; }
```

## java/switch-rule-break

**Switch arrow labels should not use redundant keywords.** An arrow case doesn't fall through, so break is redundant.

SonarQube: S6205

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1 -> { g(); break; } default -> h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1 -> { g(); } default -> h(); } } }
```

## java/readln-boilerplate

**Prefer IO.readln() over BufferedReader boilerplate.** Java 25's IO class reads a line without the wrappers.

SonarQube: S8450

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String line = IO.readln(); } }
```

## java/scoped-value-not-stable

**ScopedValue instances should be assigned to a stable reference.** A mutable field lets the identity of the key change.

SonarQube: S8465

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { ScopedValue<String> v = ScopedValue.newInstance(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final ScopedValue<String> V = ScopedValue.newInstance(); }
```

## java/duration-between-local

**Time zone-aware types should be used to compute durations.** A LocalDateTime difference ignores daylight-saving changes.

SonarQube: S8700

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Duration f(LocalDateTime a, LocalDateTime b) { return Duration.between(a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Duration f(Instant a, Instant b) { return Duration.between(a, b); } }
```

## java/system-default-zone

**Conversions between local and timezone-aware types should use explicit timezone handling.** The default zone differs between machines.

SonarQube: S8220

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ZoneId z = ZoneId.systemDefault(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ZoneId z = ZoneId.of("UTC"); } }
```

## java/junit-rule-field

**JUnit rules should be used.** TemporaryFolder and similar need @Rule to be set up.

SonarQube: S2924

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { TemporaryFolder folder = new TemporaryFolder(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Rule TemporaryFolder folder = new TemporaryFolder(); }
```

## java/random-test-data

**Tests should use fixed data instead of randomized data.** A test that fails on a random value can't be reproduced.

Off by default: add `"java/random-test-data"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5977

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { double d = Math.random(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { double d = 0.5; } }
```

## java/assertj-empty-consumer

**AssertJ assertions with Consumer arguments should contain assertion inside consumers.** A consumer with no assertion passes whatever the value is.

SonarQube: S6103

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(x).satisfies(v -> v.size()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertThat(x).satisfies(v -> assertThat(v).isNotEmpty()); } }
```

## java/exception-handler-status

**Set appropriate Status Codes on HTTP responses.** An @ExceptionHandler that returns a body answers 200 unless told otherwise.

SonarQube: S6863

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class) String handle(Exception e) { return "error"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class) @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST) String handle(Exception e) { return "error"; } }
```

## java/lambda-handler-client

**Reusable resources should be initialized at construction time of Lambda functions.** A client built per invocation wastes the warm start.

SonarQube: S6243

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements RequestHandler<String, String> { public String handleRequest(String in, Context c) { S3Client s = S3Client.builder().build(); return in; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements RequestHandler<String, String> { private final S3Client s = S3Client.builder().build(); public String handleRequest(String in, Context c) { return in; } }
```

## java/lambda-handler-invoke

**Lambdas should not invoke other lambdas synchronously.** The caller pays for the callee's whole run time.

SonarQube: S6246

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements RequestHandler<String, String> { public String handleRequest(String in, Context c) { lambdaClient.invoke(req); return in; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements RequestHandler<String, String> { public String handleRequest(String in, Context c) { return in; } }
```

## java/consumer-builder

**Consumer Builders should be used.** The SDK builds the request for you when you pass a lambda.

SonarQube: S6244

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s3Client.getObject(GetObjectRequest.builder().bucket("b").key("k").build()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s3Client.getObject(r -> r.bucket("b").key("k")); } }
```

## java/sensor-listener-leak

**Proper Sensor Resource Management.** A registered listener keeps the sensor on until it is unregistered.

SonarQube: S6889

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { sensorManager.registerListener(l, s, 0); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { sensorManager.registerListener(l, s, 0); } void g() { sensorManager.unregisterListener(l); } }
```

## java/location-manager-updates

**Use Fused Location to optimize battery power.** LocationManager polls providers one by one.

Off by default: add `"java/location-manager-updates"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6914

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { locationManager.requestLocationUpdates("gps", 0, 0, l); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { fusedClient.requestLocationUpdates(req, cb, looper); } }
```

## java/bluetooth-low-latency

**Bluetooth should be configured to use low power.** Low-latency scanning drains the battery.

Off by default: add `"java/bluetooth-low-latency"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6926

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int m = ScanSettings.SCAN_MODE_LOW_LATENCY; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int m = ScanSettings.SCAN_MODE_LOW_POWER; } }
```

## java/prepared-constant-in-loop

**Constant parameters in a PreparedStatement should not be set more than once.** A parameter that doesn't change can be set before the loop.

SonarQube: S6909

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ps.setString(1, "x"); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ps.setString(1, "x"); for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ps.setInt(2, i); } } }
```

## java/reverse-index-loop

**Reverse iteration should utilize reversed view.** Java 21's reversed() says what the loop does.

SonarQube: S6876

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (int i = l.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) { g(l.get(i)); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { for (String s : l.reversed()) { g(s); } } }
```

## java/virtual-thread-settings

**setDaemon, setPriority and getThreadGroup should not be invoked on virtual threads.** Virtual threads are always daemon, with a fixed priority and group.

SonarQube: S6901

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Thread.ofVirtual().unstarted(r).setDaemon(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Thread.ofVirtual().start(r); } }
```

## java/private-fields-no-constructor

**Classes and enums with private members should have a constructor.** A class that only has defaults for its private state is probably missing its setup.

Off by default: add `"java/private-fields-no-constructor"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1258

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int n; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int n; A(int n) { this.n = n; } }
```

## java/declared-too-early

**Variables should not be declared before they are relevant.** A variable declared far from its first use is hard to follow.

Off by default: add `"java/declared-too-early"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1941

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = 1; a(); b(); c(); d(); g(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { a(); b(); c(); d(); int x = 1; g(x); } }
```

## java/record-constructor-redundant

**Redundant constructors and methods should be avoided in records.** The compiler generates the canonical constructor.

SonarQube: S6207

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int x) { R(int x) { this.x = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int x) { }
```

## java/class-could-be-record

**Records should be used instead of ordinary classes when representing immutable data structures.** A record states the intent and writes the boilerplate.

Off by default: add `"java/class-could-be-record"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6206

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { private final int x; P(int x) { this.x = x; } int getX() { return x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record P(int x) { }
```

## java/indentation-inconsistent

**Source code should be indented consistently.** Statements of one block should start in the same column.

Off by default: add `"java/indentation-inconsistent"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1120

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
  a();
    b();
 } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {
  a();
  b();
 } }
```

## java/equals-asymmetric

**equals methods should be symmetric and work for subclasses.** instanceof makes a.equals(b) differ from b.equals(a) once a subclass adds state.

Off by default: add `"java/equals-asymmetric"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2162

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return o instanceof P; } }
class C extends P {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { public boolean equals(Object o) { return o != null && o.getClass() == getClass(); } }
class C extends P {}
```

## java/override-typo

**Child class methods named for parent class methods should be overrides.** Same name and arity with different types is usually a mistyped override.

SonarQube: S2177

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { void run(String s) {} }
class C extends P { void run(Object s) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { void run(String s) {} }
class C extends P { @Override void run(String s) {} }
```

## java/catch-exception-needlessly

**Exception should not be caught when not required by called methods.** Nothing in the try block can throw a checked exception.

Off by default: add `"java/catch-exception-needlessly"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2221

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int a) { try { a++; } catch (Exception e) { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { read(); } catch (IOException e) { g(); } } }
```

## java/list-search-in-loop

**Collection methods with O(n) performance should be used carefully.** contains and indexOf on a list inside a loop make it quadratic.

Off by default: add `"java/list-search-in-loop"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2250

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l, List<String> m) { for (String s : m) { if (l.contains(s)) { g(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Set<String> l, List<String> m) { for (String s : m) { if (l.contains(s)) { g(); } } } }
```

## java/override-contract

**Method overrides should not change contracts.** An override that tightens a parameter or loosens the result breaks callers of the parent.

SonarQube: S2638

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { @NonNull String f() { return "a"; } }
class C extends P { @Nullable String f() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { @NonNull String f() { return "a"; } }
class C extends P { @NonNull String f() { return "b"; } }
```

## java/concrete-parameter

**Method parameters should be declared with base types.** A concrete collection parameter ties callers to one implementation.

Off by default: add `"java/concrete-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3242

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void f(ArrayList<String> l) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public void f(List<String> l) {} }
```

## java/annotation-default-passed

**Default annotation parameter values should not be passed as arguments.** The value is already what you get by default.

Off by default: add `"java/annotation-default-passed"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3254

Noncompliant:

```java
@interface Cfg { int size() default 5; }
@Cfg(size = 5) class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
@interface Cfg { int size() default 5; }
@Cfg(size = 6) class A {}
```

## java/private-method-for-inner-class

**private methods called only by inner classes should be moved to those classes.** The outer class carries a method nothing in it uses.

SonarQube: S3398

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private void helper() {} class B { void f() { helper(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { class B { private void helper() {} void f() { helper(); } } }
```

## java/serializable-optional-field

**Value-based objects should not be serialized.** Optional is not Serializable, so the field breaks serialization.

Off by default: add `"java/serializable-optional-field"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3437

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private Optional<String> o; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private String o; }
```

## java/override-drops-synchronized

**Overrides should match their parent class methods in synchronization.** An unsynchronized override breaks the lock callers rely on.

SonarQube: S3551

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { synchronized void f() {} }
class C extends P { @Override void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { synchronized void f() {} }
class C extends P { @Override synchronized void f() {} }
```

## java/test-swallows-everything

**Unit tests should throw exceptions.** A test that catches Exception hides the failure it exists to find.

Off by default: add `"java/test-swallows-everything"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3658

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) { log(e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() throws Exception { g(); } }
```

## java/component-field-not-injected

**Members of Spring components should be injected.** A non-injected mutable field in a singleton bean is shared state.

Off by default: add `"java/component-field-not-injected"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3749

Noncompliant:

```java
@Service class A { private Helper helper = null; }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Service class A { @Autowired private Helper helper; }
```

## java/null-for-nonnull-parameter

**Nullness of parameters should be guaranteed.** Passing null to a @NonNull parameter breaks its contract.

SonarQube: S4449

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void m(@NonNull String s) {} void f() { m(null); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void m(@NonNull String s) {} void f() { m("a"); } }
```

## java/varargs-called-with-null

**Vararg method arguments should not be confusing.** A lone null can be read as the array or as one element.

SonarQube: S5669

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void m(String... a) {} void f() { m(null); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void m(String... a) {} void f() { m(); } }
```

## java/text-block-in-expression

**Text blocks should not be used in complex expressions.** A text block hides inside a longer expression.

Off by default: add `"java/text-block-in-expression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6203

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
 a
 """ + x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String t = """
 a
 """; String s = t + x; }
```

## java/record-accessor-override

**Custom getter method should not be used to override record's getter behavior.** Callers expect the accessor to return the component.

Off by default: add `"java/record-accessor-override"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6211

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int x) { public int x() { return x + 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int x) { public int next() { return x + 1; } }
```

## java/record-pattern

**Use record pattern instead of explicit field access.** A record pattern extracts the components directly.

Off by default: add `"java/record-pattern"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6878

Noncompliant:

```java
record P(int x, int y) {}
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof P p) { g(p.x(), p.y()); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record P(int x, int y) {}
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof P(int x, int y)) { g(x, y); } } }
```

## java/single-if-in-pattern-case

**Use when instead of a single if inside a pattern match body.** A guard belongs on the case.

Off by default: add `"java/single-if-in-pattern-case"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6916

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object o) { switch (o) { case String s -> { if (s.isEmpty()) { g(); } } default -> h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object o) { switch (o) { case String s when s.isEmpty() -> g(); default -> h(); } } }
```

## java/extra-comment-slashes

**Comments should start with the appropriate number of slashes.** Four slashes is a typo for two, and three is a Markdown doc comment.

Off by default: add `"java/extra-comment-slashes"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7476

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
 //// note
 int x;
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
 // note
 int x;
}
```

## java/gatherer-initializer

**Don't provide an initializer for a stateless stream gatherer.** A gatherer with no state needs no initializer.

Off by default: add `"java/gatherer-initializer"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7482

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.ofSequential(() -> null, integrator); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.ofSequential(integrator); } }
```

## java/gatherer-default-finisher

**When a defaultFinisher is passed to a Gatherer factory, use the overload that does not take a finisher.** The default finisher is what the shorter overload uses.

Off by default: add `"java/gatherer-default-finisher"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7629

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.ofSequential(init, integrator, Gatherer.defaultFinisher()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.ofSequential(init, integrator); } }
```

## java/class-builder-method-body

**ClassBuilder.withMethodBody should be preferred to ClassBuilder.withMethod.** withMethod plus withCode is what withMethodBody does.

Off by default: add `"java/class-builder-method-body"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7479

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cb.withMethod("m", desc, 1, mb -> mb.withCode(code)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cb.withMethodBody("m", desc, 1, code); } }
```

## java/import-order

**Module imports should come first and imports should be grouped by kind.** Static and regular imports interleaved are hard to scan.

Off by default: add `"java/import-order"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8445

Noncompliant:

```java
import static a.B.c;
import java.util.List;
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
import static a.B.c;
class A {}
```

## java/virtual-executor-synchronized

**Virtual threads should not run tasks that include synchronized code.** Synchronized blocks pin the carrier thread.

SonarQube: S6906

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ExecutorService e = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor(); e.submit(() -> { synchronized (this) { g(); } }); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ExecutorService e = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor(); e.submit(() -> { lock.lock(); }); } }
```

## java/gyroscope-sensor

**Motion Sensor should not use gyroscope.** The gyroscope costs far more battery than the accelerometer.

Off by default: add `"java/gyroscope-sensor"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6923

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Sensor s = sm.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Sensor s = sm.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER); } }
```

## java/switch-to-expression

**Use Java 14 switch expression.** Every branch assigns the same variable.

Off by default: add `"java/switch-to-expression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5194

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { int r; switch (x) { case 1: r = 1; break; case 2: r = 2; break; default: r = 0; break; } return r; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(int x) { return switch (x) { case 1 -> 1; case 2 -> 2; default -> 0; }; } }
```

## java/one-line-text-block

**Simple string literal should be used for single line strings.** A text block of one line is a string.

Off by default: add `"java/one-line-text-block"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5663

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = """
 a"""; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String s = "a"; }
```

## java/saml-comments

**OpenSAML2 should be configured to prevent authentication bypass.** A comment inside a NameID can change which user the parser reads.

SonarQube: S5679

Standards: [CWE-287](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/287.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BasicParserPool p = new BasicParserPool(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BasicParserPool p = new BasicParserPool(); p.setIgnoreComments(true); } }
```

## java/brain-method

**Methods should not perform too many tasks.** A long, branching, deeply nested method does several jobs at once.

SonarQube: S6541

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int a) {
```
