```java
 if (a == 1) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 2) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 3) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 4) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 5) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 6) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 7) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 8) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 9) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 10) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 11) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 12) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 13) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 14) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 15) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 16) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 17) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 18) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 19) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 20) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 21) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 22) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 23) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 24) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 25) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 26) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 27) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
 if (a == 28) { if (a > 1) { if (a > 2) { g(); } } }
```

## java/inner-class-super-call

**Inner class calls to super class methods should be unambiguous.** A call can mean the inner class's parent or the outer class's method.

SonarQube: S2388

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { void go() {} }
class O { void go() {} class N extends P { void f() { go(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { void go() {} }
class O { void go() {} class N extends P { void f() { super.go(); } } }
```

## java/irregular-number-pattern

**Number patterns should be regular.** Groups of different sizes in a DecimalFormat pattern are almost always a typo.

Off by default: add `"java/irregular-number-pattern"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3937

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DecimalFormat f = new DecimalFormat("#,##,###"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DecimalFormat f = new DecimalFormat("#,###,###"); } }
```

## java/bean-populated-from-request

**JavaBean properties should not be populated from untrusted input.** Copying request parameters onto a bean lets a caller set any property.

SonarQube: S4512

Standards: [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A03:2021 Injection, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BeanUtils.populate(bean, request.getParameterMap()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { bean.setName(request.getParameter("name")); } }
```

## java/serial-version-blind

**serialVersionUID should not be declared blindly.** 1L and 0L are copied from templates and never change when the class does.

Off by default: add `"java/serial-version-blind"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4926

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private static final long serialVersionUID = 4817263517L; }
```

## java/catch-hidden-by-parent

**Derived exceptions should not hide their parents' catch blocks.** A catch for a subclass after its parent can never run.

SonarQube: S4970

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) { } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { } catch (Exception e) { } } }
```

## java/bean-validation-misplaced

**Bean Validation (JSR 380) should be properly configured.** A constraint on a type it can't validate is silently ignored.

Off by default: add `"java/bean-validation-misplaced"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5128

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @NotBlank int age; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @NotNull Integer age; }
```

## java/user-enumeration

**Authentication mechanisms should not permit user enumeration.** Telling a caller whether the user exists helps guessing logins.

SonarQube: S5804

Standards: [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html), [CWE-203](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/203.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { provider.setHideUserNotFoundExceptions(false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { provider.setHideUserNotFoundExceptions(true); } }
```

## java/permit-all

**Authorizations should be based on strong decisions.** permitAll on every request is not an authorization decision.

SonarQube: S5808

Standards: [CWE-285](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/285.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.authorizeRequests().anyRequest().permitAll(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.authorizeRequests().anyRequest().authenticated(); } }
```

## java/named-group-by-index

**Names of regular expressions named groups should be used.** A named group read by number defeats the name.

SonarQube: S5860

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(String s) { Matcher m = Pattern.compile("(?<year>\\d+)").matcher(s); return m.group(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(String s) { Matcher m = Pattern.compile("(?<year>\\d+)").matcher(s); return m.group("year"); } }
```

## java/model-and-view-assertions

**Spring's ModelAndViewAssert assertions should be used instead of other assertions.** They give a better failure message.

Off by default: add `"java/model-and-view-assertions"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S5970

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void t() { assertEquals("home", mav.getViewName()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void t() { ModelAndViewAssert.assertViewName(mav, "home"); } }
```

## java/similar-tests

**Similar tests should be grouped in a single Parameterized test.** Tests that differ only in their literals are one test run with several inputs.

SonarQube: S5976

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void a() { check(1); verify(2); } @Test void b() { check(3); verify(4); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Test void a() { check(1); verify(2); } @Test void b() { other(); verify(4); } }
```

## java/record-reflection

**Reflection should not be used to increase accessibility of records' fields.** Record fields are final, and setAccessible cannot make them writable.

SonarQube: S6216

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int x) {}
class A { void f() throws Exception { R.class.getDeclaredField("x").setAccessible(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int x) {}
class A { int f(R r) { return r.x(); } }
```

## java/nullable-injection

**Nullable injected fields and parameters should provide a default value.** An optional dependency that is absent leaves the field null.

SonarQube: S6816

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired(required = false) private Helper h; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired(required = false) private Helper h = new Helper(); }
```

## java/scoped-bean-in-singleton

**Non-singleton Spring beans should not be injected into singleton beans.** The singleton holds the first instance forever.

SonarQube: S6832

Noncompliant:

```java
@Scope("prototype") @Component class P {}
@Service class S { @Autowired P p; }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Scope("prototype") @Component class P {}
@Service class S { @Autowired ObjectProvider<P> p; }
```

## java/reverse-copy

**Reverse view should be used instead of reverse copy in read-only cases.** Java 21's reversed() needs no copy.

Off by default: add `"java/reverse-copy"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6877

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { List<String> r = new ArrayList<>(l); Collections.reverse(r); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { var r = l.reversed(); } }
```

## java/blocking-in-platform-pool

**Virtual threads should be used for tasks that include heavy blocking operations.** A fixed pool of platform threads is exhausted by sleeping tasks.

SonarQube: S6881

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ExecutorService e = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4); e.submit(() -> { Thread.sleep(1000); }); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ExecutorService e = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor(); e.submit(() -> { Thread.sleep(1000); }); } }
```

## java/high-frame-rate

**High frame rates should not be used.** A high frame rate drains the battery for little gain.

Off by default: add `"java/high-frame-rate"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6898

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { surface.setFrameRate(120f, 0); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { surface.setFrameRate(60f, 0); } }
```

## java/dirties-context-marker

**Use appropriate @DirtiesContext modes.** A bare @DirtiesContext rebuilds the context after every class.

SonarQube: S7177

Noncompliant:

```java
@DirtiesContext class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
@DirtiesContext(classMode = DirtiesContext.ClassMode.AFTER_CLASS) class A {}
```

## java/gatherer-default-combiner

**Gatherer.ofSequential() should be used to build sequential gathers.** A default combiner means the gatherer is sequential.

Off by default: add `"java/gatherer-default-combiner"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7481

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.of(init, integrator, Gatherer.defaultCombiner(), finisher); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Object g = Gatherer.ofSequential(init, integrator, finisher); } }
```

## java/markdown-comment-html

**Markdown, HTML and Javadoc tags should be consistent.** A /// comment is Markdown: HTML and Javadoc tags inside it are not rendered.

Off by default: add `"java/markdown-comment-html"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7474

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
 /// Returns <b>the</b> value.
 int f() { return 1; }
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
 /// Returns **the** value.
 int f() { return 1; }
}
```

## java/print-then-readln

**Prefer IO.readln(String prompt) rather than IO.print followed by IO.readln().** The prompt can be passed to readln.

Off by default: add `"java/print-then-readln"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8469

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { IO.print("name: "); String s = IO.readln(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = IO.readln("name: "); } }
```

## java/unnamed-needs-var

**Unnamed variable declarations should use the var identifier.** `int _` and `var _` declare the same thing; var says less.

Off by default: add `"java/unnamed-needs-var"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7466

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int _ = g(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { var _ = g(); } }
```

## java/record-pattern-unused-component

**Types of unused record components should be removed from pattern matching.** A component the body never reads needs no name or type.

Off by default: add `"java/record-pattern-unused-component"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7475

Noncompliant:

```java
record P(int x, int y) {}
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof P(int x, int y)) { g(x); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
record P(int x, int y) {}
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof P(int x, int _)) { g(x); } } }
```

## java/constructor-prologue-long

**Excessive logic before super() should not bloat constructor.** Java 22 allows statements before super(), but a few lines is the limit.

Off by default: add `"java/constructor-prologue-long"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8444

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends B { A(int x) { int a = 1; int b = 2; int c = 3; int d = 4; super(a); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends B { A(int x) { super(x); } }
```

## java/validation-after-super

**Validation logic should be placed in constructor prologue when possible.** Checking an argument after super() lets the parent run on bad input.

Off by default: add `"java/validation-after-super"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8433

Noncompliant:

```java
class A extends B { A(String s) { super(s); Objects.requireNonNull(s); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A extends B { A(String s) { super(Objects.requireNonNull(s)); } }
```

## java/fields-set-after-super

**Subclass fields should be initialized before calls to super() or this().** The parent constructor can call an override that reads a field that is still null.

Off by default: add `"java/fields-set-after-super"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S8447

Noncompliant:

```java
class P { P() { init(); } void init() {} }
class C extends P { String name; C(String n) { super(); this.name = n; } void init() { name.length(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class P { P() { init(); } void init() {} }
class C extends P { String name; C(String n) { super(); } void init() { } }
```

## java/class-transform-name

**Class name should be omitted when unchanged by class transform.** The overload without a name keeps the class name.

Off by default: add `"java/class-transform-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7477

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cf.transformClass(model, model.thisClass().asSymbol(), t); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cf.transformClass(model, t); } }
```

## java/class-build-for-transform

**transformClass method should be used instead of build when transforming a class.** build plus a manual transform is what transformClass does.

Off by default: add `"java/class-build-for-transform"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7478

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cf.build(desc, cb -> cb.transform(model, t)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cf.transformClass(model, t); } }
```

## java/assertj-global-config

**AssertJ configuration should be applied.** Global settings changed inside a test leak into every test that runs after it.

SonarQube: S5831

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Test void t() { Assertions.setMaxLengthForSingleLineDescription(500); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @BeforeAll static void setup() { Assertions.setMaxLengthForSingleLineDescription(500); } }
```

## java/mock-of-everything

**Mocking all non-private methods of a class should be avoided.** A class whose every method is stubbed is an interface in disguise.

SonarQube: S5969

Noncompliant:

```java
class Svc { int a() { return 1; } int b() { return 2; } }
class T { @Test void t() { Svc m = mock(Svc.class); when(m.a()).thenReturn(1); when(m.b()).thenReturn(2); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class Svc { int a() { return 1; } int b() { return 2; } }
class T { @Test void t() { Svc m = mock(Svc.class); when(m.a()).thenReturn(1); } }
```

## c/no-gets

**gets() should never be used.** gets() cannot limit input size and always allows a buffer overflow.

Standards: [CWE-120](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/120.html), [CWE-242](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/242.html)

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(char *b) { gets(b); }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(char *b, int n) { fgets(b, n, stdin); }
```

## c/unsafe-string-function

**Unbounded string functions should not be used.** strcpy, strcat, sprintf and vsprintf do not check the destination size.

Standards: [CWE-120](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/120.html), [CWE-676](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/676.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(char *d, const char *s) { strcpy(d, s); }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(char *d, const char *s, int n) { snprintf(d, n, "%s", s); }
```

## c/assignment-in-condition

**Assignments should not be used as conditions.** `if (x = y)` is almost always a typo for `==`. Use double parentheses if intended.

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(int x) { if (x = 5) { g(); } }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(int x) { if (x == 5) { g(); } if ((x = h())) { g(); } }
```

## c/goto

**goto should not be used.** goto makes control flow hard to follow.

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(void) { goto end; end: return; }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(void) { return; }
```

## cs/self-assignment

**Variables should not be assigned to themselves.** Assigning a variable to itself does nothing; it usually means the wrong name was typed.

SonarQube: S1656

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F() { x = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F(int x) { this.x = x; } }
```

## cs/lost-increment

**Increments and decrements should not be overwritten.** In `i = i++` the increment happens, then the old value is assigned back, so `i` does not change.

SonarQube: S2123

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int i) { i = i++; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int i) { i++; } }
```

## cs/reversed-operator

**Assignment operators should not be reversed.** `x =+ 1` assigns `+1` to `x`; `x += 1` was almost certainly meant. The same goes for `=-` and `=!`.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { x =+ 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { x += 1; } }
```

## cs/double-unary

**'!' and '~' should not be doubled.** `!!x` is `x` and `~~x` is `x`: the doubled operator does nothing, or another operator was meant.

SonarQube: S2761

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return !!b; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return b; } }
```

## cs/for-loop-wrong-direction

**A for loop's update should move its counter towards the end condition.** A counter compared with `<` but decremented (or with `>` but incremented) never reaches the end, so the loop runs until the value wraps around.

SonarQube: S2251

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i--) { G(i); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { G(i); } } }
```

## cs/collection-size-sign

**Collection sizes should not be compared with negative bounds.** `Count`, `Length` and `Count()` are never negative, so `>= 0` is always true and `< 0` always false.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int[] a) { return a.Length >= 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int[] a) { return a.Length > 0; } }
```

## cs/async-void

**Async methods should not return void.** Exceptions from an `async void` method can't be caught by the caller and crash the process, and the caller can't await it. Event handlers are the exception, since their signature is fixed.

SonarQube: S3168

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async void Load() { await Task.Delay(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task Load() { await Task.Delay(1); } void OnClick(object sender, EventArgs e) { } async void OnLoad(object sender, EventArgs e) { await Load(); } }
```

## cs/accessor-ignores-value

**Set, init, add and remove accessors should use 'value'.** An accessor that never reads `value` silently drops what the caller passed in.

SonarQube: S3237

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; int X { get { return x; } set { x = 42; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; int X { get { return x; } set { x = value; } } int Y { set => throw new NotSupportedException(); } }
```

## cs/destructor-throws

**Finalizers should not throw exceptions.** An exception thrown from a finalizer terminates the process on .NET, unless it is caught.

SonarQube: S1048

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { throw new InvalidOperationException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { Release(); } }
```

## cs/to-string-returns-null

**ToString() should not return null.** Callers expect `ToString()` to return text; `null` makes string formatting and concatenation fail later.

SonarQube: S2225

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { return ""; } }
```

## cs/identical-branches

**Every branch of a conditional should not do the same thing.** When the `if` and every `else`, or both sides of `?:`, are identical, the condition is pointless, or one branch has the wrong code.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(bool b) { return b ? 1 : 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(bool b) { return b ? 1 : 2; } }
```

## cs/anonymous-unsubscribe

**Anonymous functions should not be used to unsubscribe from events.** Each lambda or anonymous method is a new delegate, so `-=` with one removes nothing and the original handler stays subscribed.

SonarQube: S3244

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Changed -= (s, e) => G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Changed -= OnChanged; } }
```

## cs/duplicate-condition

**Conditions in an if/else if chain should not repeat.** A condition already tested earlier in the chain never selects its branch, so that code is dead.

SonarQube: S1862

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) { G(); } else if (a == 1) { H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) { G(); } else if (a == 2) { H(); } } }
```

## cs/identical-operands

**Identical expressions should not be used on both sides of a binary operator.** `a == a` is always true and `a - a` always zero: one side is probably the wrong expression.

SonarQube: S1764

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a) { return a == a; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return a == b; } }
```

## cs/empty-block

**Nested blocks should not be left empty.** An empty `if`, loop, `try`, `catch` or `finally` block is either unfinished or unneeded. A comment explaining why it is empty is enough.

SonarQube: S108

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { /* already logged by G */ } } }
```

## cs/empty-method

**Methods should not be empty.** An empty method is unfinished or unneeded. If it is empty on purpose, say why in a comment, or throw `NotSupportedException` when callers shouldn't use it.

SonarQube: S1186

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Reset() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Reset() { /* stateless: nothing to reset */ } public virtual void OnStart() { } }
```

## cs/rethrow-loses-stack

**Exceptions should be rethrown with 'throw;'.** `throw e;` restarts the exception's stack trace at this line, losing where it was first thrown.

SonarQube: S3445

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); throw e; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); throw; } } }
```

## cs/useless-catch

**Catch clauses should do more than rethrow.** A `catch` that only rethrows changes nothing; without it the exception propagates the same way.

SonarQube: S2737

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException) { throw; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException) { throw; } catch (Exception e) { Log(e); } } }
```

## cs/general-exception

**General or reserved exceptions should not be thrown.** Callers can't catch `Exception` or `ApplicationException` without catching everything else too, and the runtime reserves `NullReferenceException`, `IndexOutOfRangeException` and `OutOfMemoryException` for itself.

SonarQube: S112

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new Exception("failed"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new InvalidOperationException("failed"); } }
```

## cs/obsolete-without-explanation

**[Obsolete] should say what to use instead.** Callers warned that something is obsolete need to know what replaces it.

SonarQube: S1123

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [Obsolete] void F() { G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [Obsolete("Use G instead.")] void F() { G(); } }
```

## cs/nested-ternary

**Ternary operators should not be nested.** A `?:` inside another is hard to read; an `if` chain or a `switch` expression is clearer.

SonarQube: S3358

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return a > 0 ? 1 : a < 0 ? -1 : 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return Math.Sign(a); } }
```

## cs/inverted-comparison

**Boolean checks should not be inverted.** `!(a == b)` reads worse than `a != b`, which says the same thing.

SonarQube: S1940

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return !(a == b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return a != b; } }
```

## cs/count-instead-of-any

**Any() should be used to test whether a sequence is empty.** `Count()` may enumerate the whole sequence to answer a yes-or-no question that `Any()` answers at the first element.

SonarQube: S1155

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(IEnumerable<int> xs) { return xs.Count() > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(IEnumerable<int> xs) { return xs.Any(); } }
```

## cs/gc-collect

**GC.Collect() should not be called.** Forcing a garbage collection pauses the application and usually makes overall performance worse than the runtime's own scheduling.

SonarQube: S1215

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { GC.Collect(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { GC.KeepAlive(this); } }
```

## cs/visible-mutable-static-field

**Static fields visible outside their class should be constant or read-only.** Any code can change a public, protected or internal static field, so its value can't be relied on, and changes race between threads.

SonarQube: S2223

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public static int Limit = 10; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public static readonly int Limit = 10; private static int count; }
```

## cs/redundant-base-type

**Inheritance lists should not name the default base type.** Every class already derives from `object` and every enum is already backed by `int`.

SonarQube: S1939

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum Color : int { Red, Green }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Color : byte { Red, Green }
```

## cs/empty-statement

**Empty statements should be removed.** A stray `;` does nothing, and can hide a mistake such as a semicolon after an `if`.

SonarQube: S1116

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G();; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); while (Next()) ; } }
```

## cs/redundant-jump

**Redundant return and continue statements should be removed.** A `return;` at the end of a method, or a `continue;` at the end of a loop, does what would happen anyway.

SonarQube: S3626

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); return; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) { if (b) { return; } G(); } }
```

## cs/protected-in-sealed

**Sealed classes should not declare protected members.** A sealed class has no subclasses, so `protected` means `private` there and misleads readers.

Off by default: add `"cs/protected-in-sealed"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2156

Noncompliant:

```csharp
sealed class A { protected int count; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class A : B { private int count; protected override void F() { } }
```

## cs/abstract-class-public-constructor

**Abstract classes should not have public constructors.** Only subclasses can call an abstract class's constructor, so `public` promises more than it gives.

SonarQube: S3442

Noncompliant:

```csharp
abstract class A { public A() { Init(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
abstract class A { protected A() { Init(); } }
```

## cs/redundant-constructor

**Redundant constructors and finalizers should be removed.** An empty public parameterless constructor, when it is the class's only constructor, is what the compiler generates anyway. An empty finalizer only slows garbage collection.

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-constructor"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3253

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { } public A(int x) { X = x; } int X; }
```

## cs/mergeable-if

**Nested if statements should be merged.** An `if` whose only statement is another `if`, neither with an `else`, reads more simply as one condition joined with `&&`.

SonarQube: S1066

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a) { if (b) { G(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a && b) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/empty-string-comparison

**string.IsNullOrEmpty() should be used to test for empty strings.** `s == ""` misses `null`, which usually needs the same handling; `string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)` covers both and says what is meant.

Off by default: add `"cs/empty-string-comparison"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3256

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s == ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return string.IsNullOrEmpty(s); } }
```

## cs/enum-name-suffix

**Enum names should not end with 'Enum' or 'Flags'.** The suffix repeats what the declaration already says.

SonarQube: S2344

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum ColorEnum { Red, Green }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Color { Red, Green }
```

## cs/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD5, SHA-1 and RIPEMD-160 are broken for security purposes: collisions can be computed.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(byte[] d) { return MD5.Create().ComputeHash(d); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(byte[] d) { return SHA256.Create().ComputeHash(d); } }
```

## cs/weak-cipher

**Weak cipher algorithms should not be used.** DES, 3DES and RC2 use keys or blocks small enough to break in practice.

SonarQube: S5547

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { SymmetricAlgorithm F() { return DES.Create(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { SymmetricAlgorithm F() { return Aes.Create(); } }
```

## cs/insecure-cipher-mode

**Encryption should use a secure mode and padding.** ECB mode encrypts equal blocks to equal ciphertext, which leaks patterns, and RSA with PKCS#1 v1.5 padding is open to padding-oracle attacks.

SonarQube: S5542

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Aes aes) { aes.Mode = CipherMode.ECB; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(RSA rsa, byte[] d) { rsa.Encrypt(d, RSAEncryptionPadding.OaepSHA256); } }
```

## cs/weak-tls-version

**Weak SSL/TLS protocol versions should not be used.** SSL 2, SSL 3, TLS 1.0 and TLS 1.1 have known weaknesses. Leave the choice to the operating system, or require TLS 1.2 or later.

SonarQube: S4423

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls12; } }
```

## cs/weak-key-size

**RSA and DSA keys should be at least 2048 bits long.** Keys shorter than 2048 bits can be factored with today's computing power.

SonarQube: S4426

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { RSA F() { return new RSACryptoServiceProvider(1024); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { RSA F() { return RSA.Create(3072); } }
```

## cs/certificate-validation-disabled

**Server certificates should be validated.** A certificate validation callback that always returns `true` accepts any certificate, so anyone on the network path can impersonate the server.

SonarQube: S4830

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpClientHandler h) { h.ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = (m, c, ch, e) => true; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpClientHandler h) { h.ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = (m, c, ch, e) => e == SslPolicyErrors.None; } }
```

## cs/ldap-anonymous

**LDAP connections should be authenticated.** An anonymous or unauthenticated LDAP bind lets anyone who can reach the directory read it, and sometimes change it.

SonarQube: S4433

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(DirectoryEntry d) { d.AuthenticationType = AuthenticationTypes.None; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(DirectoryEntry d) { d.AuthenticationType = AuthenticationTypes.Secure; } }
```

## cs/empty-db-password

**Database connection strings should not have an empty password.** A database reachable with an empty password is open to anyone who can connect to it.

SonarQube: S2115

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Db = "Server=db;Database=app;User Id=app;Password=;"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Db = "Server=db;Database=app;Integrated Security=true;Password=;"; }
```

## cs/cleartext-url

**Clear-text protocols should not be used.** Data sent over http, ftp or telnet can be read and changed by anyone on the network path.

SonarQube: S5332 (related)

Standards: [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Api = "http://api.payments.com/v1"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Api = "https://api.payments.com/v1"; const string Ns = "http://tempuri.org/"; }
```

## cs/hardcoded-credential

**Credentials should not be hard-coded.** A password or secret in source code is readable by everyone with the code, ends up in version history, and can't be rotated without a release.

SonarQube: S2068 (related), S6418 (related)

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string ApiKey = "k3y-8f2a9c"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string PasswordLabel = "Password"; string Key(IConfiguration c) => c["ApiKey"]; }
```

## cs/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be built from strings.** Formatting or concatenating values into a query lets an attacker change what it does; pass them as parameters.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(SqlConnection c, string id) { var cmd = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = " + id, c); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(SqlConnection c, string id) { var cmd = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = @id", c); } }
```

## cs/cookie-not-secure

**Cookies should be sent over HTTPS only.** A cookie without `Secure` is also sent over plain HTTP, where anyone on the network can read it.

SonarQube: S2092

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), [CWE-315](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/315.html), [CWE-614](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/614.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { CookieOptions F() { return new CookieOptions { Secure = false }; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { CookieOptions F() { return new CookieOptions { Secure = true }; } }
```

## cs/cookie-not-httponly

**Cookies should not be readable from scripts.** Without `HttpOnly`, any script on the page, an injected one included, can read the cookie.

SonarQube: S3330

Standards: [CWE-1004](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1004.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpCookie c) { c.HttpOnly = false; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpCookie c) { c.HttpOnly = true; } }
```

## cs/custom-crypto

**Cryptographic algorithms should not be implemented by hand.** Home-made cryptography is almost always weaker than the standard algorithms, which have been studied for years.

SonarQube: S2257

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class MyHash : HashAlgorithm { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Hasher { byte[] F(byte[] d) => SHA256.HashData(d); }
```

## cs/xxe

**XML parsers should not resolve external entities.** Processing DTDs or resolving external resources lets a crafted document read local files or make requests from the server (XXE).

SonarQube: S2755

Standards: [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html), [CWE-827](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/827.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { XmlReaderSettings F() { return new XmlReaderSettings { DtdProcessing = DtdProcessing.Parse }; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { XmlReaderSettings F() { return new XmlReaderSettings { DtdProcessing = DtdProcessing.Prohibit }; } }
```

## cs/os-command-path

**Commands should be started by their full path.** A bare command name is looked up in the PATH, so whoever controls a directory in it chooses the program that runs.

SonarQube: S4036

Standards: [CWE-426](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/426.html), [CWE-427](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/427.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Process.Start("git", "status"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Process.Start("/usr/bin/git", "status"); } }
```

## cs/csrf-disabled

**CSRF protection should not be disabled.** Without an antiforgery token, another site can make a signed-in user's browser submit requests to this one.

SonarQube: S4502

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [HttpPost, IgnoreAntiforgeryToken] public IActionResult Save() => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [HttpPost, ValidateAntiForgeryToken] public IActionResult Save() => Ok(); }
```

## cs/debug-features

**Developer error pages should only be enabled in development.** The developer exception page shows stack traces, source and configuration to whoever triggers an error.

SonarQube: S4507

Standards: [CWE-215](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/215.html), [CWE-489](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/489.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A10:2025 Mishandling of Exceptional Conditions, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class S { void Configure(IApplicationBuilder app) { app.UseDeveloperExceptionPage(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class S { void Configure(IApplicationBuilder app, IWebHostEnvironment env) { if (env.IsDevelopment()) { app.UseDeveloperExceptionPage(); } } }
```

## cs/cors-any-origin

**CORS should not allow every origin.** Allowing any origin lets every website read this API's responses with the visitor's session, where the browser would otherwise block them.

SonarQube: S5122

Standards: [CWE-346](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/346.html), [CWE-942](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/942.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class S { void F(CorsPolicyBuilder p) { p.AllowAnyOrigin(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class S { void F(CorsPolicyBuilder p) { p.WithOrigins("https://shop.example.com"); } }
```

## cs/weak-password-hashing

**Passwords should be hashed with enough iterations.** PBKDF2 with few iterations (the old default is 1,000) lets an attacker try billions of passwords against a stolen hash.

SonarQube: S5344

Standards: [CWE-256](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/256.html), [CWE-916](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/916.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(string p, byte[] s) { return new Rfc2898DeriveBytes(p, s).GetBytes(32); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(string p, byte[] s) { return Rfc2898DeriveBytes.Pbkdf2(p, s, 600_000, HashAlgorithmName.SHA256, 32); } }
```

## cs/public-tmp

**Files should not be created in shared temporary directories by name.** Other users can read files in `/tmp`, or create the same name first and have this program write into their file.

SonarQube: S5443

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { File.WriteAllText("/tmp/report.txt", "x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string dir) { File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(dir, "report.txt"), "x"); } }
```

## cs/create-temp-file

**Path.GetTempFileName() should not be used.** It creates a predictable name in the shared temporary directory, and throws once 65,535 such files exist.

SonarQube: S5445

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F() { return Path.GetTempFileName(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string dir) { return Path.Combine(dir, Path.GetRandomFileName()); } }
```

## cs/jwt-unverified

**JWT signatures should be verified.** Accepting unsigned or unverified tokens lets anyone forge one with whatever claims they want.

SonarQube: S5659

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { TokenValidationParameters F() { return new TokenValidationParameters { RequireSignedTokens = false }; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { TokenValidationParameters F() { return new TokenValidationParameters { RequireSignedTokens = true }; } }
```

## cs/request-size-unlimited

**Request size limits should not be too large.** Very large or unlimited request bodies let one client exhaust the server's memory or disk.

SonarQube: S5693

Standards: [CWE-400](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/400.html), [CWE-770](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/770.html)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [DisableRequestSizeLimit] public IActionResult Upload() => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [RequestSizeLimit(8_000_000)] public IActionResult Upload() => Ok(); }
```

## cs/request-validation-disabled

**ASP.NET request validation should not be disabled.** Request validation rejects input that looks like markup or script; turning it off opens the way to cross-site scripting.

SonarQube: S5753

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [ValidateInput(false)] public ActionResult Save(string html) => View(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { public ActionResult Save(string html) => View(); }
```

## cs/unsafe-code

**Unsafe code should be reviewed.** `unsafe` code works with raw pointers, outside the runtime's memory safety: an error there can corrupt memory or expose it.

SonarQube: S6640

Standards: [CWE-119](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/119.html), [CWE-787](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/787.html)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { unsafe void F(byte* p) { *p = 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Span<byte> p) { p[0] = 0; } }
```

## cs/equals-without-hashcode

**Equals(object) and GetHashCode() should be overridden together.** Objects that are equal must have equal hash codes, or dictionaries and hash sets lose them. Overriding only one of the two breaks that.

SonarQube: S1206

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; public override int GetHashCode() => id; }
```

## cs/single-iteration-loop

**Loops should be able to run more than once.** A loop whose body always ends with `break`, `return` or `throw`, and never continues, runs at most once: it's an `if` in disguise, or the jump is a mistake.

SonarQube: S1751

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] xs) { foreach (var x in xs) { return x; } return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] xs) { foreach (var x in xs) { if (x > 0) { return x; } } return 0; } }
```

## cs/unused-new-object

**Objects should not be created only to be dropped.** An object created and never used costs an allocation for nothing. If the constructor is run for its side effects, a method says that better.

SonarQube: S1848

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { new Report(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { var r = new Report(); r.Save(); } }
```

## cs/collection-own-argument

**A collection should not be passed to its own methods.** `list.AddRange(list)` doubles the list, `xs.Union(xs)` and `xs.Intersect(xs)` give back `xs`, and `xs.SequenceEqual(xs)` is always true. Another collection was probably meant.

SonarQube: S2114

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<int> xs) { xs.AddRange(xs); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<int> xs, List<int> ys) { xs.AddRange(ys); } }
```

## cs/self-referencing-property

**Properties should not read or write themselves.** A getter that returns its own property, or a setter that assigns it, calls itself forever and ends in a StackOverflowException, which can't be caught.

SonarQube: S2190 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int count; public int Count { get { return Count; } set { count = value; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int count; public int Count { get { return count; } set { count = value; } } }
```

## cs/for-loop-never-runs

**A for loop's condition should be true at the start.** When the counter starts past the end condition, the loop body never runs.

SonarQube: S2252

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { for (int i = 10; i < 5; i++) { G(i); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { G(i); } } }
```

## cs/format-string-arguments

**Composite format strings should match their arguments.** A placeholder such as `{2}` with fewer than three arguments, or an unbalanced brace, makes `string.Format` throw a FormatException at run time.

SonarQube: S2275

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int a, int b) { return string.Format("{0} of {1} ({2})", a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int a, int b) { return string.Format("{0} of {1}, {{done}}", a, b); } }
```

## cs/flags-enum-values

**[Flags] enum members should be powers of two.** Flags are combined bit by bit. A member that isn't 0 or a power of two, including one given the next number implicitly, overlaps others, so `HasFlag` gives wrong answers.

SonarQube: S2345, S4070 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Access { None = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2, Execute = 3 }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Access { None = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2, Execute = 4, All = Read | Write | Execute }
```

## cs/lock-on-shared-object

**Locks should not be taken on publicly reachable objects.** `this`, `typeof(...)` and string literals can be locked by code outside the class, which can deadlock with it.

SonarQube: S2551

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { lock (this) { G(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private readonly object sync = new object(); void F() { lock (sync) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/nan-comparison

**Values should not be compared with NaN.** NaN is not equal to anything, itself included, so `x == double.NaN` is always false.

SonarQube: S2688

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(double d) { return d == double.NaN; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(double d) { return double.IsNaN(d); } }
```

## cs/thread-static-initializer

**[ThreadStatic] fields should not be initialized inline.** The initializer runs once, on the thread that loads the class; every other thread sees the field's default value.

SonarQube: S2996

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [ThreadStatic] static int depth = 1; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [ThreadStatic] static int depth; }
```

## cs/thread-static-instance-field

**[ThreadStatic] should only be used on static fields.** The attribute does nothing on an instance field, so the field is shared between threads after all.

SonarQube: S3005

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [ThreadStatic] int depth; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [ThreadStatic] static int depth; }
```

## cs/returned-disposed

**Objects disposed by 'using' should not be returned.** `using` disposes the object when the block ends, so the caller gets an object that is already disposed.

SonarQube: S2997

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Stream F(string p) { using (var s = File.OpenRead(p)) { return s; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Stream F(string p) { var s = File.OpenRead(p); return s; } }
```

## cs/base-object-equality

**base.Equals() and base.GetHashCode() should not be used when the base is object.** In a class that derives straight from `object`, `base.Equals` compares references and `base.GetHashCode` hashes the reference, which undoes overriding them.

SonarQube: S3249, S3397

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public override int GetHashCode() { return base.GetHashCode(); } public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public override int GetHashCode() { return id; } public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; }
```

## cs/static-field-order

**Static fields should not be initialized from fields declared after them.** Static initializers run in declaration order, so a field initialized from one declared later reads that one's default value.

SonarQube: S3263

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { static int Twice = Once * 2; static int Once = 21; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { static int Once = 21; static int Twice = Once * 2; }
```

## cs/caller-info-parameter-position

**Caller information parameters should come last.** `[CallerMemberName]` and its siblings only work when the caller leaves the argument out. A later parameter makes callers pass it, which overrides the real value.

SonarQube: S3343

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log([CallerMemberName] string caller = "", string message = "") { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(string message, [CallerMemberName] string caller = "") { } }
```

## cs/one-way-returns-value

**One-way service operations should not return a value.** The client doesn't wait for a one-way operation, so a return value never reaches it and WCF rejects the contract.

SonarQube: S3598

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface S { [OperationContract(IsOneWay = true)] int Send(string m); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface S { [OperationContract(IsOneWay = true)] void Send(string m); }
```

## cs/pure-void-method

**[Pure] should not mark void methods.** A pure method has no side effects, so a void one does nothing. Callers and analyzers that trust the attribute will drop calls to it.

SonarQube: S3603

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [Pure] void Check(int x) { G(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [Pure] bool Check(int x) { return x > 0; } }
```

## cs/dangerous-get-handle

**SafeHandle.DangerousGetHandle() should not be called.** The raw handle can be released and recycled while it's still in use, so the code may end up using another resource's handle.

SonarQube: S3869

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { IntPtr F(SafeFileHandle h) { return h.DangerousGetHandle(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { SafeFileHandle F(SafeFileHandle h) { return h; } }
```

## cs/type-outside-namespace

**Types should be declared in a namespace.** Types in the global namespace collide with every other library that does the same.

SonarQube: S3903

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Helper { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
namespace Shop; class Helper { }
```

## cs/serialization-callback-signature

**Serialization callbacks should have the expected signature.** The serializer only calls `[OnSerializing]` and the other callbacks when they are non-public, return void and take one StreamingContext; otherwise it throws or skips them.

SonarQube: S3927

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [OnDeserialized] public void Restore() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [OnDeserialized] private void Restore(StreamingContext context) { Init(); } }
```

## cs/exception-not-thrown

**Exceptions should not be created without being thrown.** Creating an exception does nothing until it's thrown; the `throw` was probably forgotten.

SonarQube: S3984

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { if (x < 0) { new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(x)); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { if (x < 0) { throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(x)); } } }
```

## cs/overwritten-element

**Collection elements should not be overwritten right away.** Writing the same index or key twice in a row discards the first value; a different index was probably meant.

SonarQube: S4143

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int[] a) { a[0] = 1; a[0] = 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int[] a) { a[0] = 1; a[1] = 2; } }
```

## cs/task-returns-null

**Methods returning Task should not return null.** Callers await the result, and awaiting null throws a NullReferenceException.

SonarQube: S4586

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Task Save() { if (done) { return null; } return Write(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Task Save() { if (done) { return Task.CompletedTask; } return Write(); } }
```

## cs/only-private-constructors

**Classes should not have only private constructors and no way to create them.** With every constructor private and no static member or nested type to call one, nothing can create the class.

SonarQube: S3453

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Config { private Config() { } public int Port { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Config { private Config() { } public static Config Default { get; } = new Config(); }
```

## cs/to-char-array-in-foreach

**Strings should be iterated directly, not through ToCharArray().** A string is already a sequence of characters; `ToCharArray()` copies it for nothing.

SonarQube: S3456

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(string s) { int n = 0; foreach (var c in s.ToCharArray()) { n += c; } return n; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(string s) { int n = 0; foreach (var c in s) { n += c; } return n; } }
```

## cs/for-counter-modified-in-body

**A for loop's counter should not be changed in its body.** The header says how the loop advances; changing the counter in the body as well makes the iteration hard to follow and easy to break.

SonarQube: S127

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (G(i)) { i++; } } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { int i = 0; while (i < n) { i += G(i) ? 2 : 1; } } }
```

## cs/goto

**goto should not be used.** `goto` jumps make the flow of a method hard to follow; loops, `break` and methods say the same more clearly.

SonarQube: S907

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { retry: if (!G()) { goto retry; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { while (!G()) { } switch (x) { case 1: H(); goto case 2; case 2: break; } } }
```

## cs/public-field

**Fields should not be public.** A public field can't be validated, observed or changed into a computed value later without breaking callers; a property can.

SonarQube: S1104

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public int Count; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public int Count { get; set; } public const int Max = 10; public readonly int Id; }
```

## cs/redundant-parentheses

**Expressions should not be wrapped in two pairs of parentheses.** `((x))` is `(x)`; the extra pair adds nothing.

SonarQube: S1110

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return ((a + 1)); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return (a + 1) * 2; } }
```

## cs/instantiable-utility-class

**Classes with only static members should be static.** Nothing is gained by creating an instance of a class whose members are all static; declaring the class static says so and stops it.

SonarQube: S1118

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Paths { public static string Root() => "/"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
static class Paths { public static string Root() => "/"; }
```

## cs/obsolete-code

**Obsolete code should eventually be removed.** Code marked [Obsolete] is meant to go away; this marks where it still is.

SonarQube: S1133

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [Obsolete("Use G.")] void F() { G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void G() { H(); } }
```

## cs/throw-in-finally

**Exceptions should not be thrown from finally blocks.** An exception thrown in `finally` replaces the one that may already be on its way, which is then lost.

SonarQube: S1163

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } finally { throw new IOException(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } finally { Close(); } } }
```

## cs/null-instead-of-empty-collection

**Methods returning collections should return empty ones, not null.** Callers iterate the result; returning null makes each of them check for it, and the one that forgets throws a NullReferenceException.

SonarQube: S1168

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int[] F(bool b) { if (b) { return null; } return G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int[] F(bool b) { if (b) { return Array.Empty<int>(); } return G(); } }
```

## cs/override-only-calls-base

**Overrides should do more than call the base member.** An override that only forwards to `base` with the same arguments changes nothing.

SonarQube: S1185

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A : B { public override void Save(int x) { base.Save(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A : B { public override void Save(int x) { Log(x); base.Save(x); } }
```

## cs/nested-block

**Bare nested blocks should be removed.** A `{ ... }` block on its own suggests a forgotten condition, or a method waiting to be extracted.

SonarQube: S1199

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); { H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); H(); } }
```

## cs/for-as-while

**A for loop without initializer and update should be a while loop.** `for (; cond; )` is a `while (cond)` written in a harder way.

SonarQube: S1264

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { for (; More();) { G(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { while (More()) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/too-many-switch-cases

**switch statements should not have too many cases.** More than 30 cases are hard to read; a dictionary or polymorphism usually expresses the mapping better.

SonarQube: S1479

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 0: case 1: case 2: case 3: case 4: case 5: case 6: case 7: case 8: case 9: case 10: case 11: case 12: case 13: case 14: case 15: case 16: case 17: case 18: case 19: case 20: case 21: case 22: case 23: case 24: case 25: case 26: case 27: case 28: case 29: case 30: return 1; default: return 0; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 0: return 1; default: return 0; } } }
```

## cs/catch-null-reference

**NullReferenceException should not be caught.** A NullReferenceException is a bug in the code that threw it; catching it hides the bug instead of fixing it.

SonarQube: S1696

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { try { b.G(); } catch (NullReferenceException) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b?.G(); } }
```

## cs/constructor-calls-virtual

**Constructors should not call overridable methods.** A subclass's override runs before the subclass's constructor, on an object that isn't initialized yet.

SonarQube: S1699

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { Init(); } protected virtual void Init() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { Init(); } private void Init() { G(); } }
```

## cs/identical-branches-in-chain

**Two branches of an if chain should not have the same implementation.** Repeating a block in two branches suggests they should be merged into one condition.

SonarQube: S1871

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a) { G(); H(); } else if (b) { G(); H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a || b) { G(); H(); } } }
```

## cs/for-update-other-variable

**A for loop should update the variable its condition tests.** When the condition tests one variable and the update changes another, the loop probably updates the wrong one and never ends.

SonarQube: S1994

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n, int j) { for (int i = 0; i < n; j++) { G(i); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { G(i); } } }
```

## cs/empty-class

**Classes should not be empty.** An empty class has no behaviour and no data; an interface or an attribute usually says what it is for.

SonarQube: S2094

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Marker { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface IMarker { } class Failure : Exception { } [Serializable] class Tag { }
```

## cs/exception-name-without-base

**Classes named like exceptions should derive from Exception.** A class called `...Exception` that isn't one can't be thrown or caught, which surprises everyone who reads the name.

SonarQube: S2166

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class ParseException { public string Reason; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class ParseException : Exception { }
```

## cs/virtual-field-event

**Field-like events should not be virtual.** The compiler doesn't handle overridden field-like events as expected: subscribers to the base event aren't raised by the override.

SonarQube: S2290

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public virtual event EventHandler Changed; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler Changed; }
```

## cs/unused-type-parameter

**Type parameters should be used.** A type parameter that appears nowhere in its declaration makes callers specify a type for nothing.

SonarQube: S2326

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F<T>(int x) { G(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F<T>(T x) { G(x); } }
```

## cs/flags-zero-not-none

**[Flags] enums should name their zero member None.** Zero means no flag is set; calling it anything else suggests it's a flag of its own.

SonarQube: S2346

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Access { Nothing = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2 }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Access { None = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2 }
```

## cs/multidimensional-array-parameter

**Public methods should not take multidimensional or jagged arrays.** `int[,]` and `int[][]` parameters are awkward to build and easy to misuse; a dedicated type or a collection of collections is clearer.

SonarQube: S2368

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Load(int[,] grid) { G(grid); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Load(Grid grid) { G(grid); } }
```

## cs/property-getter-throws

**Property getters should not throw unexpected exceptions.** Reading a property is expected to be safe; debuggers and serializers read them freely. Throw from a method instead, or only InvalidOperationException, NotSupportedException or ObjectDisposedException.

SonarQube: S2372

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; public int X { get { if (x < 0) { throw new ArgumentException(); } return x; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; public int X { get { if (x < 0) { throw new InvalidOperationException(); } return x; } } }
```

## cs/write-only-property

**Properties should not be write-only.** A property that can be set but not read surprises callers; a method such as `SetX` says what it does.

SonarQube: S2376

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; public int X { set { x = value; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; public int X { get => x; set => x = value; } }
```

## cs/too-many-type-parameters

**Generic types and methods should not have too many type parameters.** More than two type parameters on a class, or three on a method, make it hard to use and to read.

SonarQube: S2436

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Mapper<A, B, C> { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Mapper<TIn, TOut> { }
```

## cs/useless-bitwise

**Bitwise operations should not have no effect.** `x | 0`, `x ^ 0` and `x & -1` all equal `x`: another operand was probably meant.

SonarQube: S2437

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { return x | 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { return x | 1; } }
```

## cs/index-of-positive

**IndexOf results should be tested with >= 0.** `IndexOf` returns 0 when the value is found at the start, so `> 0` misses that case.

SonarQube: S2692

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.IndexOf("x") > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.IndexOf("x") >= 0; } }
```

## cs/reflection-non-public

**Reflection should not reach non-public members.** BindingFlags.NonPublic bypasses accessibility: the code depends on another type's internals, which may change or be protected for a reason.

SonarQube: S3011

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(Type t) { return t.GetField("x", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(Type t) { return t.GetProperty("X", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance); } }
```

## cs/this-is-type

**this should not be tested for its own subtypes.** A class testing `this is Derived` knows about its subclasses; virtual members let each subclass do its part instead.

SonarQube: S3060

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F() { return this is B ? 1 : 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { protected virtual int F() { return 0; } }
```

## cs/repeated-order-by

**OrderBy should not be called twice in a row.** The second `OrderBy` discards the first ordering; `ThenBy` adds a secondary key instead.

SonarQube: S3169

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(List<P> ps) { return ps.OrderBy(p => p.Last).OrderBy(p => p.First); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(List<P> ps) { return ps.OrderBy(p => p.Last).ThenBy(p => p.First); } }
```

## cs/duplicate-cast

**A type check should not be followed by a cast to the same type.** `if (x is T) { var t = (T)x; }` checks the type twice; `if (x is T t)` does it once.

SonarQube: S3247

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(object o) { if (o is string) { var s = (string)o; G(s); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(object o) { if (o is string s) { G(s); } } }
```

## cs/empty-namespace

**Namespaces should not be empty.** An empty namespace declaration is a leftover.

SonarQube: S3261

Noncompliant:

```csharp
namespace Shop.Legacy { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
namespace Shop { class Cart { } }
```

## cs/type-suffix-from-base

**Attributes, EventArgs and exceptions should be named after their base.** By convention, attribute classes end in `Attribute`, event data in `EventArgs` and exceptions in `Exception`; tools and readers rely on it.

SonarQube: S3376

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Retry : Attribute { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class RetryAttribute : Attribute { }
```

## cs/check-before-assign

**A variable should not be checked against the value it is about to get.** `if (x != 1) { x = 1; }` gives the same result as `x = 1;` alone.

SonarQube: S3440

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F() { if (x != 1) { x = 1; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F() { x = 1; } }
```

## cs/empty-case-before-default

**Empty case labels that fall into default should be removed.** A `case` with no code of its own that falls into `default:` behaves like the default and adds nothing.

SonarQube: S3458

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: return 1; case 2: default: return 0; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: return 1; default: return 0; } } }
```

## cs/non-public-exception

**Exception types should be public.** Callers outside the assembly can't catch a non-public exception by its type, only as a general Exception.

SonarQube: S3871

Noncompliant:

```csharp
namespace N { internal class QuotaException : Exception { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
namespace N { public class QuotaException : Exception { } }
```

## cs/class-equality-operator

**operator== should not be overloaded on classes.** Readers expect `==` on a class to compare references; overloading it changes that silently. Override Equals instead, or use a record or struct.

SonarQube: S3875

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Money { public static bool operator ==(Money a, Money b) => true; public static bool operator !=(Money a, Money b) => false; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
record Money(decimal Amount);
```

## cs/throw-in-unexpected-method

**Exceptions should not be thrown from methods expected to be safe.** `ToString`, `Equals`, `GetHashCode`, `Dispose`, implicit conversions, static constructors and finalizers are called implicitly, often where an exception isn't expected or can't be handled.

SonarQube: S3877

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { throw new InvalidOperationException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { return "A"; } }
```

## cs/assembly-load-from

**Assembly.LoadFrom, LoadFile and LoadWithPartialName should not be used.** They load assemblies outside the normal binding context, which leads to type identity problems and loading the wrong version.

SonarQube: S3885

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Assembly F(string p) { return Assembly.LoadFrom(p); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Assembly F(string name) { return Assembly.Load(name); } }
```

## cs/argument-exception-param-name

**ArgumentException parameter names should name a parameter.** The parameter name tells the caller which argument was wrong; one that doesn't exist points them nowhere.

SonarQube: S3928

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string name) { if (name == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException("nmae"); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string name) { if (name == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(name)); } } }
```

## cs/suppress-finalize-outside-dispose

**GC.SuppressFinalize should only be called from Dispose.** Suppressing finalization anywhere else can skip cleanup of an object that wasn't disposed.

SonarQube: S3971

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A : IDisposable { public void Close() { GC.SuppressFinalize(this); } public void Dispose() { Close(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A : IDisposable { public void Dispose() { Close(); GC.SuppressFinalize(this); } }
```

## cs/attribute-usage-missing

**Custom attributes should declare [AttributeUsage].** Without it the attribute can go on any target, once, and is inherited, which is rarely what was intended.

SonarQube: S3993

Noncompliant:

```csharp
sealed class RetryAttribute : Attribute { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Method)] sealed class RetryAttribute : Attribute { }
```

## cs/outdated-base-type

**Types should not derive from outdated base types.** ApplicationException, CollectionBase, DictionaryBase and the non-generic collections are kept for compatibility; Exception and the generic collections replace them.

SonarQube: S4052

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class QuotaException : ApplicationException { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class QuotaException : Exception { }
```

## cs/overloads-not-adjacent

**Method overloads should be declared together.** Overloads spread across a class are easy to miss when reading or changing one of them.

SonarQube: S4136

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { } void G() { } void F(string s) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { } void F(string s) { } void G() { } }
```

## cs/default-not-first-or-last

**default clauses should be first or last.** A `default:` in the middle of the cases is easy to overlook.

SonarQube: S4524

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: return 1; default: return 0; case 2: return 2; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: return 1; case 2: return 2; default: return 0; } } }
```

## cs/new-guid

**new Guid() should not be used.** `new Guid()` is the all-zero Guid, not a new unique one; that is rarely what's meant.

SonarQube: S4581

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Guid F() { return new Guid(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Guid F() { return Guid.NewGuid(); } }
```

## cs/substring-then-search

**Strings should be searched with a start index, not after Substring.** `s.Substring(i).IndexOf(x)` copies the string and returns a position relative to `i`; `s.IndexOf(x, i)` does neither.

SonarQube: S4635

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(string s, int i) { return s.Substring(i).IndexOf('x'); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(string s, int i) { return s.IndexOf('x', i); } }
```

## cs/empty-comment

**Comments should not be empty.** An empty comment is a leftover that adds noise.

SonarQube: S4663

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { //
 G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { // explain why
 G(); } }
```

## cs/unix-epoch

**DateTime.UnixEpoch should be used for the Unix epoch.** `new DateTime(1970, 1, 1)` spells out a constant the framework already has, and easily gets the DateTimeKind wrong.

SonarQube: S6588

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime E() { return new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime E() { return DateTime.UnixEpoch; } }
```

## cs/log-template-not-constant

**Logging templates should be constant.** An interpolated or concatenated message is built even when the level is off, and loses the structured values a template such as `"User {UserId}"` keeps.

SonarQube: S2629

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation($"Saved {id}"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation("Saved {Id}", id); } }
```

## cs/format-string-unused-argument

**Composite format strings should use all their arguments.** An argument no placeholder refers to is never printed; the placeholder for it was probably forgotten.

SonarQube: S3457

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int a, int b) { return string.Format("{0}", a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int a, int b) { return string.Format("{0} {1}", a, b); } }
```

## cs/hashcode-mutable-field

**GetHashCode should not use mutable fields.** If a field the hash depends on changes while the object is in a dictionary or set, the object can't be found there any more.

SonarQube: S2328

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public override int GetHashCode() => id; public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class P { readonly int id; public override int GetHashCode() => id; public override bool Equals(object o) => o is P p && p.id == id; }
```

## cs/lock-on-mutable-field

**Locks should be taken on readonly fields.** Locking a field that can be reassigned, or a new object, lets two threads hold "the" lock at the same time, each on a different object.

SonarQube: S2445

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object sync = new object(); void F() { lock (sync) { G(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { readonly object sync = new object(); void F() { lock (sync) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/assert-side-effects

**Debug.Assert should not have side effects.** Debug.Assert calls are removed from release builds, and their side effects with them, so the program behaves differently in release.

SonarQube: S3346

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<int> xs) { Debug.Assert(xs.Remove(1)); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<int> xs) { bool removed = xs.Remove(1); Debug.Assert(removed); } }
```

## cs/public-readonly-mutable

**Visible readonly fields should not hold mutable collections.** `readonly` only stops the field being reassigned; callers can still change the array or list it holds.

SonarQube: S3887

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public readonly List<string> Names = new List<string>(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public IReadOnlyList<string> Names { get; } = new List<string>(); }
```

## cs/log-template-duplicate-placeholder

**Log message templates should not repeat placeholder names.** Placeholders are matched to arguments by position, but structured logging stores them by name; a repeated name keeps only one of the values.

SonarQube: S6677

Noncompliant:

