```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int a, int b) { log.LogInformation("Moved {Id} to {Id}", a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int a, int b) { log.LogInformation("Moved {From} to {To}", a, b); } }
```

## cs/log-template-syntax

**Log message templates should be well formed.** An unbalanced brace or an empty placeholder makes the logger misread the template, so the message or its values are lost.

SonarQube: S6674

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation("Saved {Id", id); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation("Saved {Id}", id); } }
```

## cs/world-permissions

**Files should not be readable or writable by everyone.** Permissions for "other" users or for Everyone let any account on the machine read or change the file.

SonarQube: S2612

Standards: [CWE-266](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/266.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string p) { File.SetUnixFileMode(p, UnixFileMode.UserRead | UnixFileMode.OtherWrite); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string p) { File.SetUnixFileMode(p, UnixFileMode.UserRead | UnixFileMode.UserWrite); } }
```

## cs/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hard-coded.** A literal address breaks when the network changes and can't be overridden per environment.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string Host() => "203.0.113.7"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string Host(IConfiguration c) => c["Host"]; string Local() => "127.0.0.1"; }
```

## cs/duplicated-string

**String literals should not be duplicated.** A literal repeated across a file is easy to change in one place and miss in the others.

SonarQube: S1192

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string a = "connection failed"; string b = "connection failed"; string c = "connection failed"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Failed = "connection failed"; string a = Failed; string b = Failed; }
```

## cs/unused-local-variable

**Unused local variables should be removed.** A variable nothing reads is dead code.

SonarQube: S1481

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { int unused = 3; G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F() { int used = 3; using var lease = Acquire(); return used; } }
```

## cs/local-shadows-field

**Local variables should not shadow fields or properties.** A local with a member's name hides it: code meant for the member silently uses the local.

SonarQube: S1117

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int count; void F() { int count = 0; G(count); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int count; void F() { int total = 0; G(total); } }
```

## cs/unused-private-member

**Unused private members should be removed.** A private method, field or property that nothing in its class uses is dead code.

SonarQube: S1144

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int Helper() => 1; public int F() => 2; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private int Helper() => 1; public int F() => Helper() + 1; }
```

## cs/log-and-rethrow

**Exceptions should be either logged or rethrown, not both.** Logging an exception and then rethrowing it logs it again wherever it's finally handled, so one failure shows up several times.

SonarQube: S2139

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError(e, "Failed"); throw; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError(e, "Failed"); Retry(); } } }
```

## cs/test-class-without-tests

**Test classes should contain tests.** A class marked [TestClass] or [TestFixture] without a test method runs nothing; tests were removed or mis-attributed.

SonarQube: S2187

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[TestClass] public class CartTests { public void Totals() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[TestClass] public class CartTests { [TestMethod] public void Totals() { } }
```

## cs/type-check-simplify

**Runtime type checks should use is.** `typeof(T).IsInstanceOfType(x)` and `x as T != null` are longer ways to write `x is T`.

SonarQube: S2219

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(object o) { return typeof(string).IsInstanceOfType(o); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(object o) { return o is string; } }
```

## cs/public-static-mutable-collection

**Visible static readonly fields should not hold mutable collections.** `static readonly` only stops the field being reassigned; any code can still change the shared array or list, for every caller at once.

SonarQube: S2386

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public static readonly string[] Codes = { "a", "b" }; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public static readonly IReadOnlyList<string> Codes = new[] { "a", "b" }; }
```

## cs/control-char-in-string

**Control characters in string literals should be escaped.** A raw tab or other control character in a literal is invisible in the source, and editors may change it without anyone noticing.

SonarQube: S2479

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Sep = "a	b"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Sep = "a\tb"; }
```

## cs/generic-exception-ignored

**General exceptions should not be ignored.** An empty `catch` for every exception hides all failures, including bugs and out-of-memory conditions.

SonarQube: S2486

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (Exception) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException) { /* the file is optional */ } } }
```

## cs/assertion-boolean-literal

**Assertions should not compare with boolean literals.** `Assert.AreEqual(true, x)` is `Assert.IsTrue(x)` written the long way, with a less useful failure message.

SonarQube: S2701

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(bool ok) { Assert.AreEqual(true, ok); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(bool ok) { Assert.IsTrue(ok); } }
```

## cs/static-field-in-generic

**Generic classes should not have static fields that don't use their type parameters.** Each constructed type (`Cache<int>`, `Cache<string>`) gets its own copy of the static field, which is rarely what a field unrelated to T is meant to do.

SonarQube: S2743

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Cache<T> { static int hits; T Get() => default; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Cache<T> { static T last; T Get() => last; }
```

## cs/thread-sleep-in-test

**Tests should not use Thread.Sleep.** Sleeping makes tests slow and flaky: too short fails on a busy machine, too long wastes every run. Wait for the condition itself instead.

SonarQube: S2925

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { [Fact] public void Waits() { Start(); Thread.Sleep(500); Check(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { [Fact] public async Task Waits() { Start(); await done.Task; Check(); } }
```

## cs/dispose-without-idisposable

**Dispose methods should implement IDisposable.** A `Dispose()` method on a type that doesn't implement IDisposable is never called by `using` or by containers, so its cleanup doesn't happen.

SonarQube: S2953

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Conn { public void Dispose() { Close(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Conn : IDisposable { public void Dispose() { Close(); } }
```

## cs/incomplete-assertion

**Assertions should be complete.** `x.Should()` on its own asserts nothing: the check that should follow it is missing.

SonarQube: S2970

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(int total) { total.Should(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(int total) { total.Should().Be(3); } }
```

## cs/assertion-argument-order

**Assertions should pass the expected value first.** Assert.AreEqual and Assert.Equal take the expected value first; swapped arguments give a failure message that says the opposite of what happened.

SonarQube: S3415

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(int total) { Assert.AreEqual(total, 3); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { void F(int total) { Assert.AreEqual(3, total); } }
```

## cs/expected-exception-attribute

**[ExpectedException] should not be used.** The attribute passes when any line of the test throws, so it can pass for the wrong reason. Assert.ThrowsException points at the call that should throw.

SonarQube: S3431

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod, ExpectedException(typeof(ArgumentException))] public void F() { Parse(""); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod] public void F() { Assert.ThrowsException<ArgumentException>(() => Parse("")); } }
```

## cs/test-method-signature

**Test methods should be public and not async void.** Test frameworks skip non-public test methods, and can't wait for an `async void` one, so its failures go unreported.

SonarQube: S3433

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod] async void Loads() { await Load(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod] public async Task Loads() { await Load(); } }
```

## cs/type-of-type

**GetType() should not be called on a Type.** `typeof(X).GetType()` and `x.GetType().GetType()` return `System.RuntimeType`, not the type that was meant.

SonarQube: S3443

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Type F() { return typeof(A).GetType(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Type F() { return typeof(A); } }
```

## cs/optional-on-ref-out

**[Optional] should not be used on ref or out parameters.** A ref or out parameter must always be passed, so [Optional] can't make it optional.

SonarQube: S3447

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F([Optional] ref int x) { x++; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ref int x) { x++; } }
```

## cs/default-parameter-value-without-optional

**[DefaultParameterValue] should be used with [Optional].** Without [Optional], callers must still pass the argument, so the default value is never used.

SonarQube: S3450

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F([DefaultParameterValue(5)] int x) { G(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F([Optional, DefaultParameterValue(5)] int x) { G(x); } }
```

## cs/default-value-on-parameter

**[DefaultValue] should not be used on parameters.** System.ComponentModel's [DefaultValue] is for designers and serializers; on a parameter it has no effect. [DefaultParameterValue] or a C# default value was meant.

SonarQube: S3451

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F([Optional, DefaultValue(5)] int x) { G(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x = 5) { G(x); } }
```

## cs/service-contract-without-operations

**Service contracts should have operations.** A [ServiceContract] without any [OperationContract] member exposes nothing, and WCF rejects it when the host starts.

SonarQube: S3597

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[ServiceContract] interface IOrders { int Count(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[ServiceContract] interface IOrders { [OperationContract] int Count(); }
```

## cs/equals-without-iequatable

**Types with Equals(T) should implement IEquatable<T>.** Collections and LINQ only use a typed Equals through IEquatable<T>; without it they fall back to Equals(object).

SonarQube: S3897

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P { int id; public bool Equals(P other) => other != null && other.id == id; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class P : IEquatable<P> { int id; public bool Equals(P other) => other != null && other.id == id; }
```

## cs/iequatable-not-sealed

**Classes implementing IEquatable<T> should be sealed.** A subclass inherits Equals(T) without being able to compare its own fields through it, so equality silently ignores them.

SonarQube: S4035

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P : IEquatable<P> { public bool Equals(P o) => true; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class P : IEquatable<P> { public bool Equals(P o) => true; }
```

## cs/starts-with-single-char

**StartsWith and EndsWith should take a char for one character.** The char overloads are faster and don't depend on the current culture, which the string overloads do.

SonarQube: S6610

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.StartsWith("/"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.StartsWith('/'); } }
```

## cs/logger-category-mismatch

**ILogger<T> should be typed with its own class.** The type argument is the log category; `ILogger<OtherClass>` files this class's messages under another class's name.

SonarQube: S6672

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Orders { public Orders(ILogger<Carts> log) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Orders { public Orders(ILogger<Orders> log) { } }
```

## cs/log-placeholder-pascal-case

**Log template placeholders should be PascalCase.** Structured logging stores each placeholder as a property; PascalCase names match the other properties and are easier to query.

SonarQube: S6678

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation("Saved {orderId}", id); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int id) { log.LogInformation("Saved {OrderId}", id); } }
```

## cs/ignored-test

**Ignored tests should say why.** A test switched off without a reason is easily forgotten; the reason tells the next reader when it can come back.

SonarQube: S1607

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod, Ignore] public void Slow() { Run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { [TestMethod, Ignore("Needs the staging database, see #42")] public void Slow() { Run(); } }
```

## cs/conditions-same-line

**An if should not start on the line where the previous one ends.** `} if (b) {` reads like an `else if` that lost its `else`; either it is one, or it belongs on its own line.

SonarQube: S3972

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) {
 if (a) { G(); } if (b) { H(); }
} }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) {
 if (a) { G(); }
 if (b) { H(); }
} }
```

## cs/sql-keyword-spacing

**SQL fragments should be separated by a space when concatenated.** `"SELECT *" + "FROM t"` is fine, but `"SELECT id" + "FROM t"` sends `idFROM`, which the database rejects or misreads.

SonarQube: S2857

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Q = "SELECT id, name" + "FROM users"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Q = "SELECT id, name " + "FROM users"; }
```

## cs/thread-suspend-resume

**Thread.Suspend and Thread.Resume should not be used.** Suspending a thread at an arbitrary point can leave it holding a lock, which deadlocks everything else that needs it. Both methods are obsolete.

SonarQube: S3889

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Thread worker) { worker.Suspend(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ManualResetEventSlim pause) { pause.Reset(); } }
```

## cs/winforms-main-without-stathread

**Windows Forms entry points should be marked [STAThread].** Windows Forms needs a single-threaded apartment; without [STAThread] on Main, the clipboard, drag and drop and common dialogs fail at run time.

SonarQube: S4210

Noncompliant:

```csharp
static class P { static void Main() { Application.Run(new MainForm()); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
static class P { [STAThread] static void Main() { Application.Run(new MainForm()); } }
```

## cs/part-creation-policy-without-export

**[PartCreationPolicy] should be used with [Export].** MEF only reads the creation policy of exported parts, so on its own the attribute does nothing.

SonarQube: S4428

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[PartCreationPolicy(CreationPolicy.Shared)] class Cache { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Export, PartCreationPolicy(CreationPolicy.Shared)] class Cache { }
```

## cs/constructor-argument-missing

**[ConstructorArgument] should name a constructor parameter.** XAML uses the name to match the property to a constructor parameter; a name no constructor has breaks deserialization.

SonarQube: S4260

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Ext { public Ext(string path) { Path = path; } [ConstructorArgument("file")] public string Path { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Ext { public Ext(string path) { Path = path; } [ConstructorArgument("path")] public string Path { get; set; } }
```

## cs/export-type-not-implemented

**Exported types should implement the contract they export.** MEF fails when composing a part exported as an interface or base class it doesn't actually implement.

SonarQube: S4159

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Export(typeof(IStore))] class Cache { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Export(typeof(IStore))] class Cache : IStore { }
```

## cs/jsinvokable-not-public

**[JSInvokable] methods should be public.** Blazor only calls public methods from JavaScript; a non-public [JSInvokable] method fails when the script calls it.

SonarQube: S6798

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C { [JSInvokable] void Notify() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C { [JSInvokable] public void Notify() { } }
```

## cs/route-backslash

**Route templates should not contain backslashes.** URLs use `/`; a backslash in a route template never matches a request, or matches a literal backslash.

SonarQube: S6930

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [Route("orders\\{id}")] public IActionResult Get(int id) => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C : Controller { [Route("orders/{id}")] public IActionResult Get(int id) => Ok(); }
```

## cs/type-name-pascal-case

**Type names should be PascalCase.** C# types are named in PascalCase, with acronyms of more than two letters written like words (`HtmlParser`, not `HTMLParser`), and interfaces start with I.

SonarQube: S101

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class HTMLParser { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class HtmlParser { } interface IDbStore { } class IOStream { }
```

## cs/literal-suffix-case

**Long literal suffixes should be upper case.** A lowercase `l` suffix looks like the digit 1: `100l` reads as one thousand and one.

SonarQube: S818 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { long Max = 100l; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { long Max = 100L; }
```

## cs/assignment-in-subexpression

**Assignments should not be made inside sub-expressions.** An assignment hidden in a condition or argument is easy to miss, or to mistake for a comparison.

SonarQube: S1121

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; bool F(int y) { return (x = y) > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; bool F(int y) { x = y; return x > 0; } }
```

## cs/icomparable-without-operators

**Types implementing IComparable should define the comparison operators.** Readers expect `a < b` to work on a type that can be compared; without the operators they must call CompareTo, and `==` may disagree with it.

SonarQube: S1210

Noncompliant:

```csharp
struct Money : IComparable<Money> { public int CompareTo(Money o) => 0; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
struct Money : IComparable<Money> { public int CompareTo(Money o) => 0; public static bool operator <(Money a, Money b) => a.CompareTo(b) < 0; public static bool operator >(Money a, Money b) => a.CompareTo(b) > 0; }
```

## cs/trivial-property

**Trivial properties should be auto-implemented.** A property that only reads and writes a private field nothing else uses is an auto-property written out by hand.

SonarQube: S2292

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int count; public int Count { get { return count; } set { count = value; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public int Count { get; set; } }
```

## cs/contextual-keyword-name

**Contextual keywords should not be used as names.** `await`, `async`, `var`, `dynamic`, `nameof` and `yield` are keywords in some contexts; as names they confuse readers and break when the code moves into such a context.

SonarQube: S2306

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int await) { G(await); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int delay) { G(delay); } }
```

## cs/flags-enum-name-plural

**[Flags] enum names should be plural.** A [Flags] value holds several options at once, so a plural name (`FileAccessRights`) says so; a singular one reads like a single choice.

SonarQube: S2342 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Permission { None = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2 }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Permissions { None = 0, Read = 1, Write = 2 }
```

## cs/property-copies-collection

**Properties should not return copies of collections.** A property that copies an array or list on every read looks cheap but isn't, and changes to what it returns are silently lost. Use a method, or return a read-only view.

SonarQube: S2365

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { List<int> items = new(); public int[] Items => items.ToArray(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { List<int> items = new(); public IReadOnlyList<int> Items => items; }
```

## cs/multiline-without-braces

**Statements indented under an unbraced if or loop should be in braces.** Without braces only the first statement belongs to the if or loop; one indented under it as well runs every time, whatever the indentation suggests.

SonarQube: S2681

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) {
    if (b)
        G();
        H();
} }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) {
    if (b)
    {
        G();
        H();
    }
} }
```

## cs/instance-writes-static

**Instance members should not write static fields.** Every instance shares a static field, so a write from one instance's method changes what all the others see, and races when they run on different threads.

SonarQube: S2696

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { static int count; void Hit() { count++; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { static int count; static void Hit() { count++; } }
```

## cs/static-field-set-in-constructor

**Instance constructors should not set static fields.** Each new instance overwrites the shared value, so it ends up describing whichever object was created last.

SonarQube: S3010

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { static string Name; public A(string name) { Name = name; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string name; public A(string name) { this.name = name; } }
```

## cs/private-class-not-sealed

**Private classes that nothing derives from should be sealed.** Sealing says the class isn't designed for inheritance and lets the runtime call its members directly.

SonarQube: S3260

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private class Node { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private sealed class Node { } }
```

## cs/static-constructor-only-initializes

**Static fields should be initialized inline.** A static constructor that only assigns static fields says the same as their initializers, and stops the runtime from initializing the type lazily.

SonarQube: S3963

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { static int max; static A() { max = 10; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { static int max = 10; }
```

## cs/identical-methods

**Methods should not have identical implementations.** Two methods with the same body are duplicated code; one should call the other.

SonarQube: S4144

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); H(); } void K() { G(); H(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); H(); } void K() { F(); } }
```

## cs/public-extern

**Native methods should be wrapped.** Exposing a P/Invoke declaration directly lets callers pass anything to native code, without the checks a managed wrapper would add.

SonarQube: S4200

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class N { [DllImport("lib")] public static extern int Run(string s); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class N { [DllImport("lib")] private static extern int Run(string s); public static int Start(string s) => Run(s ?? ""); }
```

## cs/null-check-before-is

**Null checks should not come before a type check.** `x is T` is already false for null, so `x != null && x is T` checks null twice.

SonarQube: S4201

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(object o) { return o != null && o is string; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(object o) { return o is string; } }
```

## cs/event-args-null

**Events should not be raised with null arguments.** Handlers expect `sender` to be the object and the event data to exist; passing null makes every handler check for it, and the ones that don't throw.

SonarQube: S4220

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler Changed; void F() { Changed?.Invoke(this, null); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler Changed; void F() { Changed?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty); } }
```

## cs/datetime-now-benchmark

**Stopwatch should be used to measure elapsed time.** DateTime.Now has a coarse resolution and jumps with clock changes; Stopwatch measures intervals accurately.

SonarQube: S6561

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { TimeSpan F(DateTime start) { return DateTime.Now - start; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { TimeSpan F(Stopwatch watch) { return watch.Elapsed; } }
```

## cs/trace-write

**Trace.Write and Trace.WriteLine should not be used for logging.** They carry no severity, so listeners can't filter them; TraceInformation, TraceWarning and TraceError do.

SonarQube: S6670

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Trace.WriteLine("started"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Trace.TraceInformation("started"); } }
```

## cs/logger-field-name

**Logger fields should be named logger.** One name for the logger field (`logger`, `_logger`, `Logger`, `log` and the like) makes logging code easy to find and read across a codebase.

SonarQube: S6669

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private readonly ILogger<A> output; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private readonly ILogger<A> logger; }
```

## cs/log-exception-not-passed

**Caught exceptions should be passed to the logger.** Logging only a message in a catch block drops the exception's type and stack trace, which is what the log is needed for.

SonarQube: S6667

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError("Write failed"); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError(e, "Write failed"); } } }
```

## cs/log-template-argument-order

**Log arguments should be in the order of their placeholders.** Placeholders take the arguments by position, not by name; `"{From} {To}", to, from` logs them the wrong way round.

SonarQube: S6673

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int from, int to) { log.LogInformation("Moved {From} to {To}", to, from); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log, int from, int to) { log.LogInformation("Moved {From} to {To}", from, to); } }
```

## cs/action-route-leading-slash

**Action route templates should not start with /.** A template starting with `/` ignores the controller's [Route], so the action ends up at a URL its controller doesn't suggest.

SonarQube: S6931

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Route("api/orders")] class C : ControllerBase { [HttpGet("/all")] public IActionResult All() => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Route("api/orders")] class C : ControllerBase { [HttpGet("all")] public IActionResult All() => Ok(); }
```

## cs/controller-route-missing

**Controllers whose actions have routes should have a [Route].** Without a controller-level [Route], every action repeats the common prefix, and changing it means editing them all.

SonarQube: S6934

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class OrdersController : Controller { [HttpGet("orders/{id}")] public IActionResult Get(int id) => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Route("orders")] class OrdersController : Controller { [HttpGet("{id}")] public IActionResult Get(int id) => Ok(); }
```

## cs/api-controller-derives-controller

**API controllers should derive from ControllerBase.** `Controller` adds view support an API never uses; ControllerBase is the base for [ApiController] classes.

SonarQube: S6961

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : Controller { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : ControllerBase { }
```

## cs/new-httpclient

**HttpClient should not be created per call.** Each new HttpClient opens its own connections, which exhausts sockets under load and misses DNS changes. Use IHttpClientFactory or one shared instance.

SonarQube: S6962

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task<string> F() { using var http = new HttpClient(); return await http.GetStringAsync("https://a.example"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { static readonly HttpClient Http = new HttpClient(); Task<string> F() => Http.GetStringAsync("https://a.example"); }
```

## cs/action-without-http-verb

**API actions should declare their HTTP method.** Without [HttpGet], [HttpPost] and the like, an API action answers every verb, and API descriptions can't document it.

SonarQube: S6965

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : ControllerBase { public IActionResult List() => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpGet] public IActionResult List() => Ok(); }
```

## cs/produces-response-type-missing

**API actions returning IActionResult should declare their response types.** `IActionResult` hides what the action returns, so API descriptions show nothing for it. [ProducesResponseType], or ActionResult<T>, says it.

SonarQube: S6968

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpGet] public IActionResult List() => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[ApiController] class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpGet] public ActionResult<Order[]> List() => Ok(); }
```

## cs/generic-null-comparison

**Unconstrained type parameters should not be compared to null.** When T can be a value type, `x == null` is always false for it, so the check doesn't do what it seems to for every T.

SonarQube: S2955

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F<T>(T x) { return x == null; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F<T>(T x) where T : class { return x == null; } }
```

## cs/optional-field-without-callbacks

**[OptionalField] members should get a value when deserialized.** Data serialized by an older version has no value for an [OptionalField] member; without an [OnDeserializing] or [OnDeserialized] method to set one, it stays at its default.

SonarQube: S3926

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class P { [OptionalField] int version; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class P { [OptionalField] int version; [OnDeserialized] void Restore(StreamingContext c) { version = 2; } }
```

## cs/accessor-wrong-field

**Getters and setters should use the field of their property.** A property `Name` whose accessor reads or writes `count` instead of `name` was probably copied from another property and not updated.

SonarQube: S4275

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int name; int count; public int Name { get { return count; } set { name = value; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int name; int count; public int Name { get { return name; } set { name = value; } } }
```

## cs/query-parameter-unsupported-type

**[SupplyParameterFromQuery] properties should have a supported type.** Blazor only binds query string values to simple types such as int, string, Guid and DateTime (and their nullable and array forms); other types fail at run time.

SonarQube: S6797

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class P { [Parameter, SupplyParameterFromQuery] public List<int> Ids { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class P { [Parameter, SupplyParameterFromQuery] public int[] Ids { get; set; } }
```

## cs/regex-without-timeout

**Regular expressions should have a timeout.** Without a timeout, a crafted input can make a backtracking pattern run for minutes, blocking a thread per request (ReDoS).

SonarQube: S6444

Standards: [CWE-400](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/400.html), [CWE-1333](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1333.html)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return new Regex("(a+)+$").IsMatch(s); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return new Regex("(a+)+$", RegexOptions.None, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1)).IsMatch(s); } }
```

## cs/random-secret

**System.Random should not produce secrets.** Random is predictable: values derived from it, such as tokens, passwords or salts, can be guessed. RandomNumberGenerator is made for that.

SonarQube: S2245 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html), [CWE-338](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/338.html), [CWE-1241](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1241.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F() { var token = new Random().Next().ToString(); return token; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F() { var token = RandomNumberGenerator.GetInt32(int.MaxValue).ToString(); return token; } }
```

## cs/hardcoded-uri

**URIs and file paths should not be hard-coded.** An absolute URL or path in the code breaks when the environment changes and can't be configured per deployment.

SonarQube: S1075

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { var uploadPath = "/var/data/uploads"; G(uploadPath); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(IConfiguration c) { var uploadPath = c["UploadPath"]; G(uploadPath); } }
```

## cs/unused-parameter

**Unused parameters of private methods should be removed.** A parameter a private method never reads makes every call pass something for nothing.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public int F() => G(1, 2); private int G(int a, int b) => a; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public int F() => G(1); private int G(int a) => a; }
```

## cs/string-concat-in-loop

**Strings should not be built with += in a loop.** Each `+=` copies the whole string, so building one in a loop takes time proportional to the square of its length. StringBuilder appends in place.

SonarQube: S1643

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int[] xs) { string s = ""; foreach (var x in xs) { s += x; } return s; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int[] xs) { var sb = new StringBuilder(); foreach (var x in xs) { sb.Append(x); } return sb.ToString(); } }
```

## cs/abstract-class-only-abstract

**Abstract classes with only abstract members should be interfaces.** An abstract class without fields or implemented members is an interface that uses up the single base class a type can have.

SonarQube: S1694 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
abstract class Shape { public abstract double Area(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface IShape { double Area(); }
```

## cs/test-without-assertion

**Tests should assert something.** A test that asserts nothing passes as long as nothing throws, so it doesn't check the behaviour it's named after.

SonarQube: S2699

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class T { [Fact] public void Totals() { var cart = new Cart(); cart.Add(3); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class T { [Fact] public void Totals() { var cart = new Cart(); cart.Add(3); Assert.Equal(3, cart.Total); } }
```

## cs/field-could-be-readonly

**Private fields only assigned in constructors should be readonly.** `readonly` documents that the field doesn't change after construction, and the compiler then makes sure of it.

SonarQube: S2933

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int size; public A(int s) { size = s; } public int Size => size; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private readonly int size; public A(int s) { size = s; } public int Size => size; }
```

## cs/optional-overload-collision

**Optional parameters should not make an overload unreachable.** When `F(int a, int b = 0)` and `F(int a)` both exist, a call `F(1)` always picks the second, so the default value is never used.

SonarQube: S3427

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { } void F(int a, int b = 0) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a, int b = 0) { } }
```

## cs/field-never-assigned

**Private fields should be assigned before they're read.** A private field that is read but never assigned always holds its default value; the assignment was forgotten, or the field isn't needed.

SonarQube: S3459

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int limit; public bool Over(int x) => x > limit; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private int limit = 10; public bool Over(int x) => x > limit; }
```

## cs/redundant-field-initializer

**Fields should not be initialized when every constructor sets them.** An initializer that every constructor overwrites runs for nothing and misleads readers about the starting value.

SonarQube: S3604

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int size = 10; public A(int s) { size = s; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int size; public A(int s) { size = s; } }
```

## cs/iserializable-without-constructor

**ISerializable types should have a deserialization constructor.** The serializer calls a constructor taking (SerializationInfo, StreamingContext); without it, deserialization fails at run time.

SonarQube: S3925 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class P : ISerializable { public void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo i, StreamingContext c) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class P : ISerializable { protected P(SerializationInfo i, StreamingContext c) { } public void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo i, StreamingContext c) { } }
```

## cs/unbraced-body-not-indented

**The statement controlled by an unbraced if or loop should be indented.** A body on the next line at the same indentation as its `if` reads like code that always runs.

SonarQube: S3973

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) {
    if (b)
    G();
} }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) {
    if (b)
        G();
} }
```

## cs/operator-without-equals

**Types overloading operators should override Equals.** A type with its own `==`, `+` or `-` defines what its values mean; without matching Equals and GetHashCode, collections and LINQ compare them differently.

SonarQube: S4050

Noncompliant:

```csharp
struct Money { public static Money operator +(Money a, Money b) => a; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
struct Money { public static Money operator +(Money a, Money b) => a; public override bool Equals(object o) => false; public override int GetHashCode() => 0; }
```

## cs/iterator-argument-validation

**Iterator methods should validate their arguments eagerly.** The body of a `yield` method only runs when the result is enumerated, so its argument checks throw later, far from the bad call.

SonarQube: S4456

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { IEnumerable<int> F(int[] xs) { if (xs == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(xs)); } foreach (var x in xs) { yield return x; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { IEnumerable<int> F(int[] xs) { if (xs == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(xs)); } return Iterate(xs); } IEnumerable<int> Iterate(int[] xs) { foreach (var x in xs) { yield return x; } } }
```

## cs/unread-private-field

**Private fields that are written but never read should be removed.** A field only ever assigned is dead state: whatever it holds is never used.

SonarQube: S4487

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int last; public void Set(int x) { last = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private int last; public void Set(int x) { last = x; } public int Last => last; }
```

## cs/debugger-display-unknown-member

**[DebuggerDisplay] should refer to existing members.** The debugger shows an error instead of the value when a {placeholder} names a member the type doesn't have.

SonarQube: S4545

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[DebuggerDisplay("{Nmae}")] class P { public string Name { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[DebuggerDisplay("{Name}")] class P { public string Name { get; set; } }
```

## cs/azure-function-client-recreated

**Azure Functions should reuse their clients.** Creating an HttpClient or a storage, Cosmos or Service Bus client on every invocation exhausts connections under load; create it once and reuse it.

SonarQube: S6420

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run() { var http = new HttpClient(); await http.GetAsync("https://a.example"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class F { static readonly HttpClient Http = new HttpClient(); [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run() { await Http.GetAsync("https://a.example"); } }
```

## cs/azure-function-blocking

**Azure Functions should not block on async calls.** .Result and .Wait() hold a thread while the call runs, which starves the Functions host under load and can deadlock.

SonarQube: S6422

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public string Run(HttpClient h) { return h.GetStringAsync("https://a.example").Result; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task<string> Run(HttpClient h) { return await h.GetStringAsync("https://a.example"); } }
```

## cs/datetime-without-kind

**New DateTime values should say their DateTimeKind.** Without a kind the value is Unspecified, and conversions to and from UTC then guess which time zone it was in.

SonarQube: S6562

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime F() { return new DateTime(2024, 1, 31); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime F() { return new DateTime(2024, 1, 31, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc); } }
```

## cs/parse-without-culture

**Dates should be parsed with a format provider.** Without one, parsing uses the current culture, so the same string means another date (or fails) on a machine with other regional settings.

SonarQube: S6580

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime F(string s) { return DateTime.Parse(s); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime F(string s) { return DateTime.Parse(s, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture); } }
```

## cs/too-many-log-calls

**Blocks should not contain too many logging calls.** More than four logging calls in one block drown the code it logs, and usually log the same thing several ways.

SonarQube: S6664

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { log.LogInformation("a"); log.LogInformation("b"); log.LogInformation("c"); log.LogInformation("d"); log.LogInformation("e"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { log.LogInformation("a"); log.LogInformation("b"); } }
```

## cs/exception-as-template-argument

**Exceptions should be passed to the logger as the exception argument.** An exception passed after the message template is only formatted into the text; the logger's exception parameter keeps its type and stack trace.

SonarQube: S6668

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError("Failed: {Error}", e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { log.LogError(e, "Failed"); } } }
```

## cs/raw-request-data-in-controller

**Controllers should use model binding instead of reading the raw request.** Reading Request.Form, Query or RouteValues by hand skips validation, type conversion and API descriptions, which model binding gives for free.

SonarQube: S6932

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpGet] public IActionResult Find() { var id = Request.Query["id"]; return Ok(id); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpGet] public IActionResult Find([FromQuery] int id) { return Ok(id); } }
```

## cs/datetime-primary-key

**Dates should not be used as primary keys.** Two rows created in the same tick get the same key, and the value changes meaning with time zones and clock adjustments.

SonarQube: S3363

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Reading { [Key] public DateTime Taken { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Reading { public int Id { get; set; } public DateTime Taken { get; set; } }
```

## cs/optional-not-passed-to-base

**Optional parameters should be passed on to base calls.** An override that calls `base.M(a)` without its own optional `b` makes the base use its default, whatever the caller passed.

SonarQube: S3466

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class B : A { public override void Save(string path, bool force = false) { base.Save(path); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class B : A { public override void Save(string path, bool force = false) { base.Save(path, force); } }
```

## cs/invalid-regex

**Regular expressions should be syntactically valid.** An invalid pattern throws an ArgumentException when the Regex is created, often only on the code path that tests miss.

SonarQube: S5856 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return Regex.IsMatch(s, "(ab"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return Regex.IsMatch(s, "(ab)"); } }
```

## cs/unsafe-csp

**Content Security Policies should be restrictive.** 'unsafe-inline', 'unsafe-eval' and wildcard sources let injected scripts run, which is what the policy is there to stop.

SonarQube: S7039

Standards: [CWE-693](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/693.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpResponse r) { r.Headers.Append("Content-Security-Policy", "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpResponse r) { r.Headers.Append("Content-Security-Policy", "script-src 'self'"); } }
```

## cs/xml-signature-unverified-key

**XML signatures should be checked against a trusted key.** SignedXml.CheckSignature() without arguments trusts the key embedded in the document, so anyone can sign a forged document with their own key.

SonarQube: S6377 (related)

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(SignedXml xml) { return xml.CheckSignature(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(SignedXml xml, AsymmetricAlgorithm key) { return xml.CheckSignature(key); } }
```

## cs/unchecked-sum

**Overflow checking should not be disabled for Sum.** Enumerable.Sum throws on overflow on purpose; inside `unchecked` that protection still applies, so the block suggests a wrap-around that won't happen.

SonarQube: S2291

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] xs) { return unchecked(xs.Sum()); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] xs) { return xs.Sum(); } }
```

## cs/caller-info-passed

**Caller information arguments should not be passed explicitly.** [CallerMemberName] and the like fill in the caller's details; passing a value replaces them with whatever was written, which drifts as code moves.

SonarQube: S3236

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(string m, [CallerMemberName] string c = "") { } void F() { Log("x", "F"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(string m, [CallerMemberName] string c = "") { } void F() { Log("x"); } }
```

## cs/bitwise-on-non-flags-enum

**Bitwise operations should only combine [Flags] enums.** Values of an enum without [Flags] aren't bits; `Color.Red | Color.Blue` is just another number, possibly another member.

SonarQube: S3265 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum Color { Red, Green, Blue } class A { Color F() => Color.Red | Color.Blue; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Flags] enum Colors { None = 0, Red = 1, Green = 2, Blue = 4 } class A { Colors F() => Colors.Red | Colors.Blue; }
```

## cs/array-for-params

**Arrays should not be created for params parameters.** A `params` parameter takes the values themselves; wrapping them in `new[] { ... }` is noise.

SonarQube: S3878 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(params string[] parts) { } void F() { Log(new[] { "a", "b" }); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(params string[] parts) { } void F() { Log("a", "b"); } }
```

## cs/arglist

**params should be used instead of __arglist.** `__arglist` is an undocumented, non-CLS feature; `params` arrays do the same in a way every .NET language and tool understands.

SonarQube: S4061

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(string f, __arglist) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Log(string f, params object[] args) { } }
```

## cs/azure-function-static-state

**Azure Functions should be stateless.** Static state is shared by every invocation on an instance and lost when the host scales out or restarts, so results depend on which instance ran.

SonarQube: S6419

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class F { static int calls; [FunctionName("Run")] public void Run() { calls++; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public void Run(ICounter counter) { counter.Increment(); } }
```

## cs/order-before-where

**Collections should be filtered before they're sorted.** `OrderBy(...).Where(...)` sorts elements the filter then throws away; filtering first sorts fewer.

SonarQube: S6607

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(int[] xs) { return xs.OrderBy(x => x).Where(x => x > 0); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(int[] xs) { return xs.Where(x => x > 0).OrderBy(x => x); } }
```

## cs/concurrent-dictionary-capture

**ConcurrentDictionary factories should use their key parameter.** A factory lambda that ignores its key and uses a captured variable instead allocates a closure on every call; the key parameter is the same value.

SonarQube: S6612

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(ConcurrentDictionary<string, int> d, string key) { return d.GetOrAdd(key, k => key.Length); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(ConcurrentDictionary<string, int> d, string key) { return d.GetOrAdd(key, k => k.Length); } }
```

## cs/blocking-call-in-async

**Async methods should await, not block.** `.Result`, `.Wait()` and Thread.Sleep in an async method hold a thread while waiting and can deadlock; the awaitable form doesn't.

SonarQube: S6966 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { Thread.Sleep(100); await G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { await Task.Delay(100); await G(); } }
```

## cs/modelstate-not-checked

**Controller actions that accept input should check ModelState.IsValid.** Model binding records validation errors instead of throwing; an action that doesn't check ModelState goes on with invalid input. [ApiController] does it automatically.

SonarQube: S6967 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class OrdersController : Controller { [HttpPost] public IActionResult Create(Order o) { Save(o); return Ok(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class OrdersController : Controller { [HttpPost] public IActionResult Create(Order o) { if (!ModelState.IsValid) { return BadRequest(); } Save(o); return Ok(); } }
```

## cs/field-keyword-conflict

**Identifiers in property accessors should not be named field.** Since C# 14, `field` in an accessor means the property's backing field, so a local, parameter or member named `field` changes meaning there.

SonarQube: S8367

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int field; public int X { get { return field; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int value_; public int X { get { return value_; } } }
```

## cs/extension-keyword-conflict

**Types should not be named extension.** Since C# 14, `extension` starts an extension block, so a type with that name needs escaping as @extension.

SonarQube: S8368

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class extension { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Extension { }
```

## cs/partial-return-type

**Return types named partial should be escaped.** Since C# 14, `partial` before a method name starts a partial member, so a return type named `partial` must be written @partial.

SonarQube: S8380

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { partial Make() => null; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { @partial Make() => null; }
```

## cs/scoped-lambda-parameter

**Lambda parameters named scoped should be escaped.** Since C# 14, `scoped` in a parenthesized lambda parameter list is a modifier, so a parameter or type named `scoped` must be written @scoped.

SonarQube: S8381

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Func<int, int> f = (int scoped) => scoped; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Func<int, int> f = (int @scoped) => @scoped; } }
```

## cs/member-name-pascal-case

**Methods and properties should be named in PascalCase.** C# methods and properties are named in PascalCase, with acronyms of more than two letters written like words.

Off by default: add `"cs/member-name-pascal-case"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S100

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void loadData() { G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void LoadData() { G(); } }
```

## cs/console-output

**Console output should not be used for logging.** Console.Write and Console.WriteLine can't be filtered, formatted or routed like a logger's output.

Off by default: add `"cs/console-output"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S106

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Console.WriteLine("saved"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(ILogger log) { log.LogInformation("saved"); } }
```

## cs/magic-number

**Magic numbers should not be used.** A bare number says nothing about what it means; a named constant does, and keeps every use of it in step.

Off by default: add `"cs/magic-number"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S109

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int n) { return n > 42; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Limit = 42; bool F(int n) { return n > Limit; } }
```

## cs/missing-braces

**Control structures should use braces.** Without braces, a statement added under an if or loop silently isn't part of it.

Off by default: add `"cs/missing-braces"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S121

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) { if (b) G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) { if (b) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/statements-same-line

**Statements should be on separate lines.** Two statements on one line are easy to read as one, and diffs can't show which changed.

Off by default: add `"cs/statements-same-line"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S122

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() {
 G(); H();
} }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() {
 G();
 H();
} }
```

## cs/if-chain-without-else

**if ... else if chains should end with else.** A final `else` shows the remaining case was considered, even when it only says nothing happens.

Off by default: add `"cs/if-chain-without-else"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S126

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { if (x > 0) { G(); } else if (x < 0) { H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { if (x > 0) { G(); } else if (x < 0) { H(); } else { K(); } } }
```

## cs/switch-without-default

**switch statements should have a default clause.** A `default` shows the other values were considered, and catches new ones added later.

Off by default: add `"cs/switch-without-default"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S131

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); break; default: break; } } }
```

## cs/deep-nesting

**Control flow statements should not be nested too deeply.** Code nested more than three levels deep is hard to follow; early returns or extracted methods flatten it.

Off by default: add `"cs/deep-nesting"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S134

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b, bool c, bool d) { if (a) { if (b) { if (c) { if (d) { G(); } } } } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b, bool c, bool d) { if (!a || !b) { return; } if (c && d) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/increment-in-expression

**Increments should not be used inside other expressions.** `a[i++] = b[++j]` packs several changes in one line; separate statements say in which order they happen.

Off by default: add `"cs/increment-in-expression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S881

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] a, int i) { return a[i++]; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int[] a, int i) { int v = a[i]; i++; return v; } }
```

## cs/complex-condition

**Conditions should not combine too many operators.** More than three && and || in one expression are hard to read; named variables or a method make each part clear.

Off by default: add `"cs/complex-condition"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1067

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool a, bool b, bool c, bool d, bool e) { return a && b || c && d || e; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool a, bool b, bool c) { return a && b || c; } }
```

## cs/closing-brace-position

**A closing brace should start its line.** A `}` after code on the same line hides where the block ends.

Off by default: add `"cs/closing-brace-position"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1109

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() {
 G(); }
}
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() {
 G();
 }
}
```

## cs/environment-exit

**Environment.Exit should not be called.** Exiting the process from inside the code skips finally blocks and the host's shutdown, and makes the code impossible to reuse or test.

Off by default: add `"cs/environment-exit"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1147

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Environment.Exit(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new InvalidOperationException(); } }
```

## cs/long-switch-section

**switch sections should not have too many statements.** A case with more than eight statements belongs in a method of its own.

Off by default: add `"cs/long-switch-section"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1151

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); G(); G(); G(); G(); G(); G(); G(); G(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: Handle(); break; } } }
```

## cs/too-few-switch-cases

**switch statements should have at least three cases.** A switch with one or two cases reads more simply as an if.

Off by default: add `"cs/too-few-switch-cases"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1301

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); break; default: H(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { if (x == 1) { G(); } else { H(); } } }
```

## cs/in-source-suppression

**Suppressed warnings should be tracked.** `#pragma warning disable` and [SuppressMessage] silence a warning for good; listing them keeps them visible for review.

Off by default: add `"cs/in-source-suppression"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1309

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A {
#pragma warning disable CS0168
 void F() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { } }
```

## cs/logger-not-static-readonly

**Logger fields should be private static readonly.** One logger per type, never reassigned or shared outside it, is the convention most logging libraries expect.

Off by default: add `"cs/logger-not-static-readonly"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1312 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public ILog Log = LogManager.GetLogger(typeof(A)); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private static readonly ILog Log = LogManager.GetLogger(typeof(A)); }
```

## cs/culture-sensitive-case

**Case conversion should state its culture.** ToLower() and ToUpper() use the current culture, so "I".ToLower() isn't "i" in Turkish. Say which culture is meant.

Off by default: add `"cs/culture-sensitive-case"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1449 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { return s.ToLower(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { return s.ToLowerInvariant(); } }
```

## cs/multiple-declarations

**Each declaration should declare one variable.** `int a, b = 0;` reads as if both were 0; one declaration per line says what each is.

Off by default: add `"cs/multiple-declarations"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1659

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int a, b; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int a; int b; }
```

## cs/nested-switch

**switch statements should not be nested.** A switch inside another is hard to follow; the inner one belongs in a method.

Off by default: add `"cs/nested-switch"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1821

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x, int y) { switch (x) { case 1: switch (y) { case 2: G(); break; } break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x, int y) { switch (x) { case 1: Handle(y); break; } } }
```

## cs/large-number-without-separators

**Large numbers should use digit separators.** `1_000_000` is easier to read, and to get right, than `1000000`.

Off by default: add `"cs/large-number-without-separators"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2148

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Max = 1000000; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Max = 1_000_000; }
```

## cs/modulus-equality

**Remainders should not be compared with 1 or -1.** `x % 2 == 1` is false for negative odd numbers, whose remainder is -1; `x % 2 != 0` holds for both.

Off by default: add `"cs/modulus-equality"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2197

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool Odd(int x) { return x % 2 == 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool Odd(int x) { return x % 2 != 0; } }
```

## cs/catch-exception

**Exception should not be caught.** Catching every exception also catches bugs and conditions the code can't recover from. Catch the exceptions the code expects.

Off by default: add `"cs/catch-exception"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2221

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (Exception e) { Log(e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); } } }
```

## cs/use-nameof

**nameof should be used for parameter names.** A parameter name written as a string goes out of date silently when the parameter is renamed; nameof(x) is checked by the compiler.

Off by default: add `"cs/use-nameof"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2302

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string name) { if (name == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException("name"); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string name) { if (name == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(name)); } } }
```

## cs/public-constant

**Public constants should be static readonly fields.** A const is copied into every assembly that uses it, so changing its value means recompiling them all; a static readonly field is read at run time.

Off by default: add `"cs/public-constant"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2339

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public const int Version = 2; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public static readonly int Version = 2; }
```

## cs/non-private-field

**Fields should be private.** A protected or internal field lets other code change it without the type knowing; a property keeps that under control.

Off by default: add `"cs/non-private-field"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2357

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { protected int count; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private int count; protected int Count => count; }
```

## cs/optional-parameter

**Optional parameters should not be used in public APIs.** A default value is compiled into every caller, so changing it later doesn't reach them until they're recompiled. Overloads don't have that problem.

Off by default: add `"cs/optional-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2360

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Save(string path, bool force = false) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Save(string path) => Save(path, false); public void Save(string path, bool force) { } }
```

## cs/repeated-condition-in-sequence

**Consecutive if statements should not test the same condition.** Two ifs in a row with the same condition are one if with both bodies, or one of them tests the wrong thing.

Off by default: add `"cs/repeated-condition-in-sequence"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2760

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a) { if (a) { G(); } if (a) { H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a) { if (a) { G(); H(); } } }
```

## cs/default-value-initializer

**Fields should not be initialized to their default value.** Fields already start at 0, false or null; writing it again is noise.

Off by default: add `"cs/default-value-initializer"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3052

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int count = 0; bool done = false; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int count; bool done; }
```

## cs/missing-configure-await

**Library code should use ConfigureAwait(false).** Without it, the continuation tries to resume on the caller's synchronization context, which costs time and can deadlock a caller that blocks.

Off by default: add `"cs/missing-configure-await"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3216

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { await G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { await G().ConfigureAwait(false); } }
```

## cs/suppress-finalize-without-finalizer

**GC.SuppressFinalize should only be called for types with finalizers.** In a sealed type without a finalizer there is nothing to suppress, so the call only adds noise.

Off by default: add `"cs/suppress-finalize-without-finalizer"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3234

Noncompliant:

```csharp
sealed class A : IDisposable { public void Dispose() { GC.SuppressFinalize(this); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class A : IDisposable { public void Dispose() { Close(); } }
```

## cs/redundant-parentheses-around-value

**Returned and assigned values should not be in parentheses.** `return (x);` and `y = (x);` don't need the parentheses.

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-parentheses-around-value"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3235

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return (a + 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return a + 1; } }
```

## cs/could-be-const

**Locals that never change should be const.** `const` says the value is fixed, and the compiler makes sure of it.

Off by default: add `"cs/could-be-const"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3353

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { int factor = 3; return x * factor; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int x) { const int factor = 3; return x * factor; } }
```

## cs/this-escapes-constructor

**Constructors should not pass this to other code.** Code that receives `this` before the constructor ends sees a half-initialized object, and a subclass's fields not set at all.

Off by default: add `"cs/this-escapes-constructor"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3366

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public A(Registry r) { r.Add(this); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public static A Create(Registry r) { var a = new A(); r.Add(a); return a; } }
```

## cs/logger-for-other-type

**Loggers should be named for their enclosing type.** A logger created for another type files this type's messages under the wrong name.

Off by default: add `"cs/logger-for-other-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3416

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Orders { static readonly ILog Log = LogManager.GetLogger(typeof(Carts)); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Orders { static readonly ILog Log = LogManager.GetLogger(typeof(Orders)); }
```

## cs/empty-default-clause

**Empty default clauses should be removed.** A `default: break;` does what leaving it out does.

Off by default: add `"cs/empty-default-clause"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3532

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); break; default: break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: G(); break; } } }
```

## cs/not-implemented

**NotImplementedException should be tracked.** A NotImplementedException marks unfinished code that will fail when called.

Off by default: add `"cs/not-implemented"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3717

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new NotImplementedException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new NotSupportedException(); } }
```

## cs/parameter-named-like-method

**Parameters should not be named like their method.** `Format(string format)` makes the body harder to read, since the name means two things.

Off by default: add `"cs/parameter-named-like-method"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3872

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Login(string login) { G(login); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Login(string userName) { G(userName); } }
```

## cs/out-ref-parameter

**out and ref parameters should not be used in public methods.** They make methods harder to call and to compose; returning a tuple or a type is clearer.

Off by default: add `"cs/out-ref-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3874

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Split(string s, out string a, out string b) { a = s; b = s; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public (string, string) Split(string s) => (s, s); }
```

## cs/empty-finalizer

**Finalizers should not be empty.** An empty finalizer puts every instance on the finalization queue for nothing, which slows garbage collection.

Off by default: add `"cs/empty-finalizer"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3880

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { Release(); } }
```

## cs/get-executing-assembly

**Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly should not be called.** It's slower than typeof(T).Assembly and returns another assembly when the code is inlined into a caller.

Off by default: add `"cs/get-executing-assembly"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3902

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Assembly F() { return Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Assembly F() { return typeof(A).Assembly; } }
```

## cs/static-readonly-could-be-const

**static readonly fields with constant values should be const.** A private static readonly field set to a literal is a constant; `const` lets the compiler use it as one.

Off by default: add `"cs/static-readonly-could-be-const"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3962

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private static readonly int Max = 10; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private const int Max = 10; }
```

## cs/multidimensional-array

**Multidimensional arrays should not be used.** Jagged arrays (`int[][]`) are faster in .NET and easier to work with than `int[,]`.

Off by default: add `"cs/multidimensional-array"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3967

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int[,] grid = new int[3, 3]; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int[][] grid = new int[3][]; }
```

## cs/reserved-enum-member

**Enum members should not be named Reserved.** A placeholder member is never removed safely: adding the real one later is a breaking change anyway.

Off by default: add `"cs/reserved-enum-member"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4016

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum Mode { On, Off, Reserved }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Mode { On, Off }
```

## cs/enum-storage-not-int

**Enums should use Int32 storage.** A smaller storage type saves little and gets in the way of interop and of adding members later.

Off by default: add `"cs/enum-storage-not-int"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4022

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum Color : byte { Red, Green }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Color { Red, Green }
```

## cs/empty-interface

**Interfaces should not be empty.** An empty interface only marks types; an attribute does that without an empty contract.

Off by default: add `"cs/empty-interface"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4023

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface IMarker { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface IShape { double Area(); }
```

## cs/parameter-overwritten

**Parameters should not be overwritten before they're read.** Assigning a parameter before reading it throws away the caller's argument; the method probably meant a local.

Off by default: add `"cs/parameter-overwritten"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1226 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int limit) { limit = 10; return limit * 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int limit) { limit = Math.Min(limit, 10); return limit * 2; } }
```

## cs/disposable-fields-without-idisposable

**Classes with disposable fields should be disposable.** A class that creates a stream, timer or client and keeps it in a field must dispose of it, which callers can only trigger through IDisposable.

Off by default: add `"cs/disposable-fields-without-idisposable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2931 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Log { private readonly FileStream file = File.OpenWrite("log"); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Log : IDisposable { private readonly FileStream file = File.OpenWrite("log"); public void Dispose() => file.Dispose(); }
```

## cs/lock-on-local

**Locks should not be taken on local variables.** Each call has its own local, so a lock on it never makes another thread wait.

Off by default: add `"cs/lock-on-local"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6507

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { var sync = new object(); lock (sync) { G(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { readonly object sync = new object(); void F() { lock (sync) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/redundant-to-string

**ToString() should not be called when concatenating.** String concatenation and interpolation already call ToString().

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-to-string"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1858 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int n) { return "n = " + n.ToString(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int n) { return "n = " + n; } }
```

## cs/use-null-coalescing

**?? should be used instead of a null-checking conditional.** `a != null ? a : b` is `a ?? b`.

Off by default: add `"cs/use-null-coalescing"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3240 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string a) { return a != null ? a : ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string a) { return a ?? ""; } }
```

## cs/default-argument-passed

**Default parameter values should not be passed explicitly.** Passing the default value says nothing the declaration doesn't, and stops following a change of the default.

Off by default: add `"cs/default-argument-passed"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3254 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Save(string p, bool force = false) { } void F() { Save("x", false); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Save(string p, bool force = false) { } void F() { Save("x"); } }
```

## cs/explicit-delegate-creation

**Delegates should not be created explicitly.** `new EventHandler(OnClick)` is what `OnClick` alone becomes; the explicit form is noise.

Off by default: add `"cs/explicit-delegate-creation"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3257 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Clicked += new EventHandler(OnClick); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Clicked += OnClick; } }
```

## cs/redundant-anonymous-property-name

**Anonymous type properties should not repeat their value's name.** `new { Name = p.Name }` is `new { p.Name }`; the compiler takes the name from the member.

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-anonymous-property-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3441

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(P p) { return new { Name = p.Name }; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(P p) { return new { p.Name, Total = p.Count }; } }
```

## cs/indexer-parameter-type

**Indexers should take strings or integers.** An indexer looks like an array or dictionary lookup; other parameter types make a method clearer.

Off by default: add `"cs/indexer-parameter-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3876

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int this[double d] => 0; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int this[int i] => 0; }
```

## cs/struct-without-iequatable

**Structs should implement IEquatable<T>.** Without it, Equals(object) boxes the struct and compares field by field through reflection, which is slow.

Off by default: add `"cs/struct-without-iequatable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3898 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
struct Point { public int X; public override bool Equals(object o) => false; public override int GetHashCode() => X; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
struct Point : IEquatable<Point> { public int X; public bool Equals(Point p) => p.X == X; public override bool Equals(object o) => o is Point p && Equals(p); public override int GetHashCode() => X; }
```

## cs/custom-event-handler-delegate

**Events should use EventHandler<T>.** A custom delegate with the (sender, args) shape is EventHandler<TArgs> written by hand.

Off by default: add `"cs/custom-event-handler-delegate"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3908 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
delegate void SavedHandler(object sender, SavedEventArgs e); class A { public event SavedHandler Saved; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler<SavedEventArgs> Saved; }
```

## cs/non-generic-collection

**Collections should implement the generic interfaces.** A collection that only implements IEnumerable or ICollection hands out objects, so every caller casts and LINQ can't infer the element type.

Off by default: add `"cs/non-generic-collection"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3909

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Bag : IEnumerable { public IEnumerator GetEnumerator() => null; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Bag : IEnumerable<int> { public IEnumerator<int> GetEnumerator() => null; IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator() => GetEnumerator(); }
```

## cs/irregular-digit-groups

**Digit separators should group regularly.** `1_00_000` hides the magnitude the separators are there to show.

Off by default: add `"cs/irregular-digit-groups"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3937

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Max = 1_00_000; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Max = 100_000; }
```

## cs/list-in-public-api

**List<T> should not be exposed by public APIs.** List<T> can't be extended or observed; a public API that exposes it can't change that later. Use IList<T>, IReadOnlyList<T> or Collection<T>.

Off by default: add `"cs/list-in-public-api"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3956

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public List<int> Items { get; } = new(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public IReadOnlyList<int> Items { get; } = new List<int>(); }
```

## cs/string-uri-parameter

**URI parameters should be System.Uri.** A Uri is validated when it's created and offers the parts of the address; a string is checked nowhere.
