Off by default: add `"cs/string-uri-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3994

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Fetch(string url) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Fetch(Uri url) { } }
```

## cs/string-uri-return

**Methods returning URIs should return System.Uri.** Callers of a method named like a URL getter expect a Uri, with its parts and validation.

Off by default: add `"cs/string-uri-return"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3995

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public string GetUrl() => "https://a.example"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public Uri GetUrl() => new Uri("https://a.example"); }
```

## cs/string-uri-property

**URI properties should be System.Uri.** A Uri property is validated when set and offers the parts of the address; a string isn't.

Off by default: add `"cs/string-uri-property"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3996

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public string HomeUrl { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public Uri HomeUrl { get; set; } }
```

## cs/settable-collection-property

**Collection properties should be read-only.** A settable collection property lets callers replace the whole collection, which is rarely wanted; they can still add and remove through the getter.

Off by default: add `"cs/settable-collection-property"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4004

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public List<int> Items { get; set; } = new(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public List<int> Items { get; } = new(); }
```

## cs/nested-generic-signature

**Public signatures should not nest generic types.** `List<Dictionary<string, int>>` in a public signature is hard to read and to call; a named type says what it holds.

Off by default: add `"cs/nested-generic-signature"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4017

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Load(List<Dictionary<string, int>> rows) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { public void Load(IEnumerable<Row> rows) { } }
```

## cs/uninferable-type-parameter

**Generic methods should let callers infer their type parameters.** A type parameter that no parameter uses must always be written out by the caller.

Off by default: add `"cs/uninferable-type-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4018

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { T Read<T>(string key) => default; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { T Read<T>(string key, T fallback) => fallback; }
```

## cs/exception-standard-constructors

**Exceptions should provide the standard constructors.** Callers and serializers expect (), (string message) and (string message, Exception inner).

Off by default: add `"cs/exception-standard-constructors"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4027

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class QuotaException : Exception { public QuotaException(string m) : base(m) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class QuotaException : Exception { public QuotaException() { } public QuotaException(string m) : base(m) { } public QuotaException(string m, Exception i) : base(m, i) { } }
```

## cs/normalize-to-upper

**Strings should be normalized to upper case.** Some characters don't round-trip through lower case; upper-case normalization is the safe one for comparisons.

Off by default: add `"cs/normalize-to-upper"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4040

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { return s.ToLowerInvariant(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { return s.ToUpperInvariant(); } }
```

## cs/type-named-like-namespace

**Types should not be named like well-known namespaces.** A type called `Collections` or `Linq` collides with the namespace in using directives and confuses readers.

Off by default: add `"cs/type-named-like-namespace"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4041

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Collections { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class CollectionHelpers { }
```

## cs/ref-object-parameter

**Generics should be used instead of ref object parameters.** A `ref object` parameter takes any type and makes callers cast; a generic `ref T` keeps the type.

Off by default: add `"cs/ref-object-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4047

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Swap(ref object a, ref object b) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Swap<T>(ref T a, ref T b) { } }
```

## cs/get-method-could-be-property

**Simple getter methods should be properties.** A public `GetName()` without parameters or side effects reads better as a `Name` property.

Off by default: add `"cs/get-method-could-be-property"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4049

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class A { string name; public string GetName() { return name; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class A { string name; public string Name => name; }
```

## cs/string-comparison-overload

**String comparisons should state how they compare.** Without a StringComparison, Compare, StartsWith, EndsWith and IndexOf(string) depend on the current culture.

Off by default: add `"cs/string-comparison-overload"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4058 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.StartsWith("abc"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s.StartsWith("abc", StringComparison.Ordinal); } }
```

## cs/property-and-get-method

**Types should not have both a property X and a method GetX.** Two ways to read the same thing make readers wonder how they differ.

Off by default: add `"cs/property-and-get-method"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4059

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public int Count => 1; public int GetCount() => 1; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public int Count => 1; }
```

## cs/attribute-not-sealed

**Attribute classes should be sealed.** Looking up a sealed attribute is faster, and attributes are rarely designed to be inherited.

Off by default: add `"cs/attribute-not-sealed"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4060

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class RetryAttribute : Attribute { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class RetryAttribute : Attribute { }
```

## cs/operator-without-named-alternative

**Operator overloads should have named alternatives.** Languages without operator overloading can only call `Add` or `Subtract`, not `+` or `-`.

Off by default: add `"cs/operator-without-named-alternative"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4069

Noncompliant:

```csharp
struct Money { public static Money operator +(Money a, Money b) => a; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
struct Money { public static Money operator +(Money a, Money b) => Add(a, b); public static Money Add(Money a, Money b) => a; }
```

## cs/extension-on-object

**Extension methods should not extend object.** An extension on object shows up on every value in the codebase, including where it makes no sense.

Off by default: add `"cs/extension-on-object"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4225

Noncompliant:

```csharp
static class X { public static string Dump(this object o) => o.ToString(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
static class X { public static string Dump(this Order o) => o.ToString(); }
```

## cs/async-method-name

**Asynchronous methods should end with Async.** The suffix tells callers to await the result, and sets it apart from a synchronous method of the same name.

Off by default: add `"cs/async-method-name"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4261 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task Save() { await G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task SaveAsync() { await G(); } }
```

## cs/async-argument-validation

**Async methods should validate their arguments before the first await.** An async method's exceptions end up in the returned task, so an argument check throws only when the task is awaited, far from the bad call.

Off by default: add `"cs/async-argument-validation"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4457

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F(string s) { if (s == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s)); } await G(s); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Task F(string s) { if (s == null) { throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s)); } return FCore(s); } async Task FCore(string s) { await G(s); } }
```

## cs/blocking-async-call

**Async calls should not be blocked on.** `.Result`, `.Wait()` and `.GetAwaiter().GetResult()` hold a thread while the task runs, and can deadlock where a synchronization context is in use.

Off by default: add `"cs/blocking-async-call"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4462 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(HttpClient h) { return h.GetStringAsync("https://a.example").Result; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task<string> F(HttpClient h) { return await h.GetStringAsync("https://a.example"); } }
```

## cs/untestable-clock

**Code should take the current time from a provider.** DateTime.Now and UtcNow can't be controlled in tests; a TimeProvider or clock interface can.

Off by default: add `"cs/untestable-clock"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6354

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool Expired(DateTime at) { return at < DateTime.UtcNow; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool Expired(DateTime at, TimeProvider clock) { return at < clock.GetUtcNow(); } }
```

## cs/exclude-from-coverage-without-justification

**[ExcludeFromCodeCoverage] should give a justification.** The justification tells reviewers why the code doesn't need tests.

Off by default: add `"cs/exclude-from-coverage-without-justification"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6513

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[ExcludeFromCodeCoverage] class Startup { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[ExcludeFromCodeCoverage(Justification = "Composition root, covered by integration tests")] class Startup { }
```

## cs/local-time-recorded

**Instants should be recorded in UTC.** DateTime.Now changes with daylight saving and the server's zone, so stored or compared times drift. UtcNow doesn't.

Off by default: add `"cs/local-time-recorded"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6563

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime Stamp() { return DateTime.Now; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime Stamp() { return DateTime.UtcNow; } }
```

## cs/datetime-instead-of-offset

**DateTimeOffset should be used instead of DateTime.** A DateTime doesn't carry its offset, so the same value can mean different instants; a DateTimeOffset can't be misread that way.

Off by default: add `"cs/datetime-instead-of-offset"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6566

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTime Stamp() { return DateTime.UtcNow; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DateTimeOffset Stamp() { return DateTimeOffset.UtcNow; } }
```

## cs/hardcoded-date-format

**Date formats should not be hard-coded.** A fixed format such as "MM/dd/yyyy" reads wrong for users of other cultures; standard format specifiers adapt.

Off by default: add `"cs/hardcoded-date-format"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6585 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(DateTime d) { return d.ToString("MM/dd/yyyy"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(DateTime d) { return d.ToString("d"); } }
```

## cs/field-disposed-outside-dispose

**Fields should be disposed of in the class's own Dispose method.** Dispose is where callers expect a class to clean up; disposing of a field from another method leaks it whenever that method isn't called.

Off by default: add `"cs/field-disposed-outside-dispose"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2952

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Holder : IDisposable { FileStream fs; public void CleanUp() { fs.Dispose(); } public void Dispose() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Holder : IDisposable { FileStream fs; public void Dispose() { fs.Dispose(); } }
```

## cs/unnecessary-using

**Unnecessary using directives should be removed.** A repeated using directive, or one for the namespace the code is already in, imports nothing.

Off by default: add `"cs/unnecessary-using"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1128 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
using System;
using System;
class A { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
using System;
class A { }
```

## cs/class-coupling

**Classes should not depend on too many other types.** A class that uses dozens of types likely has more than one responsibility.

Off by default: add `"cs/class-coupling"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1200

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Dep1 a1; Dep2 a2; Dep3 a3; Dep4 a4; Dep5 a5; Dep6 a6; Dep7 a7; Dep8 a8; Dep9 a9; Dep10 a10; Dep11 a11; Dep12 a12; Dep13 a13; Dep14 a14; Dep15 a15; Dep16 a16; Dep17 a17; Dep18 a18; Dep19 a19; Dep20 a20; Dep21 a21; Dep22 a22; Dep23 a23; Dep24 a24; Dep25 a25; Dep26 a26; Dep27 a27; Dep28 a28; Dep29 a29; Dep30 a30; Dep31 a31; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Dep1 a1; }
```

## cs/method-complexity

**Methods and properties should not be too complex.** Every branch is another path to understand and test; past 10 the code is hard to follow.

Off by default: add `"cs/method-complexity"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1541

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) {} if (a == 2) {} if (a == 3) {} if (a == 4) {} if (a == 5) {} if (a == 6) {} if (a == 7) {} if (a == 8) {} if (a == 9) {} if (a == 10) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) {} } }
```

## cs/mergeable-try

**Consecutive try statements with the same handlers should be merged.** Two try statements in a row that catch and clean up the same way are one try statement written twice.

Off by default: add `"cs/mergeable-try"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2327

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); } try { H(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); H(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); } } }
```

## cs/array-covariance

**Array covariance should not be used.** An `object[]` that holds a `string[]` compiles, but storing anything but a string in it throws ArrayTypeMismatchException at run time.

Off by default: add `"cs/array-covariance"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2330

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object[] items = new string[3]; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object[] items = new object[3]; }
```

## cs/redundant-modifier

**Redundant modifiers should not be used.** `sealed` on a member of a sealed class and `unsafe` inside an unsafe type change nothing.

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-modifier"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2333 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
sealed class B : A { public sealed override void F() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class B : A { public override void F() { } }
```

## cs/member-more-visible-than-type

**Members should not be more visible than their type.** A public member of an internal type is no more reachable than an internal one; the modifier only misleads.

Off by default: add `"cs/member-more-visible-than-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3059

Noncompliant:

```csharp
internal class Counter { public int GetOne() => 1; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
internal class Counter { internal int GetOne() => 1; }
```

## cs/interface-downcast

**Interface references should not be cast to concrete types.** Casting an interface to a class means the interface is missing something, and the cast fails for every other implementation.

Off by default: add `"cs/interface-downcast"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3215 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(IShape s) { var c = (Circle)s; return c.Radius; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(IShape s) { return s.Size; } }
```

## cs/event-handler-signature

**Events should use the (sender, args) handler signature.** Handlers that take (object sender, TEventArgs e) can be shared and extended; an Action or Func event can't add data later without breaking subscribers.

Off by default: add `"cs/event-handler-signature"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3906 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public event Action<int> Changed; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler<ChangedEventArgs> Changed; }
```

## cs/string-uri-overload-not-delegating

**String URI overloads should call the System.Uri overload.** When a method has string and Uri overloads, the string one should only convert and delegate, so the parsing and validation live in one place.

Off by default: add `"cs/string-uri-overload-not-delegating"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3997

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Fetch(string uri) { Download(uri); } public void Fetch(Uri uri) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Fetch(string uri) { Fetch(new Uri(uri)); } public void Fetch(Uri uri) { } }
```

## cs/visible-pointer-field

**Pointers to unmanaged memory should not be visible.** A public or protected writable IntPtr or pointer field lets any caller point the class at arbitrary memory.

Off by default: add `"cs/visible-pointer-field"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4000

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class Native { public IntPtr Handle; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class Native { private IntPtr handle; }
```

## cs/disposable-handle-without-finalizer

**Disposable types holding native handles should have finalizers.** If callers forget Dispose, only a finalizer releases the native resource behind an IntPtr field.

Off by default: add `"cs/disposable-handle-without-finalizer"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4002

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Native : IDisposable { private IntPtr handle; public void Dispose() { Free(handle); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Native : IDisposable { private IntPtr handle; ~Native() { Free(handle); } public void Dispose() { Free(handle); GC.SuppressFinalize(this); } }
```

## cs/field-differs-from-base-by-case

**Fields should not differ from base class fields only by case.** `plantingseason` next to an inherited `plantingSeason` is bound to be confused with it.

Off by default: add `"cs/field-differs-from-base-by-case"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4025

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Fruit { protected string plantingSeason; }
class Raspberry : Fruit { protected string plantingseason; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Fruit { protected string plantingSeason; }
class Raspberry : Fruit { protected string whenToPlant; }
```

## cs/explicit-implementation-hidden

**Explicit interface implementations should be reachable from derived types.** A derived class can only call an explicit interface member through the interface, and loses it entirely if it re-implements the member.

Off by default: add `"cs/explicit-implementation-hidden"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4039

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class Store : IStore { void IStore.Save() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class Store : IStore { void IStore.Save() => Save(); protected void Save() { } }
```

## cs/culture-overload

**Formatting should state its culture.** string.Format and ToString(format) without an IFormatProvider use the current culture, so the output changes from machine to machine.

Off by default: add `"cs/culture-overload"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4056 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(decimal d) { return string.Format("{0:N2}", d); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(decimal d) { return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0:N2}", d); } }
```

## cs/data-table-locale

**DataTable and DataSet locales should be set.** They default to the current culture, so sorting and comparing shared data changes from machine to machine.

Off by default: add `"cs/data-table-locale"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4057

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { DataTable F() { var t = new DataTable("Customers"); return t; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { DataTable F() { var t = new DataTable("Customers"); t.Locale = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture; return t; } }
```

## cs/extension-in-same-namespace

**Extension methods should not be in the namespace of the type they extend.** An extension next to the type it extends could simply be a method of that type.

Off by default: add `"cs/extension-in-same-namespace"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4226 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
namespace Shop { class Order { } static class OrderExtensions { public static int Total(this Order o) => 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
namespace Shop { class Order { public int Total() => 0; } }
```

## cs/azure-function-without-try

**Azure Functions should handle errors at the top level.** A function whose body isn't in a try/catch lets failures escape unlogged, which makes the app's health hard to monitor.

Off by default: add `"cs/azure-function-without-try"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6421

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run(ILogger log) { await Work(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run(ILogger log) { try { await Work(); } catch (Exception e) { log.LogError(e, "Run failed"); throw; } } }
```

## cs/azure-function-catch-not-logged

**Azure Functions should log the failures they catch.** A failure caught without being logged (at Information level or above) never shows up in monitoring.

Off by default: add `"cs/azure-function-catch-not-logged"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6423

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run(ILogger log) { try { await Work(); } catch (Exception e) { throw; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class F { [FunctionName("Run")] public async Task Run(ILogger log) { try { await Work(); } catch (Exception e) { log.LogError(e, "Run failed"); throw; } } }
```

## cs/use-list-find

**List<T>.Find should be used instead of FirstOrDefault.** List<T>.Find does the same without LINQ's enumerator, and is faster.

Off by default: add `"cs/use-list-find"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6602 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.FirstOrDefault(x => x > 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.Find(x => x > 1); } }
```

## cs/use-list-true-for-all

**List<T>.TrueForAll should be used instead of All.** List<T>.TrueForAll does the same without LINQ's enumerator, and is faster.

Off by default: add `"cs/use-list-true-for-all"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6603 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs) { return xs.All(x => x > 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs) { return xs.TrueForAll(x => x > 1); } }
```

## cs/use-list-exists

**List<T>.Exists should be used instead of Any.** List<T>.Exists does the same without LINQ's enumerator, and is faster.

Off by default: add `"cs/use-list-exists"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6605 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs) { return xs.Any(x => x > 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs) { return xs.Exists(x => x > 1); } }
```

## cs/shift-out-of-range

**Integers should not be shifted by zero or by their width or more.** C# masks the shift count: shifting an int by 33 shifts it by 1, and shifting by 0 changes nothing.

SonarQube: S2183

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int i) { return i << 33; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int i) { return i << 1; } }
```

## cs/reference-equals-value-type

**ReferenceEquals should not be used on value types.** A value type is boxed into a new object for each argument, so ReferenceEquals on it is always false.

SonarQube: S2995

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return ReferenceEquals(a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return a == b; } }
```

## cs/shift-dynamic-non-int

**Right operands of shifts on dynamic values should be integers.** With a dynamic left operand the compiler doesn't check the shift count, so a non-integer throws RuntimeBinderException at run time.

SonarQube: S3449

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(dynamic d) { return d >> 5.4; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { object F(dynamic d) { return d >> 5; } }
```

## cs/nullable-get-type-comparison

**GetType() should not be compared with a nullable type.** GetType() on a nullable value returns the underlying type, so comparing it with typeof(int?) is always false, or throws on null.

SonarQube: S3610

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int? v) { return v.GetType() == typeof(int?); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int? v) { return v.GetType() == typeof(int); } }
```

## cs/route-constraint-type

**Component parameters should match their route constraint's type.** Blazor converts a constrained route value to the constraint's type; a parameter of another type fails at run time.

SonarQube: S6800

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Route("/orders/{Id:int}")] class Order : ComponentBase { [Parameter] public string Id { get; set; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Route("/orders/{Id:int}")] class Order : ComponentBase { [Parameter] public int Id { get; set; } }
```

## cs/delegate-chain-subtraction

**Delegate chains should not be subtracted.** Subtracting `a + b` only removes a and b when they appear in that order, next to each other; otherwise nothing is removed.

SonarQube: S3172 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { Action F(Action all, Action a, Action b) { return all - (a + b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { Action F(Action all, Action a, Action b) { return all - a - b; } }
```

## cs/ignored-pure-result

**Results of methods without side effects should be used.** Strings, dates, immutable collections and LINQ return a new value instead of changing the receiver, so calling them without using the result does nothing.

SonarQube: S2201 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { s.Trim(); return s; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string s) { s = s.Trim(); return s; } }
```

## cs/unchecked-stream-read

**The count returned by Stream.Read should be checked.** Read may return fewer bytes than asked for, even before the end of the stream; ignoring the count processes stale buffer contents.

SonarQube: S2674

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Stream s, byte[] b) { s.Read(b, 0, b.Length); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Stream s, byte[] b) { s.ReadExactly(b, 0, b.Length); } }
```

## cs/conflicting-transparency

**Members should not have transparency attributes that conflict with their type's.** Security transparency set on a type and differently on its members is ambiguous, and the type-level one wins, which may expose critical code.

SonarQube: S4211

Standards: [CWE-269](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/269.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[SecuritySafeCritical] public class Foo { [SecurityCritical] public void Bar() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class Foo { [SecurityCritical] public void Bar() { } }
```

## cs/impossible-comparison

**Comparisons with values outside a type's range should not be made.** A byte is never greater than 255 and a char never negative; such a comparison is always true or always false.

SonarQube: S2198

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(byte b) { return b > 300; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(byte b) { return b > 200; } }
```

## cs/foreach-explicit-conversion

**foreach should not cast elements of generic collections.** foreach silently casts each element to the loop variable's type, so a narrower type throws InvalidCastException at run time.

SonarQube: S3217 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<object> items) { foreach (string s in items) { G(s); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(List<object> items) { foreach (var s in items.OfType<string>()) { G(s); } } }
```

## cs/timezone-converter

**Time zones should be looked up without converting their IDs.** TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById accepts both IANA and Windows IDs on every OS, so converting with TimeZoneConverter first is unnecessary.

SonarQube: S6575

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { TimeZoneInfo F() { return TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(TZConvert.IanaToWindows("Asia/Tokyo")); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { TimeZoneInfo F() { return TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById("Asia/Tokyo"); } }
```

## cs/use-string-create

**string.Create should be used instead of FormattableString.** Since .NET 6, string.Create formats with a culture using pooled buffers, allocating less than FormattableString.Invariant or CurrentCulture.

SonarQube: S6618 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int n) { return FormattableString.Invariant($"n = {n}"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(int n) { return string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"n = {n}"); } }
```

## cs/trace-if-switch-level

**Trace.WriteLineIf should not be used with TraceSwitch levels.** `WriteLineIf(sw.TraceError, ...)` evaluates the message whether or not it's written; TraceError and TraceSource categorize and filter properly.

SonarQube: S6675

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(TraceSwitch sw) { Trace.WriteLineIf(sw.TraceError, "failed"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { Trace.TraceError("failed"); } }
```

## cs/value-task-misuse

**ValueTasks should be consumed once.** A ValueTask may reuse its backing object once it completes, so awaiting it twice, or reading its result before it's known to be done, gives wrong results.

SonarQube: S5034 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { ValueTask<int> vt = GetAsync(); var a = await vt; var b = await vt; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task F() { ValueTask<int> vt = GetAsync(); var a = await vt; } }
```

## cs/redundant-cast

**Redundant casts should not be used.** Casting a value to the type it already has does nothing but add noise.

SonarQube: S1905 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int i) { return (int)i; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int i) { return i; } }
```

## cs/indexing-instead-of-linq

**Lists and arrays should be indexed instead of using First, Last or ElementAt.** On a list or an array, `xs[0]` reads the element directly, where First() goes through LINQ.

SonarQube: S6608 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.First(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs[0]; } }
```

## cs/set-min-max

**Sorted sets' Min and Max properties should be used.** SortedSet<T> keeps its smallest and largest elements at hand; the LINQ Min() and Max() walk the whole set.

SonarQube: S6609 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(SortedSet<int> xs) { return xs.Min(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(SortedSet<int> xs) { return xs.Min; } }
```

## cs/linked-list-first-last

**LinkedList's First and Last properties should be used.** LinkedList<T> holds its first and last nodes; the LINQ First() and Last() enumerate.

SonarQube: S6613 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(LinkedList<int> xs) { return xs.First(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(LinkedList<int> xs) { return xs.First.Value; } }
```

## cs/readonly-generic-field-member-assignment

**Members of readonly fields of unconstrained generic types should not be assigned.** If the type argument is a struct, `field.X = 1` sets X on a copy of the readonly field and is lost.

SonarQube: S2934

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Mover<T> where T : IPoint { readonly T point; public Mover(T p) { point = p; } public void Move() { point.X = 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Mover<T> where T : class, IPoint { readonly T point; public Mover(T p) { point = p; } public void Move() { point.X = 1; } }
```

## cs/shared-part-created-with-new

**Shared MEF parts should not be created with new.** A part marked CreationPolicy.Shared is meant to have one instance from the container; `new` creates another one outside it.

SonarQube: S4277 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Export(typeof(IFoo))] [PartCreationPolicy(CreationPolicy.Shared)] class Foo : IFoo { }
class P { void F() { var f = new Foo(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Export(typeof(IFoo))] [PartCreationPolicy(CreationPolicy.Shared)] class Foo : IFoo { }
class P { void F(IServiceProvider s) { var f = s.GetService(typeof(IFoo)); } }
```

## cs/deserialization-skips-validation

**Serializable classes should validate data during deserialization.** Deserialization doesn't run the regular constructor, so checks done there are bypassed by a crafted payload unless the deserialization path repeats them.

SonarQube: S5766 (related)

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class Url : ISerializable { string url; public Url(string u) { if (!u.StartsWith("https://")) { throw new ArgumentException(); } url = u; } protected Url(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext c) { url = info.GetString("url"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[Serializable] class Url : ISerializable { string url; public Url(string u) { if (!u.StartsWith("https://")) { throw new ArgumentException(); } url = u; } protected Url(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext c) { url = info.GetString("url"); if (!url.StartsWith("https://")) { throw new SerializationException(); } } }
```

## cs/deep-inheritance

**Inheritance trees should not be too deep.** Each level of inheritance is more behavior to understand before changing a class; past five, composition usually fits better.

SonarQube: S110 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A1 { } class A2 : A1 { } class A3 : A2 { } class A4 : A3 { } class A5 : A4 { } class A6 : A5 { } class A7 : A6 { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A1 { } class A2 : A1 { }
```

## cs/parameter-name-mismatch

**Parameter names should match the declaration they implement.** Callers using named arguments see the base declaration's names; a different name in an override or implementation confuses them.

SonarQube: S927

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface IAccount { void Add(int money); } class Account : IAccount { public void Add(int amount) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface IAccount { void Add(int money); } class Account : IAccount { public void Add(int money) { } }
```

## cs/override-changes-default

**Overrides should not change parameter defaults.** Defaults come from the static type: the same call passes different values depending on whether the variable is typed as the base or the derived class.

SonarQube: S1006

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Run(int distance = 42) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Run(int distance = 5) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Run(int distance = 42) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Run(int distance = 42) { } }
```

## cs/field-could-be-local

**Private fields only used as locals should be locals.** A field that every method assigns before reading carries no state between calls; as a field, it suggests otherwise and isn't thread-safe.

SonarQube: S1450 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Foo { private int total; public int Sum(int x) { total = x + 5; return total * 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Foo { public int Sum(int x) { var total = x + 5; return total * 2; } }
```

## cs/argument-order

**Arguments should be passed in the order of the parameters they're named after.** `Divide(dividend, divisor)` to a method declared `Divide(divisor, dividend)` is almost certainly swapped.

SonarQube: S2234 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { double Divide(int divisor, int dividend) => divisor / dividend; void F(int divisor, int dividend) { Divide(dividend, divisor); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { double Divide(int divisor, int dividend) => divisor / dividend; void F(int divisor, int dividend) { Divide(divisor, dividend); } }
```

## cs/could-be-static

**Private methods and properties that don't use instance data should be static.** Making them static shows they don't depend on the object's state, and saves the hidden `this` argument.

SonarQube: S2325 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int Sum(int a, int b) { return a + b; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private static int Sum(int a, int b) { return a + b; } }
```

## cs/inner-member-shadows-outer

**Nested type members should not shadow the outer type's static members.** Inside the nested type, the name means its own member, which readers may take for the outer one.

SonarQube: S3218 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Outer { public static int A; class Inner { public int A; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Outer { public static int A; class Inner { public int B; } }
```

## cs/unused-private-return

**Private methods should not return values that no caller uses.** When every call discards the result, the return value is dead code.

SonarQube: S3241 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { private int Count() => 42; public void F() { Count(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { private void Count() { } public void F() { Count(); } }
```

## cs/generic-variance

**Generic type parameters should be co- or contravariant when possible.** An interface or delegate that only returns T can be `out T`, and one that only takes T can be `in T`, so it converts like IEnumerable<T> and Action<T> do.

SonarQube: S3246

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface ISource<T> { T Next(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface ISource<out T> { T Next(); }
```

## cs/override-without-params

**Overrides should keep params.** An override inherits `params` from the base declaration whether it says so or not; leaving it out misleads readers.

SonarQube: S3262

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Log(params string[] parts) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Log(string[] parts) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Log(params string[] parts) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Log(params string[] parts) { } }
```

## cs/override-adds-params

**Overrides should not add params.** `params` on an override has no effect: callers use the base declaration's signature.

SonarQube: S3600

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Log(string[] parts) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Log(params string[] parts) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Base { public virtual void Log(string[] parts) { } } class Derived : Base { public override void Log(string[] parts) { } }
```

## cs/private-static-only-nested

**Private static methods only used by a nested type should move there.** The nested type has the same access to the outer type's static members, and the outer type gets smaller.

SonarQube: S3398

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Outer { private static int Twice(int n) => n * 2; class Nested { int F() => Twice(2); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Outer { class Nested { private static int Twice(int n) => n * 2; int F() => Twice(2); } }
```

## cs/interface-colliding-members

**Interfaces should not inherit colliding members without resolving them.** Two base interfaces with a member of the same name make every use through the derived interface ambiguous (CS0229): callers must cast.

SonarQube: S3444

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface IA { int Id { get; } } interface IB { int Id { get; } } interface IC : IA, IB { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface IA { int Id { get; } } interface IB { int Id { get; } } interface IC : IA, IB { new int Id { get; } }
```

## cs/dispose-pattern

**IDisposable should be implemented with the dispose pattern.** A class that derived classes can extend needs `protected virtual Dispose(bool)`, called by a non-virtual Dispose() and by its finalizer, so each level releases its own resources once.

SonarQube: S3881 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class Holder : IDisposable { public virtual void Dispose() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class Holder : IDisposable { public void Dispose() { Dispose(true); GC.SuppressFinalize(this); } protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing) { } }
```

## cs/hiding-member-less-visible

**Inherited members should not be hidden by less visible ones.** A private method with a base method's signature hides it only inside the class; callers outside still reach the base method, which is confusing.

SonarQube: S4015

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Base { public void Run(int n) { } } class Derived : Base { private void Run(int n) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Base { public void Run(int n) { } } class Derived : Base { private void RunLocally(int n) { } }
```

## cs/base-method-hidden

**Base class methods should not be hidden by wider overloads.** A derived method taking `object` where the base takes `string` wins overload resolution for every derived instance, so the base method is never called.

SonarQube: S4019

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Base { internal void Write(string s) { } } class Derived : Base { internal void Write(object o) { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Base { internal void Write(string s) { } } class Derived : Base { internal void WriteObject(object o) { } }
```

## cs/durable-entity-interface

**Durable entity interfaces should follow the proxy restrictions.** Entity proxies only support interfaces of non-generic methods with at most one parameter, returning void, Task or Task<T>; anything else fails at run time.

SonarQube: S6424 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
interface ICounter { void Add(int a, int b); }
class F { Task G(IDurableEntityClient c, EntityId id) => c.SignalEntityAsync<ICounter>(id, x => x.Add(1, 2)); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
interface ICounter { void Add(int amount); }
class F { Task G(IDurableEntityClient c, EntityId id) => c.SignalEntityAsync<ICounter>(id, x => x.Add(1)); }
```

## cs/controller-mixed-responsibilities

**Controllers should not have mixed responsibilities.** When groups of actions use disjoint sets of injected services, the controller holds several unrelated responsibilities and can be split.

SonarQube: S6960 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class MediaController : ControllerBase { readonly IMovies movies; readonly IPhotos photos; public MediaController(IMovies m, IPhotos p) { movies = m; photos = p; } public IActionResult Movie() => Ok(movies.Get()); public IActionResult Photo() => Ok(photos.Get()); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class MovieController : ControllerBase { readonly IMovies movies; public MovieController(IMovies m) { movies = m; } public IActionResult Movie() => Ok(movies.Get()); public IActionResult Trailer() => Ok(movies.Trailer()); }
```

## cs/underposting-value-type

**Value-type properties of action models should be nullable or required.** When a client leaves a non-nullable int or bool out of the request, the model silently gets 0 or false; nullable, `required` or [JsonRequired] lets the server tell.

SonarQube: S6964 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
public class Order { public int Quantity { get; set; } }
public class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpPost] public IActionResult Create(Order order) => Ok(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
public class Order { public required int Quantity { get; set; } }
public class OrdersController : ControllerBase { [HttpPost] public IActionResult Create(Order order) => Ok(); }
```

## cs/equality-on-equals-type

**== should not compare values of a type that overrides Equals.** Without an == operator, == compares references even when the class defines value equality with Equals.

Off by default: add `"cs/equality-on-equals-type"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1698 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class Money { public override bool Equals(object o) => true; public override int GetHashCode() => 0; }
class A { bool F(Money a, Money b) => a == b; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class Money { public override bool Equals(object o) => true; public override int GetHashCode() => 0; }
class A { bool F(Money a, Money b) => a.Equals(b); }
```

## cs/undisposed-local

**Disposable objects created in a method should be disposed.** A stream, connection or client that is neither disposed, returned nor handed to someone else keeps its file handle or socket until the garbage collector finalizes it.

SonarQube: S2930 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string p) { var reader = new StreamReader(p); return reader.ReadToEnd(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string p) { using var reader = new StreamReader(p); return reader.ReadToEnd(); } }
```

## cs/recursive-generic-inheritance

**Generic types should not inherit from expansions of themselves.** `class D<T> : C<D<D<T>>>` makes the runtime build ever larger types when loading D, and fails with TypeLoadException.

SonarQube: S3464

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class C<T> { } class D<T> : C<D<D<T>>> { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class C<T> { } class D<T> : C<D<T>> { }
```

## cs/dead-store

**Values assigned to locals should be used.** A value overwritten or dropped before anything reads it was computed for nothing, or the code that should read it is missing.

SonarQube: S1854 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int y) { int x = Compute(); x = 200; return x + y; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int y) { int x = 200; return x + y; } }
```

## cs/string-builder-never-read

**StringBuilder contents should be used.** A StringBuilder that is appended to but never turned into a string, passed on or returned only costs time.

SonarQube: S3063

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(string[] xs) { var sb = new StringBuilder(); foreach (var x in xs) { sb.Append(x); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { string F(string[] xs) { var sb = new StringBuilder(); foreach (var x in xs) { sb.Append(x); } return sb.ToString(); } }
```

## cs/assembly-version-missing

**Assemblies should have version information.** A hand-written AssemblyInfo without AssemblyVersion ships as version 0.0.0.0, which breaks binding redirects and support. SDK-style projects generate it instead.

SonarQube: S3904 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
[assembly: AssemblyProduct("Lib")]
namespace Lib { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
[assembly: AssemblyVersion("1.0.0.0")]
namespace Lib { }
```

## cs/assembly-cls-compliant

**Assemblies should state their CLS compliance.** CLSCompliant tells callers in other .NET languages whether they can use every public member.

Off by default: add `"cs/assembly-cls-compliant"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3990 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
namespace Lib { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
[assembly: CLSCompliant(true)]
namespace Lib { }
```

## cs/assembly-com-visible

**Assemblies should state their COM visibility.** Without ComVisible, an assembly's public types are visible to COM clients by default.

Off by default: add `"cs/assembly-com-visible"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3992 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
namespace Lib { }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
[assembly: AssemblyTitle("Lib")]
[assembly: ComVisible(false)]
namespace Lib { }
```

## cs/parameter-could-be-enumerable

**Parameters should be declared with the most general type they need.** A List<T> or array parameter that is only enumerated could be an IEnumerable<T>, which accepts more callers.

Off by default: add `"cs/parameter-could-be-enumerable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3242 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int Sum(List<int> xs) { var s = 0; foreach (var x in xs) { s += x; } return s; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int Sum(IEnumerable<int> xs) { var s = 0; foreach (var x in xs) { s += x; } return s; } }
```

## cs/string-uri-argument

**System.Uri overloads should be called instead of string ones.** When a method has string and Uri overloads, the Uri one parses and validates the address.

Off by default: add `"cs/string-uri-argument"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4005 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void Fetch(string url) { } void Fetch(Uri url) { } void F() { Fetch("https://a.example"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void Fetch(string url) { } void Fetch(Uri url) { } void F() { Fetch(new Uri("https://a.example")); } }
```

## cs/localizable-literal

**Literals should not be passed where text is localized.** A literal given as a Text, Message or Caption, or written to the console, shows the same words to every user.

Off by default: add `"cs/localizable-literal"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S4055 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Label label) { label.Text = "Hello"; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Label label) { label.Text = Resources.Hello; } }
```

## cs/float-equality

**Floating-point values should not be tested for equality.** Most decimal fractions have no exact binary representation, so `x == 0.1` can be false for a value that prints as 0.1. `x < 4 || x > 4` is the same test written indirectly.

SonarQube: S1244

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(double x) { return x == 0.1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(double x) { return Math.Abs(x - 0.1) < 1e-9; } }
```

## cs/integer-division-to-floating

**Integer division should not be assigned to floating-point variables.** `double d = 1 / 3;` divides integers first, which gives 0, and only then converts it.

SonarQube: S2184

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { double Ratio(int a, int b) { double r = a / b; return r; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { double Ratio(int a, int b) { double r = (double)a / b; return r; } }
```

## cs/begin-invoke-without-end

**BeginInvoke should be paired with EndInvoke.** Without EndInvoke a delegate's asynchronous call leaks its resources, and any exception it throws is lost.

SonarQube: S4583

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Func<int> work) { work.BeginInvoke(null, null); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Func<int> work) { var r = work.BeginInvoke(null, null); work.EndInvoke(r); } }
```

## cs/non-short-circuit-logic

**Boolean conditions should use && and ||.** `&` and `|` evaluate both sides even when the result is already known, so a guard such as `x != null & x.Ok` still throws.

SonarQube: S2178

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(B b) { return b != null & b.Ok; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(B b) { return b != null && b.Ok; } }
```

## cs/redundant-boolean-literal

**Boolean literals should not be redundant.** `b ? true : false` is `b`, `x && true` is `x`, and `x == true` is `x`. Comparisons with a literal are only reported when the other side is a plain bool: on a nullable bool they aren't redundant.

SonarQube: S1125

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return b == true; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b, bool? c) { return b || c == true; } }
```

## cs/any-instead-of-contains

**Contains should be used instead of Any for simple equality.** On a List, HashSet or SortedSet, `xs.Contains(y)` is faster than `xs.Any(x => x == y)`, which walks every element.

SonarQube: S6617

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs, int y) { return xs.Any(x => x == y); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(List<int> xs, int y) { return xs.Contains(y); } }
```

## cs/event-never-used

**Events should be invoked.** An event that nothing in its class invokes never fires: subscribing to it and testing it for null don't raise it.

SonarQube: S3264

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler Changed; public void Watch(EventHandler h) { Changed += h; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public event EventHandler Changed; public void Set() { Changed?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty); } }
```

## cs/method-returns-constant

**Methods should not return constants.** A method whose only statement returns a literal is a constant in disguise, and callers may expect it to do more.

SonarQube: S3400

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int Limit() { return 10; } int F() => Limit(); }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const int Limit = 10; int F() => Limit; }
```

## cs/loop-could-be-where

**Loops should be simplified with LINQ expressions.** A foreach whose whole body is an `if` filters, and one that only uses one member of each element selects: `xs.Where(...)` and `xs.Select(x => x.P)` say so directly.

SonarQube: S3267

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int[] xs) { foreach (var x in xs) { if (x > 0) { G(x); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int[] xs) { foreach (var x in xs.Where(x => x > 0)) { G(x); } } }
```

## cs/where-then-predicate-method

**LINQ expressions should be simplified.** `Where(p).First()` is `First(p)`; `Select(x => x as T)` followed by a null check, and `Where(x => x is T)` followed by a cast, are `OfType<T>()`; Count() on a collection is its Count or Length; and ToList() or ToArray() in the middle of a query copies for nothing.

SonarQube: S2971

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.Where(x => x > 0).First(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.First(x => x > 0); } }
```

## cs/lock-on-weak-identity

**Locks should not be taken on objects with weak identity.** Strings, types, reflection objects, threads and a few exceptions can be shared across application domains, so a lock on one may block threads elsewhere.

SonarQube: S3998

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { readonly string key = "k"; void F() { lock (key) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { readonly object key = new object(); void F() { lock (key) { } } }
```
